- **Setting:** The barrier, full frame. Ordinary station behind him, a smear of red hair that is Molly nodding. Sound of the station in the background, magic in the bricks.
- **Who:** Harry, jacket flapping over robes, glasses, both hands locked on the trolley, Hedwig’s cage rocking, trunk labeled. Molly’s hand lifted in a small go-on, out of focus.
- **Faces:** Eyes shut, so the feeling is in his mouth: braced, brave, a little sick. Hedwig’s eye is open, calm.

### Page 2 — Ron

#### C5-P2-P1

- **Beat:** A scarlet steam train. Owls, cats, steam.
- **Image:** Wide. The Hogwarts Express, scarlet and huge, steam blooming. Students in robes say goodbye to parents. Cats slip under trolleys. Owls hoot.
- **Setting:** Platform 9¾. Victorian iron roof, lanterns, the train filling the frame. Daylight through steam.
- **Who:** Harry small with his trolley, jacket, glasses, suddenly among dozens of children who look like the reflection in the shop window. A conductor in a cap. Parents in cloaks waving. A girl with a cat. No Ron yet.
- **Faces:** Harry looks up at the train, relief and awe. He looks for the red-haired family and finds too many redheads to be sure.

#### C5-P2-P2

- **Beat:** An empty compartment. The train lurches.
- **Image:** Harry has just sat. The train jerks. Hedwig’s cage slides. He catches it. The corridor outside is full of passing robes and trunks.
- **Setting:** A compact train compartment. Benches, a window onto the platform sliding away, luggage rack, warm wood. Steam outside.
- **Who:** Harry alone, robes straightened, jacket off now and folded, jeans and sneakers still on under the robes, glasses, scar. Hedwig in her cage on the seat beside him. His trunk overhead. He holds the cage steady.
- **Faces:** He looks at the empty seat opposite, lonely but proud he managed the trolley. A small breath of a smile at Hedwig. She looks back, unruffled.

#### C5-P2-P3

- **Beat:** Ron slides the door, shy, and asks if the seat is free.
- **Image:** Ron in the doorway with a trunk that has a broken corner and a rat cage. He is taller than Harry and trying to be smaller. Dirt on his nose.
- **Setting:** The compartment. The corridor behind Ron is busy. The train is moving. Countryside begins.
- **Who:** Ron, bright red hair, freckles, long nose, secondhand robes a bit short, maroon sweater, scuffed shoes, rat cage in one hand, the other on the door. Scabbers, a fat gray rat, asleep. Harry on the bench, robes, glasses, Hedwig.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at the empty seat, not quite at Harry, shy. Harry looks at Ron, relieved, and nods. A start of friendliness, cautious on both sides.

#### C5-P2-P4

- **Beat:** Ron’s sandwiches are dry. Harry offers sweets. Ron lights up.
- **Image:** Ron’s sandwich is a sad curl of corned beef. Harry holds out a box of bright wizard sweets from the trolley. Ron’s whole posture changes, leaning in.
- **Setting:** The compartment table. Countryside smearing past the window. Wrappers.
- **Who:** Ron, maroon sweater, freckles, the sad sandwich in one hand. Harry, robes, a colorful box in both hands, offering. Hedwig and Scabbers ignore each other. A witch with a trolley is just leaving, hat and apron.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at the sweets, then at Harry, delighted and a little ashamed of being delighted. Harry looks at Ron, glad to have something to give, smiling. This is the first easy smile between them.

#### C5-P2-P5

- **Beat:** Ron says his whole family has been talking about Harry. Harry does not know why.
- **Image:** Ron talking with his hands, a sweet half in his mouth. Harry has gone still, a chocolate frog box unopened. The scar is visible where his hair parted.
- **Setting:** The moving countryside, green and ordinary, outside. Inside, wrappers and the start of a long conversation.
- **Who:** Ron, freckles, red hair, sweater, leaning forward. Harry, robes, glasses, hands around the unopened box. Scabbers on a wrapper.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at Harry with the shine of someone meeting a story. Harry looks back, uncomfortable, honest confusion. He does not enjoy the fame. Ron sees that and his own face sobers.

#### C5-P2-P6

- **Beat:** Ron starts to explain, then stops, because it is too big.
- **Image:** Ron’s mouth open, one hand raised, the explanation stuck. Harry waits. Between them on the table, the chocolate frog box, unopened, like the story.
- **Setting:** The compartment. A tunnel comes, and the window goes dark, their reflections appearing.
- **Who:** The two boys. Ron, sweater sleeves short, dirt still on his nose, sweets forgotten. Harry, neat enough robes, taped glasses from home still, scar.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at Harry, kind, suddenly aware that explaining a murder to the boy who survived it is not a train game. Harry looks at Ron, patient, a little afraid of the answer. Their reflections look older than they are.

### Page 3 — Hermione and Draco

#### C5-P3-P1

- **Beat:** Hermione opens the door, looking for a lost toad.
- **Image:** The door slides. Hermione stands there with bushy hair, a book, and the urgent face of someone on a mission. Behind her, a round boy — Neville — is panicking further down the corridor.
- **Setting:** Train corridor meeting the compartment. Daylight again after the tunnel. Robes, trunks, a prefect passing.
- **Who:** Hermione, about eleven, bushy brown hair, large front teeth, brand-new robes, tie perfect, a book under one arm, the other hand on the door. Harry and Ron inside, sweets between them. Neville in the corridor, sandy hair, robes crooked, empty hands, toad missing.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks past them, searching the seats, businesslike. Ron looks at her, already overwhelmed. Harry looks at her, polite. Neville looks at the floor, near tears.

#### C5-P3-P2

- **Beat:** She sees the scar and blurts that she has read about him.
- **Image:** Hermione’s eyes drop to Harry’s forehead. Her mission pauses. She leans in, delighted by a fact come to life. Harry leans back.
- **Setting:** The doorway. The book under her arm is thick. Ron is a grimace in the background.
- **Who:** Hermione, new robes, book, bushy hair haloed by the corridor lamp. Harry, scar exposed, glasses, sweet box as a shield. Ron, red hair, unimpressed.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at the scar, bright, impressed, no malice. Harry looks at the book, then at her, weary of the scar already. Ron looks at Hermione, wary.

#### C5-P3-P3

- **Beat:** She corrects Ron about a spell and leaves. Ron groans.
- **Image:** Hermione is already walking away, a finger raised with the correction, Neville in tow. Ron has both hands over his face. Harry is trying not to laugh.
- **Setting:** The compartment, door still open, corridor moving.
- **Who:** Hermione from behind, book, perfect tie, one finger up. Neville trotting, still toadless. Ron, maroon sweater, face in his freckled hands. Harry, a hand over his mouth.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks ahead, satisfied she was right. Ron looks at nobody, muffled despair. Harry looks at Ron, amused and a little impressed by her.

#### C5-P3-P4

- **Beat:** Draco enters. Crabbe and Goyle block the door.
- **Image:** The doorway goes dark. Draco in front, sleek. Two much larger boys fill the frame behind him so there is no leaving.
- **Setting:** The compartment suddenly smaller. Countryside ignored.
- **Who:** Draco, platinum hair slicked, expensive black robes, green lining flashing, a cold smile, hands clasped behind his back. Crabbe and Goyle, heavy, short hair, bulky robes, arms folded, no expressions yet. Harry and Ron seated, sweets, rat, owl.
- **Faces:** Draco looks at Harry, assessing, then at Ron’s sweater, already mocking. Crabbe and Goyle look at a point over the boys’ heads, dull. Harry looks at Draco, recognizing him from the alley. Ron looks at the blocked door.

#### C5-P3-P5

- **Beat:** Draco insults Ron’s family and says Harry will regret his company.
- **Image:** Draco’s finger flicks Ron’s maroon sleeve. Ron’s ears go red. Harry’s hand closes on the bench.
- **Setting:** Tight in the compartment. Scabbers hides in Ron’s pocket. Hedwig’s eyes narrow.
- **Who:** Draco leaning in, perfect robes, sneer, one finger on Ron’s sweater as if it stains him. Ron, secondhand robes, freckles, fists. Harry, glasses, scar, sitting forward. Crabbe and Goyle, a wall.
- **Faces:** Draco looks at Harry while insulting Ron, recruiting. Ron looks at Draco’s finger, humiliated and angry. Harry looks at Draco, the friendliness gone. Hedwig looks at Draco, unfriendly.

#### C5-P3-P6

- **Beat:** Harry says he can tell the wrong sort for himself. Draco’s smile dies.
- **Image:** Harry standing now, shorter than Draco but square to him. Draco’s smile is half-gone, caught mid-collapse. Ron looks at Harry as if a goal has been scored.
- **Setting:** The compartment. The corridor behind Crabbe and Goyle, who have shifted, unsure.
- **Who:** Harry, robes, sneakers planted, glasses, scar, empty hands, chin up. Draco, platinum hair, expensive robes, the offered friendship withdrawn. Ron half-risen, red hair, grateful. The two big boys looking at Draco for orders.
- **Faces:** Harry looks Draco in the eye, calm, final. Draco looks back, smile dead, a flicker of insulted surprise. Ron looks at Harry, loyal already.

### Page 4 — The journey’s end

#### C5-P4-P1

- **Beat:** The boys trade Dursley stories and Weasley stories.
- **Image:** They sit with their shoes up, talking. Outside, the land is darkening. Empty sweet boxes. A feeling of hours passed.
- **Setting:** The compartment at dusk. Lamps on. Fields becoming black. Reflections on the window.
- **Who:** Harry, jacket off, robes loosened, glasses, talking with his hands about a cupboard, not performing it. Ron, maroon sweater, listening hard, then talking about hand-me-down books and a lot of brothers. Scabbers on Ron’s knee. Hedwig dozing.
- **Faces:** They look at each other, easier now. Harry looks grateful. Ron looks indignant on Harry’s behalf about the Dursleys, which matters more than fame.

#### C5-P4-P2

- **Beat:** Scabbers sleeps on a sweet wrapper.
- **Image:** A small quiet panel. The fat gray rat curled on a bright wrapper, tiny paws, snoring. Ron’s finger strokes his back. It is a nothing-moment that makes them friends.
- **Setting:** The table in lamplight. Night window. Crumbs.
- **Who:** Scabbers, old, sleepy, a little bald on the back. Ron’s freckled hand. Harry’s hand nearby, not touching, respectful. No faces except the boys’ chins at the top, both looking down, soft.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at his rat, fond. Harry looks at the rat, kindly, thinking of Hedwig.

#### C5-P4-P3

- **Beat:** Robes on. Harry fumbles a sleeve.
- **Image:** The train is slowing. Students in the corridor are pulling on robes and ties. Harry has his sleeve inside out. Ron, already dressed, helps without commenting.
- **Setting:** Compartment and corridor. Lanterns outside now, among trees. Excitement in the passing faces.
- **Who:** Harry, white shirt, gray V-neck, tie crooked, one sleeve wrong, glasses, frustrated. Ron, robes on, maroon sweater underneath, patient hands fixing the sleeve. Hermione passes in the corridor, already perfect, a toad — Trevor — in her hands, triumphant. Neville behind her, weeping with relief.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at his sleeve, embarrassed. Ron looks at the sleeve, not at Harry’s embarrassment, kind. Hermione looks at the toad, pleased. Neville looks at Trevor, sobbing happily.

#### C5-P4-P4

- **Beat:** The train slows. Lanterns in the trees.
- **Image:** Through the window, a forest platform, no station building, only lanterns and a crowd of waiting lamps. The scarlet train huge beside the trees.
- **Setting:** Night. Hogsmeade station, or the first-year stop: wood, steam, owls, cold breath.
- **Who:** Seen from inside: Harry and Ron’s faces close to the glass, robes, hair, glasses and freckles. Outside, dozens of students and floating lanterns. A huge figure with a lamp is not clearly Hagrid yet, just a bigger light.
- **Faces:** Harry looks out, nervous excitement. Ron looks out, grinning, he has done this in stories if not in life. Both are reflected over the forest.

#### C5-P4-P5

- **Beat:** A voice calls the first years. Hagrid’s lamp is a moon.
- **Image:** On the platform, Hagrid holds a lantern that really does look like a small moon. First years cluster. Steam. He is calling, beard wild, utterly at home.
- **Setting:** Night platform, trees, the scarlet train, trunks being unloaded by magic in the background, older students heading somewhere else.
- **Who:** Hagrid, moleskin coat, lantern, huge, waving. Harry and Ron stepping down, robes, Harry’s trunk, Hedwig. Hermione nearby with books and Trevor’s owner Neville. Draco sleek, already bored, Crabbe and Goyle with his trunk.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks over the children, counting, and his face opens when he sees Harry. Harry looks up at him, the hollow from King’s Cross gone. Ron looks at Hagrid, impressed. Draco looks at the dark, disdainful.

#### C5-P4-P6

- **Beat:** Harry steps off. Hagrid grins down at him.
- **Image:** Page-turn. Low angle. Hagrid’s grin fills the top of the panel, lantern beside his cheek. Harry at the bottom, looking up, small and arrived. Steam wraps them.
- **Setting:** The platform at night. The train is a red wall. Trees. Other first years are soft shapes. The next thing is the lake, not shown yet.
- **Who:** Hagrid, coat, beard, lantern in one hand, the other hand hovering as if he might ruffle Harry’s hair and decides not to. Harry, school robes finally worn properly, tie almost straight, glasses, scar, Hedwig’s cage at his feet.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at Harry, proud, a little emotional, grinning. Harry looks at Hagrid, safe. He is not smiling big. He is smiling true.
---

## Chapter 6 — Arrival and the Sorting

Boats, the hall, the Hat’s rules, the names, and Harry’s choice.

### Page 1 — The lake

#### C6-P1-P1

- **Beat:** First years follow Hagrid down a steep path.
- **Image:** A chain of small lanterns winding down a black hill. Hagrid’s lamp leads. Harry and Ron are two of the lights near the front.
- **Setting:** Night. A steep muddy path, trees, roots, the lake not seen yet, only a smell of water. Cold breath. The castle is hidden by the slope.
- **Who:** Hagrid at the front, coat, lantern, boots sure on the mud. Harry behind, robes, scarf not yet, glasses, careful feet. Ron, red hair bright even at night, maroon sweater under robes. Hermione with a book she is not reading. Neville slipping. Draco picking his way as if the mud is a personal insult. Other first years in black robes, pointed silhouettes.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks ahead, cheerful. Harry looks at his feet, then up, excited. Neville looks at the ground, worried. Draco looks at Hagrid’s back, sneering.

#### C6-P1-P2

- **Beat:** Small boats wait on black water.
- **Image:** The path opens. A fleet of little wooden boats rocks with no one rowing. Each holds four. The water is black and still. Lanterns double in it.
- **Setting:** The lake shore at night. Pebbles, reeds, a cliff. No castle yet; Hagrid has them facing the water first.
- **Who:** Hagrid gesturing them in, lantern high. Harry, Ron, Hermione, and Neville naturally grouping. Draco choosing a boat with Crabbe, Goyle, and a nervous girl. Harry’s hand on a boat rim.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the boats, wonder. Hermione looks at the empty oarlocks, already curious how they move. Ron looks at Harry, a let’s-go grin. Neville looks at the water, pale.

#### C6-P1-P3

- **Beat:** Four in a boat. Neville clutches the sides.
- **Image:** Inside one boat. It feels too small for the dark. Neville’s knuckles are white on the gunwale. Hermione sits straight. Ron’s knee bumps Harry’s.
- **Setting:** Black water, the shore lanterns behind, mist on the surface. Other boats are soft neighbors.
- **Who:** Harry, robes, glasses, hands on his knees, scar, looking forward. Ron, freckles, red hair, one hand trailing near the water and then thinking better of it. Hermione, bushy hair, tie perfect, a hand on Neville’s sleeve without making a fuss. Neville, sandy hair, robes crooked, both hands locked on the wood. No Trevor; he is in a pocket, a lump.
- **Faces:** Neville looks at the water, terrified. Hermione looks at Neville, steady, kind. Harry looks ahead, calm for someone else for the first time. Ron looks at Harry, copying the calm.

#### C6-P1-P4

- **Beat:** The boats move with nobody rowing.
- **Image:** A wide shot from higher up. The fleet slides by itself, four by four, leaving clean lines on the black water. No splashing. Lanterns like a migrating constellation.
- **Setting:** The open lake at night. Cliffs begin. The far shore is still dark.
- **Who:** Tiny figures. Harry’s boat near the front. Hagrid in a boat alone because no one else fits, lantern between his boots, coat spread.
- **Faces:** Too far for detail. The feeling is hush. A few pale faces turned forward.

#### C6-P1-P5

- **Beat:** Hogwarts rises, windows lit, doubled in the lake.
- **Image:** The castle fills the panel. Towers, lit windows, rock, a glow on the water so perfect the boats seem to float between two castles.
- **Setting:** Night lake, clear enough for the reflection. The cliff and the castle. Warm window-light against cold stone.
- **Who:** The boats are small at the bottom. Harry is standing in his, despite the rock of it, robes, hair, glasses catching a window. Ron grabs his robe hem so he will not fall. Hermione and Neville seated, faces up.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the castle, undone by it. Ron looks at Harry, then at the castle, grinning. Hermione looks at the towers, lips parted, memorizing. Neville looks up, fear forgotten.

#### C6-P1-P6

- **Beat:** Nobody speaks. Harry’s face in the reflection.
- **Image:** The page-turn is quiet. The water shows Harry’s face among the lit windows, scar and glasses and all. The real Harry is only a chin at the top edge, looking down.
- **Setting:** Black water, gold windows, the boat’s wooden edge.
- **Who:** Harry’s reflection, robes, messy hair, round glasses, lightning scar, expression open. Ron’s reflection beside him, red even in gold light, also silent. No one holds anything. Hands are still.
- **Faces:** The reflection looks wonderstruck and a little scared of wanting this. Harry looks at himself in the water as if meeting the boy from the shop window again, and this time the castle is behind him.

### Page 2 — The Hat’s rules

#### C6-P2-P1

- **Beat:** The Great Hall. Floating candles. A night-sky ceiling.
- **Image:** They enter and the hall explodes upward. Four long tables, hundreds of students, candles hanging in the air, and a ceiling that is not there: clouds and stars.
- **Setting:** The Great Hall at night. Stone, banners for four houses — red, yellow, blue, green — gold plates, pumpkins not yet, it is September. The staff table is a bright line at the far end.
- **Who:** First years clustered at the door, muddy hems, faces tipped up. Harry, Ron, Hermione, Neville among them. Older students turning to look. McGonagall ahead in emerald robes, leading. Dumbledore a small tall shape at the staff table. Snape a black shape. Quirrell a purple turban.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the ceiling, awed. A Ravenclaw student looks at the first years, mild. McGonagall looks ahead, brisk, not looking at the ceiling she has seen for years.

#### C6-P2-P2

- **Beat:** A ghost drifts through a table. Neville yelps.
- **Image:** A pearly figure in ruff and tights rises through a bench and a student’s body. Neville jumps and bumps Hermione. Plates do not move.
- **Setting:** Beside the Gryffindor table, though they are not sorted. Candles. Laughter starting.
- **Who:** Nearly Headless Nick, transparent, elegant, head a bit wobbly, a smile. Neville, robes, sandy hair, hands up. Hermione steadying her books. Harry and Ron flanking, startled. Students at the table amused.
- **Faces:** Nick looks at Neville, apologetic and pleased to be noticed. Neville looks at the ghost, yelping, eyes round. Harry looks at Nick, delighted fear. Ron looks at Harry, whispering a grin.

#### C6-P2-P3

- **Beat:** McGonagall names the four houses and the Hat.
- **Image:** She stands before the first years, a scroll in one hand, the other indicating the banners. Behind her, a stool and an old hat wait, not yet alive.
- **Setting:** The front of the hall. Staff watching. Four banner colors frame her.
- **Who:** McGonagall, bun, square spectacles, emerald robes and hat, scroll. The Sorting Hat, patched, leather, brim slumped, on a three-legged stool. First years in a nervous knot: Harry, Ron twisting his hands, Hermione mouthing facts, Draco smooth, Neville pale.
- **Faces:** McGonagall looks along the line of children, fair and severe. Hermione looks at the hat, intent. Draco looks at the green banner, already home in his head. Harry looks at the four colors, not knowing which one is his.

#### C6-P2-P4

- **Beat:** The Sorting Hat is set on a stool and twitches.
- **Image:** Close. The hat’s brim ripples. A tear near the brim opens like a mouth. The stool creaks. A first year flinches.
- **Setting:** Candlelight on old leather. The hall soft behind.
- **Who:** The Hat only, brown, patched, ancient. McGonagall’s hand has just left it. No face but the rip.
- **Faces:** The rip shapes a sly, knowing almost-smile. It looks, somehow, at all of them at once.

#### C6-P2-P5

- **Beat:** It sings that it can see courage, loyalty, wit, and ambition.
- **Image:** The hat mid-song, brim wide, and behind it four ghostly flashes of the houses: a lion, a badger, an eagle, a snake, not literal animals in the room, just shapes in the candle smoke as a visual.
- **Setting:** The hall listening. Even the ghosts have stopped drifting.
- **Who:** The Hat on the stool. McGonagall beside it, patient. Dumbledore at the high table, beard, purple, enjoying this. Students leaning in. Harry in the crowd, glasses shining.
- **Faces:** The Hat looks proud of its own song. Dumbledore looks at the first years, twinkling, kind. Harry looks at the lion-shape, then the snake-shape, unsettled. Draco looks at the snake, satisfied.

#### C6-P2-P6

- **Beat:** The first name. One child walks up alone. The hall holds its breath.
- **Image:** A small student crosses a huge empty stretch of floor. The hat waits. Hundreds of faces are a blur of eyes. The walk feels longer than the hall.
- **Setting:** Flagstones, candle shadows, the stool tiny in the distance, then closer. Banners.
- **Who:** A first year who is not one of the core group, robes too long, alone. McGonagall with the scroll. The Hat. Harry, Ron, Hermione, Neville, and Draco in the waiting line, watching the walk they will all have to make.
- **Faces:** The walking child looks at the hat, swallowing. McGonagall looks at her scroll, neutral. Harry looks at the empty floor, measuring it. Ron looks at his own shoes.

### Page 3 — Names

#### C6-P3-P1

- **Beat:** Hermione. The Hat barely touches her and calls Gryffindor.
- **Image:** The Hat is only just on her bushy hair and already shouting. She lifts it off herself, smiling in relief, and the red table erupts.
- **Setting:** The stool, the hall, Gryffindor table reaching for her. Red and gold.
- **Who:** Hermione, new robes, tie perfect, the Hat in her hands, bookless for once. McGonagall nodding once. Harry and Ron still in line. Gryffindor students cheering, including the twins.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks toward the red table, relieved, a bright true smile. She glances back at Harry and Ron, encouraging. The Hat, in her hands, looks spent for a second. Draco looks bored.

#### C6-P3-P2

- **Beat:** Draco asks for Slytherin and gets it at once.
- **Image:** Draco sits as if the stool is a throne. He does not look nervous. The Hat touches him and agrees immediately. Green table applause, polite and sharp.
- **Setting:** The same floor. Green and silver banners seeming brighter.
- **Who:** Draco, platinum hair still perfect, expensive robes, green lining, hands on his knees, chin lifted. The Hat on his head. Crabbe and Goyle waiting, dull. Snape at the high table, a tiny approval in the set of his mouth.
- **Faces:** Draco looks at the green table, already walking there in his mind, a cool smile. He does not look at the Hat; he looks at Harry in the line. Snape looks at Draco, satisfied. Harry looks back, unimpressed.

#### C6-P3-P3

- **Beat:** From the green table, Draco looks back at Harry.
- **Image:** Draco seated among Slytherins, a goblet in hand he is too young to need, turned fully around. The line of unsorted children is in the distance. Harry is the one in focus.
- **Setting:** Slytherin table, green light from a banner falling on silver plates. The hall’s noise.
- **Who:** Draco, robes arranged, platinum hair, one hand on a goblet, the other arm along the bench, performing ease. Older Slytherins watching the sorting, not him. Harry far off, black hair, glasses, scar, standing between Ron and Neville.
- **Faces:** Draco looks at Harry, a challenge and an invitation mixed, smile thin. Harry looks forward at the hat, refusing to look back. Ron looks at Draco, scowling.

#### C6-P3-P4

- **Beat:** Neville trips. The hall laughs, then the Hat shouts Gryffindor.
- **Image:** Neville is on his hands and knees halfway to the stool, robes over his head. Laughter. Then, a beat later in the same panel’s right side, or the feeling of the next second: he is on the stool, the Hat yelling, and his face is pure joy.
- **Setting:** The open floor, then the stool. Gryffindor table half-laughing, half-ready to cheer.
- **Who:** Neville, sandy hair, robes tangled, Trevor visible in a pocket, first on the floor, then under the Hat. McGonagall’s mouth tight against a smile. Hermione at the red table, already standing to make room.
- **Faces:** On the floor, Neville looks at the flagstones, mortified, ears red. Under the Hat, he looks out, beaming, disbelief. Hermione looks at him, welcoming. A few Slytherins look cruel. Harry looks at Neville, glad for him.

#### C6-P3-P5

- **Beat:** Ron twists his hands. He does not want to be last, or in Slytherin. The Hat shouts Gryffindor.
- **Image:** Ron walks up with his hem twisted into a rope, ears as red as his hair. The Hat drops. A beat later it shouts Gryffindor, and he sprints for the red table before the brim is fully off.
- **Setting:** The open floor, then the Gryffindor bench. The hall is a wash of noise. Draco’s green table is a smear Ron refuses to look at.
- **Who:** Ron, freckles, secondhand robes, maroon sweater cuffs, dirty thumbnail, the Hat coming off in his hands as he runs. Harry still in the unsorted line, glasses, a steady presence. Hermione and Neville already at the red table, making room.
- **Faces:** On the walk, Ron looks at the stool, worried, ashamed of being scared. When the Hat shouts, the worry breaks into a grin. Harry looks at him, solid, a little smile that says he is scared too, and glad.

#### C6-P3-P6

- **Beat:** “Potter, Harry.” The hall goes quiet.
- **Image:** McGonagall’s mouth open on the name. The entire hall has turned. Even the candles seem to lean. Harry is a small figure at the start of that huge attention.
- **Setting:** The Great Hall, suddenly silent. Banners, faces, the stool waiting. The quiet is drawn as empty space around him.
- **Who:** McGonagall, scroll, spectacles, emerald, looking at Harry with a flicker of something warmer than neutral. Harry, robes, messy hair, glasses, scar suddenly the point of the picture. Ron already on the red bench, half-risen. Dumbledore at the high table, watching. Snape, unblinking.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at McGonagall, then at the stool, throat tight. Hundreds of students look at him. Ron looks at him from the Gryffindor table, sympathetic, willing him over. Snape looks at him, unreadable and intense. Dumbledore looks at him, gentle.

### Page 4 — The choice

#### C6-P4-P1

- **Beat:** The Hat covers Harry’s eyes. A voice in the brim.
- **Image:** Black inside the hat. Only a rim of candlelight at the bottom, and Harry’s mouth and scar below the brim. The leather is scratched and close.
- **Setting:** Inside the Hat. Smells of old cloth and dust. The hall is muffled.
- **Who:** Harry, the Hat down to his nose, glasses pressed, hands gripping the stool. No one else visible. The voice is a presence in the dark, not a body.
- **Faces:** His eyes are hidden. His mouth is tense. He looks into the dark of the brim as if someone is standing there.

#### C6-P4-P2

- **Beat:** It says Slytherin would make him great. Harry thinks no.
- **Image:** Split feeling in one panel. On one side, inside the dark, a green-silver vision of Harry older, colder, admired. On the other, his real mouth shapes no. He shakes his head under the hat.
- **Setting:** The stool in the silent hall, but most of the panel is the vision and the dark.
- **Who:** Harry, small hands on the stool, robes, the Hat. The vision-Harry wears finer robes, green lining, and does not look kind. Draco’s table is a distant real color.
- **Faces:** Real Harry, eyes still hidden, jaw set, refusing. The vision looks proud and alone. He does not want it. He looks, in his mind, toward the red table where Ron and Hermione are.

#### C6-P4-P3

- **Beat:** A pause. Then the Hat shouts Gryffindor.
- **Image:** The Hat’s brim flies wide on the shout. Harry flinches at his own result. The word breaks the silence.
- **Setting:** The stool, candles flaring in the noise that is about to hit. Red banner behind.
- **Who:** Harry under the Hat, scar, glasses askew from the brim, both hands leaving the stool. McGonagall a step away, the corner of a smile. The Hat itself looks exerted and pleased.
- **Faces:** Harry’s eyes are visible again as the brim lifts. He looks shocked, then flooded with relief. He looks toward Gryffindor. McGonagall looks at him, brief warmth.

#### C6-P4-P4

- **Beat:** The red table explodes. Ron pounds the wood. The twins yell.
- **Image:** Chaos of welcome. Hands pull Harry onto the bench. Fred and George are standing on the bench yelling. Ron is pounding the table hard enough to jump the plates. Hermione is clapping, precise and happy.
- **Setting:** Gryffindor table, red and gold, spilled pumpkin juice, candles, the hall noise rushing back.
- **Who:** Harry pulled onto the bench, robes rumpled, glasses, a helpless grin. Ron already seated from his own sorting, red hair, freckles, both fists pounding the wood, juice jumping. Fred and George, identical, older, on the bench, yelling. Hermione clapping beside a space she saved. Neville beaming further down.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at Harry, pure joy, the worry gone. Harry looks at Ron, grinning, belonging. The twins look at Harry, welcoming and loud. Hermione looks at both boys, happy and right.

#### C6-P4-P5

- **Beat:** Dumbledore welcomes them and forbids the third-floor corridor. Ron’s laugh stops.
- **Image:** Dumbledore standing at the high table, arms open, benign, and then one finger raised on the warning. In the foreground Ron’s laugh is caught and dropped. A pumpkin juice goblet paused at his mouth.
- **Setting:** The feast beginning. Food appearing — roast, pies, piles of plates — magic of plenty. The high table. Candles.
- **Who:** Dumbledore, tall, silver beard, half-moon spectacles, purple star robes, hat. Harry, Ron, Hermione, Neville at the near table with full plates they have not touched. Snape further along the high table, black, watching Harry not the food. Quirrell in a purple turban, nervous, clapping a beat late.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks over the hall, kind, and the warning is light in his voice but his eyes are not joking. Ron looks at Harry, the laugh dying into a question. Harry looks at Dumbledore, snagging on “painful death.” Hermione looks thoughtful, already filing it. Snape looks at Harry.

#### C6-P4-P6

- **Beat:** Snape stares at Harry and does not blink.
- **Image:** Page-turn. Across the feast, through steam and candles, Snape’s face is locked on Harry. Harry has a fork halfway up and has gone still. Everyone else is eating and blurred.
- **Setting:** The Great Hall feast, warm, loud, gold. The line of sight between them is clear and cold.
- **Who:** Snape, sallow, hooked nose, greasy black shoulder-length hair, buttoned black robes, hands flat on the staff table, not eating. Harry, robes, glasses, scar, fork in hand, a roast potato on it. Ron beside him eating, out of focus, happy. Hermione talking, out of focus.
- **Faces:** Snape looks at Harry, no blink, dislike that feels personal, a thin mouth. Harry looks back, the belonging of the last panel punctured, uneasy. He does not look away first.

---

## Chapter 7 — First classes, and Snape’s hostility

Lost in the castle, small magics, the dungeon, and points taken.

### Page 1 — The castle wakes

#### C7-P1-P1

- **Beat:** A staircase moves. Harry and Ron miss their landing.
- **Image:** They step and the staircase swings away from the landing they wanted. Their robes fly. A suit of armor watches.
- **Setting:** A tall Hogwarts stairwell, morning. Moving staircases, high windows, stone, portraits. Other students ride different flights calmly.
- **Who:** Harry, school robes, tie, gray V-neck, glasses, book bag slipping. Ron, red hair, secondhand robes, maroon sweater, a map he does not have, arms windmilling. A suit of armor, empty, spear in its gauntlet.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the departing landing, alarmed. Ron looks at Harry, a wild grin that is also panic. The armor looks, somehow, unimpressed.

#### C7-P1-P2

- **Beat:** A portrait scolds them for being lost.
- **Image:** A painted wizard in a ruff leans out of his frame, finger wagging. They have stopped on a landing that is the wrong century of the castle.
- **Setting:** A corridor of portraits, morning light, a tapestry, a window onto the grounds far below. They are high up.
- **Who:** The portrait, an old wizard, painted robes, angry little beard, leaning out so far his frame cuts him at the waist. Harry and Ron, books clutched, out of breath, ties crooked.
- **Faces:** The portrait looks down his nose at them, scolding. Harry looks up, polite, lost. Ron looks at the portrait, offended at being told off by paint.

#### C7-P1-P3

- **Beat:** Peeves drops a wastebasket on their heads.
- **Image:** A poltergeist in a jester’s motley hangs from a chandelier, both hands letting go of a metal bin. Trash cascades. Harry and Ron duck too late.
- **Setting:** A junction of corridors. The chandelier. Morning. A prefect in the distance pretending not to see.
- **Who:** Peeves, small, cruel, belled hat, orange and purple motley, tongue out. Harry and Ron underneath, robes, arms over their heads, paper and apple cores bouncing off them. Books on the floor.
- **Faces:** Peeves looks down, gleeful, eyes on their pain. Harry looks up through his arms, annoyed. Ron looks at Peeves, spluttering. Neither is hurt, both are humiliated.

#### C7-P1-P4

- **Beat:** They tumble into class late. McGonagall is already teaching.
- **Image:** The classroom door bangs. They fall in with the wastebasket still on Ron’s head. The class turns. McGonagall does not even pause the sentence, but her eyes do.
- **Setting:** Transfiguration classroom. Desks, sunlight, cats that might be teapots or the other way around, neat students. A blackboard.
- **Who:** Harry and Ron in the doorway, robes dusty, Ron with a bin on his head, red hair sticking out. McGonagall at the front, emerald robes, bun, spectacles, a desk wand in hand. Hermione in the front row, already perfect, wincing for them. Neville hiding a smile. Draco, in this class, smug at the back.
- **Faces:** McGonagall looks at them, cold disappointment, not surprise. Hermione looks at them, secondhand embarrassment. Draco looks at them, delighted. Harry looks at McGonagall, apologetic. Ron looks from inside the bin, muffled.

#### C7-P1-P5

- **Beat:** She changes from cat to woman. The class gasps.
- **Image:** A tabby cat on the desk stands up into McGonagall, the transformation half-done: spectacles on a cat face, then the woman, robes settling. The class is one inhale.
- **Setting:** The same sunlit classroom. Chalk dust. Wooden desks carved with old initials.
- **Who:** McGonagall, emerald, the cat-markings fading from her cloak, square glasses, composed as if she did not just do the impossible. Students, including Harry, Ron without the bin now, Hermione, Neville, Draco. Harry’s hands are flat on the desk.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at her, amazed, the lateness forgotten. Ron looks, mouth open. Hermione looks, hungry to learn it, already raising her hand. McGonagall looks at the class, challenging them to consider it ordinary. Draco looks bored to hide that he is impressed.

#### C7-P1-P6

- **Beat:** Hermione’s hand is up before the question ends.
- **Image:** Her hand is the tallest thing in the front row. McGonagall has barely finished. Hermione’s face is bright with the answer. Behind her, other hands are late and low.
- **Setting:** The classroom, blackboard with a cat sketch becoming a teapot sketch. Sun.
- **Who:** Hermione, bushy hair, perfect tie, robes, arm straight, the other hand pinning her book open. McGonagall, a small approving glance. Harry and Ron in the row behind, no hands up, quills idle.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at McGonagall, eager, not showing off on purpose. McGonagall looks at her, the sternness thawing a degree. Ron looks at the hand, a groan in his eyes. Harry looks at Hermione, impressed.

### Page 2 — Ordinary magic

#### C7-P2-P1

- **Beat:** A feather twitches for someone else. Harry’s lies still.
- **Image:** Two desks. A classmate’s feather hops. Harry’s feather is a dead shape on the wood. His wand hand is too tight.
- **Setting:** Charms classroom. Afternoon. Professor Flitwick, tiny, on a pile of books so he can see over the desk, somewhere in the background. Sun stripes.
- **Who:** Harry, robes, glasses, wand in a fist, tongue between his teeth, the feather unmoving. A brown-haired classmate, delighted, their feather up. Ron at the next desk, his feather smoking slightly. Flitwick, tiny wizard, white hair, cheerful.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at his feather, frustrated, then at the hopping one, envious. Ron looks at his own smoke, worried. Flitwick looks at the class, encouraging, not at Harry’s failure.

#### C7-P2-P2

- **Beat:** Hermione’s feather floats. She tries not to look proud.
- **Image:** Her feather hangs perfectly at eye level. She has both hands folded now, wand down, as if it were easy. A smile escapes.
- **Setting:** The same classroom. Other feathers in various states of disaster. Flitwick clapping.
- **Who:** Hermione, neat robes, wand set parallel to her book, feather in the air. Harry watching from the side, his feather flat. Ron’s hair singed at one tip.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at the feather, proud, then bites the smile, not wanting to be the nightmare Ron will call her. Flitwick looks at her, beaming. Harry looks at her feather, not unkind, just left behind.

#### C7-P2-P3

- **Beat:** Neville is suddenly sure of himself among the plants.
- **Image:** A greenhouse. Neville, usually clumsy, holds a pot steady and smiles at a wriggling seedling as if it spoke to him. He looks taller.
- **Setting:** Greenhouse Three. Wet glass, fogged panes, dripping plants, earth smell, afternoon light turned green. Professor Sprout, a stout witch in a patched hat, waist-deep in a pot somewhere behind.
- **Who:** Neville, robes with the sleeves rolled, dirt on his hands, sandy hair, a pot in both hands, earmuffs around his neck. A seedling with little grabbing arms. Harry beside him with a worse pot, glasses fogged. Sprout’s hat in the background.
- **Faces:** Neville looks at the plant, calm, happy, competent. The plant leans toward him. Harry looks at Neville, glad for him, a little mud on his cheek. Sprout looks at Neville, approving.

#### C7-P2-P4

- **Beat:** A plant bites Ron. Harry smiles at Neville.
- **Image:** A pod snaps shut on Ron’s finger. He yelps. Harry, turning to help, catches Neville’s proud little stance and smiles first.
- **Setting:** The greenhouse, messy table, spilled soil, earmuffs.
- **Who:** Ron, red hair, freckles, finger in a plant, wand dropped. Harry, mud, glasses, one hand reaching for Ron, the smile aimed at Neville. Neville, dirt-handed, surprised to be smiled at. The plant, toothy.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at his finger, offended. Harry looks at Neville, warm. Neville looks at Harry, shy pleasure. He is not used to being the one who can.

#### C7-P2-P5

- **Beat:** In the corridor, Draco mocks the scar.
- **Image:** Between classes. Draco taps his own forehead and smirks. Crabbe and Goyle laugh on cue. Students slow down to hear.
- **Setting:** A crowded stone corridor, suits of armor, a window, books clutched, the press between lessons.
- **Who:** Draco, platinum hair, expensive robes, green lining, a book he is not reading, one finger on his own brow. Crabbe and Goyle bulky behind him. Harry stopped, robes, glasses, scar, bag in hand. Ron at his shoulder, already angry. Hermione a step behind, arms full of books.
- **Faces:** Draco looks at Harry’s scar, mocking, performing for the corridor. Harry looks at Draco, tired of it, quiet. Ron looks at Draco, furious. Hermione looks at Draco, disdain, chin up.

#### C7-P2-P6

- **Beat:** The dungeon stairs. Cold air. Torches gutter.
- **Image:** They go down. The light changes from windows to torches. Their breath shows. The corridor behind them is bright and already far.
- **Setting:** Dungeon steps. Wet stone, iron brackets, greenish torch flame, a draft. Potions is below.
- **Who:** Harry in front, robes, bag, glasses, scar, one hand on the cold wall. Ron behind, red hair dulled by the dark. Hermione behind him, books, a determined face. Other Gryffindors and Slytherins mixing, Draco’s pale hair further down, already at home in the dark.
- **Faces:** Harry looks down into the cold, uneasy. Ron looks at Harry’s back. Hermione looks at the steps, practical. Draco, distant, looks up at them, already smiling.

### Page 3 — The dungeon

#### C7-P3-P1

- **Beat:** Snape sweeps in, talking of bottling fame and stoppering death.
- **Image:** The dungeon door opens on a billow of black. Snape’s robes move more than he does. The class goes silent mid-whisper. His first gesture takes in the jars of pale things.
- **Setting:** The Potions dungeon. Long tables, cauldrons, shelves of glass jars with unpleasant contents, torchlight, no windows. A blackboard. Damp.
- **Who:** Snape, sallow, hooked nose, greasy black hair to his shoulders, black robes buttoned, hands slightly spread as he speaks. Harry, Ron, Hermione at a Gryffindor table. Draco, Crabbe, Goyle at a Slytherin table, quills ready to take his side. Neville already nervous, toad not present.
- **Faces:** Snape looks over them, soft-voiced and dangerous, a thin almost-smile. He looks at the jars when he says death, then his eyes snag on Harry. Harry looks at him, alert. Hermione looks ready to answer anything. Draco looks at Snape, admiring.

#### C7-P3-P2

- **Beat:** Silence. Hermione’s hand goes up.
- **Image:** The room is still. Hermione’s hand is up, alone. Snape has not called on anyone. The hand looks brave and doomed.
- **Setting:** Torchlit tables. Steam not started. Jars glint.
- **Who:** Hermione, arm up, robes, bushy hair, the other hand on a closed book she does not need. Snape at the front, not looking at her. Harry beside her, quill, empty parchment. Ron sinking in his seat.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at Snape, hopeful, sure she knows. Snape looks past her hand as if it is not there. Harry looks at her hand, willing Snape to take it. Ron looks at the ceiling, already miserable.

#### C7-P3-P3

- **Beat:** He ignores her and says the name Potter.
- **Image:** Snape’s head turns with a snap. The name is aimed. Hermione’s hand is still up, ridiculous now. Harry’s shoulders rise.
- **Setting:** The dungeon. A jar behind Snape holds something that looks like an eye.
- **Who:** Snape, black robes, hair shadowing one eye, one hand on a desk, leaning toward the Gryffindor table. Harry, glasses, scar, parchment blank, wand and quill idle. Hermione’s hand lowering at last. Draco’s mouth curling.
- **Faces:** Snape looks at Harry, soft and mean. Harry looks back, wary. Hermione looks at her own lowering hand, flushed. Draco looks at Harry, enjoying it.

#### C7-P3-P4

- **Beat:** A potions question. Harry says he does not know.
- **Image:** Snape looms at the end of their table. Harry’s mouth has just said the honest answer. The parchment is still blank. Hermione is vibrating with the answer.
- **Setting:** Close on their table: cauldron, crushed herbs they have not touched, torch shadow of Snape’s nose across the wood.
- **Who:** Snape, black, both hands on the table, leaning in. Harry seated, robes, glasses, empty hands turned up. Hermione beside him, lips pressed, hand twitching and staying down because she was ignored. Ron staring at his quill.
- **Faces:** Snape looks at Harry, contempt. Harry looks at him, not defiant yet, simply without the answer, embarrassed. Hermione looks at Snape, frustrated. Ron looks at Harry, sympathetic and useless.

#### C7-P3-P5

- **Beat:** Hermione waves harder. Draco smirks.
- **Image:** Hermione’s hand is up again, higher, her book bouncing. Draco is a pale profile of amusement. Snape does not turn his head one degree toward her.
- **Setting:** The two tables. Torches. The class is a held breath.
- **Who:** Hermione, half out of her seat, robes, hair, arm aching upward. Draco, platinum hair, perfect posture, a smirk, quill tapping. Snape, back partly to her, face to Harry. Harry wishing the floor would take him.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at Snape, desperate to be fair to the answer. Draco looks at Harry, smug. Snape looks only at Harry. Harry looks at the blank parchment, jaw tight.

#### C7-P3-P6

- **Beat:** Snape asks again, softer and meaner. Harry’s scar prickles.
- **Image:** Extreme close. Snape’s mouth, quiet. Harry’s scar, a tiny flare of discomfort, his fingers touching it under the table where Snape cannot see. Sweat at his hairline.
- **Setting:** Torchlight, black robes filling the background, the dungeon gone abstract.
- **Who:** Snape’s face, greasy hair, hooked nose, black eyes, no blink. Harry’s face, glasses, scar, a hand below frame on the scar. No one else.
- **Faces:** Snape looks at the scar itself, not only the boy, intense. Harry looks at Snape’s eyes and does not understand the hatred. Pain is small and private on his brow.

### Page 4 — Points

#### C7-P4-P1

- **Beat:** Harry says Hermione knows. Snape takes points for arrogance.
- **Image:** Harry points his chin toward Hermione, giving her the answer. Snape’s hand flicks as if removing something from the air: points, gone. Hermione looks stricken that helping has made it worse.
- **Setting:** The dungeon table. A house-point hourglass is not in the room; the loss is in their faces. Torches.
- **Who:** Harry, half-standing, robes, one hand indicating Hermione. Hermione, hand finally down, hurt. Snape, black, a cold small smile, straightening. Ron furious and silent.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Hermione, apologetic, then at Snape, confused. Snape looks at Harry, satisfied, calling it arrogance. Hermione looks at Harry, grateful and dismayed. Ron looks at Snape, hate.

#### C7-P4-P2

- **Beat:** More points, because Harry could not answer.
- **Image:** Snape turns his back, robes flaring, and the loss is a second flick of his fingers. Draco mouths the number, savoring it. Harry sits down hard.
- **Setting:** The dungeon. Slytherin table relaxed. Gryffindor table small.
- **Who:** Snape walking to the blackboard, black hair, black robes, chalk in hand now, done with them. Draco, Crabbe, Goyle pleased. Harry seated, glasses, blank parchment, fists under the table. Ron and Hermione on either side.
- **Faces:** Snape does not look back. Draco looks at Harry, victorious. Harry looks at his fists. He is angry now, not just embarrassed. Hermione looks at the blackboard, blinking hard.

#### C7-P4-P3

- **Beat:** The dislike looks personal.
- **Image:** A close profile of Snape at the board. He is writing ingredients. His eye, though, is slid sideways, still on Harry. This is not about a wrong answer.
- **Setting:** Blackboard, white chalk words, torch shadow, jars.
- **Who:** Snape only, sallow cheek, hooked nose, greasy hair, black collar. Harry is a soft shape in the eye’s direction, out of focus.
- **Faces:** Snape’s mouth is neutral. His eye is not. He looks at Harry with an old grievance Harry has not earned this week.

#### C7-P4-P4

- **Beat:** After class, Ron says Snape hates him. Hermione says it is obvious and unfair.
- **Image:** They climb the dungeon stairs into daylight. Ron is talking with both hands. Hermione has a book open and is still arguing. Harry is quiet between them.
- **Setting:** The stairs from dark to a window. Other students pass, glancing. Afternoon.
- **Who:** Ron, red hair, freckles, robes, angry hands. Hermione, books, bushy hair, tie still perfect, indignant. Harry, bag, glasses, scar, shoulders down.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at Harry, loyal outrage. Hermione looks at Ron, agreeing and correcting his wording at the same time. Harry looks ahead, not at them, trying to find what he did.

#### C7-P4-P5

- **Beat:** Harry asks what he did. Neither friend has an answer.
- **Image:** They stop at a window. The grounds are green below. Harry’s question hangs. Ron scratches his neck. Hermione closes her book, which means she does not know.
- **Setting:** A corridor window. Lawns, the lake, a distant willow. Daylight on their faces after the dungeon.
- **Who:** Harry, robes, glasses, one hand on the stone sill, looking at them. Ron, sweater cuffs, empty gesture. Hermione, book closed against her chest, the first time today she has no answer.
- **Faces:** Harry looks from Ron to Hermione, genuinely asking, a little small. Ron looks at his shoes, frustrated. Hermione looks at Harry, sorry, intelligent and useless. None of them look at the view.

#### C7-P4-P6

- **Beat:** Down the corridor, Snape watches them go.
- **Image:** Page-turn. From behind Snape, a black shape in the dungeon doorway far below, or at the end of the corridor. The three friends are small in the window light, not seeing him. His head is turned toward Harry.
- **Setting:** The long corridor, light at their end, dark at his. Torches between.
- **Who:** Snape, full black, hair, hands hidden in his robes, still. Harry, Ron, and Hermione walking away, bags, the red of Ron’s hair the only warm color.
- **Faces:** We see a slice of Snape’s face, cold, watching Harry’s back. The three look away from him, toward the day. Harry’s profile is troubled. Snape does not blink.

---

## Chapter 8 — Flying class and the Seeker

Brooms, the theft, the dive, and a place on the team.

### Page 1 — Brooms

#### C8-P1-P1

- **Beat:** Dawn lawn. Two lines of students. Brooms on the grass.
- **Image:** A wide, cold, beautiful panel. Dew. Two rows of first years. A broom lies in front of each like a sleeping animal. The castle is pink with sunrise behind them.
- **Setting:** The Hogwarts lawn at dawn. Mist low. The Quidditch pitch is a distant oval. Birds. Madam Hooch’s whistle not yet blown.
- **Who:** Madam Hooch, short gray hair, yellow hawk eyes, dark flying cloak, whistle, boots, standing at the end of the lines. Harry, Ron, Hermione, Neville, Draco, and the others in robes or simplified flying gear, each behind a broom. Harry’s broom is old and twiggy. Draco’s looks newer.
- **Faces:** Hooch looks along the lines, sharp. Harry looks at his broom, curious, a pull in his hands. Neville looks at his, already afraid. Draco looks at Neville, scenting weakness. Hermione looks determined.

#### C8-P1-P2

- **Beat:** Madam Hooch says nobody kicks off until she says.
- **Image:** Her hand is up, flat, a command. Whistle in the other. Students have their hands over their brooms, not yet touching. Neville’s fingers twitch.
- **Setting:** The dew lawn, close. Broom bristles detailed. Sunrise side-light.
- **Who:** Hooch, yellow eyes, cloak, whistle at her lips almost. Neville, sandy hair, robes, hand drifting down too early. Harry, glasses, hand correctly hovering. Draco, platinum hair, relaxed, watching Neville not Hooch.
- **Faces:** Hooch looks at all of them, stern care. Neville looks at his broom, anxious to get it wrong over with. Draco looks at Neville, a smile starting. Harry looks at Hooch, listening.

#### C8-P1-P3

- **Beat:** Neville kicks off early. The broom shoots up.
- **Image:** Neville is yanked vertical. The broom stands like a pole and hauls him. His feet leave the grass. Hooch’s whistle is a second too late. Dew scatters.
- **Setting:** The lawn, the lines breaking as people look up. Sky pale.
- **Who:** Neville, robes flapping, both hands locked on the handle, sandy hair blown, face already losing the ground. His broom, wild. Hooch starting to move. Harry stepping forward and stopping because he was told not to. Draco delighted.
- **Faces:** Neville looks down, terror. Hooch looks up, alarmed, professional. Harry looks up, worried. Draco looks up, laughing.

#### C8-P1-P4

- **Beat:** He hangs on, circling, terrified.
- **Image:** From the ground, Neville is a struggling shape against the morning sky, circling because he cannot steer. One hand is slipping. The broom bucks.
- **Setting:** Open sky, a tower at the edge, the class small below. Cold air.
- **Who:** Neville, robes, white-knuckled, one shoe loose. The broom. Below, out of focus, Hooch shouting instructions he cannot use.
- **Faces:** Neville looks at his slipping hand, then at the ground, mouth open, no pride left. He looks at Harry for half a second as if Harry could catch him. Harry, tiny below, looks up, helpless.

#### C8-P1-P5

- **Beat:** He falls. A crack. His wrist is wrong.
- **Image:** Neville hits the grass. The sound is a crack drawn as a sharp white break in the panel border near his wrist. The broom clatters beside him. The Remembrall, a small glass ball, rolls from his pocket.
- **Setting:** The lawn, dew, the class in a circle now, sunrise.
- **Who:** Neville on his back, robes tangled, sandy hair, one wrist clutched, face screwed. The glass Remembrall rolling toward Draco’s foot, smoke-white inside. Hooch kneeling. Harry and Hermione close. Ron pale.
- **Faces:** Neville looks at his wrist, crying, ashamed as well as hurt. Hooch looks at the wrist, competent concern. Hermione looks at Neville, worried. Draco looks at the rolling ball, not at Neville.

#### C8-P1-P6

- **Beat:** Hooch takes him to the castle and orders the class to stay down.
- **Image:** She levitates Neville gently, his wrist supported, and points a strict finger at the class. Her yellow eyes promise consequences. They are small on the grass with their brooms.
- **Setting:** The lawn, the castle doors in the distance where she is headed. The Remembrall has stopped near Draco.
- **Who:** Hooch, cloak, whistle, one hand wand-guiding Neville, the other pointing down: stay. Neville floating, crying, looking back at his broom. Draco’s shoe beside the Remembrall. Harry, Ron, Hermione standing still, obedient. The rest of the class frozen.
- **Faces:** Hooch looks at the class, severe. Neville looks back at the ball he dropped, distressed. Draco looks at the ball, thoughtful. Harry looks at Hooch, nodding, he will stay. He means it, for about one panel.

### Page 2 — The theft

#### C8-P2-P1

- **Beat:** Draco picks up the glass ball that rolled from Neville’s pocket.
- **Image:** Draco crouches, elegant even there, and pinches the Remembrall between two fingers like something faintly dirty. Smoke swirls inside it.
- **Setting:** The dew grass, abandoned brooms, the castle swallowing Hooch and Neville. The class is an uneasy ring.
- **Who:** Draco, platinum hair, fine robes, the Remembrall held up to the light. Crabbe and Goyle shadowing him. Harry a few steps away. Hermione with her arms folded. Ron glaring.
- **Faces:** Draco looks at the ball, then at Harry, a game beginning. Harry looks at the ball, recognizing it is not Draco’s. Hermione looks at Draco, warning. Ron looks at Draco’s hand, angry.

#### C8-P2-P2

- **Beat:** Hermione says it is a Remembrall, a gift, and to give it back.
- **Image:** Hermione steps in, chin up, books nowhere, both feet planted. She is smaller than Draco and clearer. He holds the ball just out of her reach, childish and precise.
- **Setting:** The lawn. Wind in the grass. Other students watching, unwilling to side against him.
- **Who:** Hermione, bushy hair, perfect tie, empty hands, robes. Draco, ball between finger and thumb, high. Harry moving closer. Ron at Harry’s shoulder.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at Draco, stern, in the right. Draco looks at her, amused, not threatened. He looks at Harry to see if this is working. Harry looks at the ball, deciding.

#### C8-P2-P3

- **Beat:** Draco mounts. He tells Harry to try to take it.
- **Image:** Draco is astride the broom, already a foot off the ground, the Remembrall dangling. He crooks a finger. Harry’s broom is at his feet.
- **Setting:** Open lawn, sky, the forbidden air. The class steps back.
- **Who:** Draco hovering, platinum hair barely moved, expensive posture, ball in one hand, broom in the other. Harry on the ground, glasses, robes, his own broom by his shoe. Hermione reaching for Harry’s sleeve. Ron looking at the sky, torn.
- **Faces:** Draco looks down at Harry, baiting, smile sharp. Harry looks up, the decision already in his shoulders. Hermione looks at Harry, alarmed. She looks at the castle, where Hooch went.

#### C8-P2-P4

- **Beat:** Harry mounts. Hermione says he will be expelled.
- **Image:** Harry swings a leg over. Hermione’s hand catches empty air. The broom stirs under him before he kicks off, eager.
- **Setting:** Grass, dawn fully up now, long shadows.
- **Who:** Harry, robes, glasses, scar, both hands on the broom, sneakers hooked. Hermione, hair blown, one hand out, face urgent. Ron stepping back to give him room, loyal and scared. Draco above, waiting.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at Harry, pleading, the word expelled serious to her. Harry looks at the ball, not at her. His mouth is sorry and his eyes are already gone. Ron looks at Harry, a nod. Draco looks delighted.

#### C8-P2-P5

- **Beat:** He kicks off. The ground drops. This feels right.
- **Image:** Harry rises. The lawn falls away cleanly. His body knows the broom. Hair and robes stream. His face changes from stubborn to astonished joy.
- **Setting:** Air. The castle tilting. The class shrinking. Clouds.
- **Who:** Harry alone in the sky, broom, glasses, scar, hands easy now, not tight. Tiny Draco ahead with the glint of the ball. Tiny Hermione below with her hands on her head.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at his own hands on the broom, then forward, a wild gladness. He looks like someone who has been in a cupboard and has just found the sky. No fear.

#### C8-P2-P6

- **Beat:** He levels with Draco above the towers.
- **Image:** Two boys and the castle towers below them. Wind. Draco is no longer higher. Harry is beside him, steady. The ball is between them in Draco’s fist.
- **Setting:** High above Hogwarts. Towers, battlements, a bird below them, the lake a dark coin. Morning gold.
- **Who:** Harry, robes snapping, glasses, messy hair worse and better, broom an extension of his arms. Draco, platinum hair finally disturbed, fine robes, the Remembrall clenched, his hover less sure.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Draco, calm, the joy banked into focus. Draco looks at Harry, the smile slipping, surprised to be matched. He looks at the ball, then at Harry’s hands.

### Page 3 — The catch

#### C8-P3-P1

- **Beat:** Draco throws the Remembrall. It falls toward stone.
- **Image:** Draco’s hand open, spiteful. The glass ball drops past Harry’s knee toward a courtyard far below. It is small and getting smaller. Harry’s head snaps down.
- **Setting:** Above a stone courtyard, flagstones rushing away in perspective. Towers on either side.
- **Who:** Draco, arm extended from the throw, sneer returning. Harry, body already tipping, eyes tracking the ball. The Remembrall, clear glass, white smoke inside, falling.
- **Faces:** Draco looks at Harry, expecting him to freeze. Harry looks only at the ball, the world narrowed. His mouth is set. Fear is there and it is not in charge.

#### C8-P3-P2

- **Beat:** Harry dives. Wind tears at his glasses.
- **Image:** A vertical panel feeling even inside the grid: Harry arrowed down, broom screaming, the ball a target below. His glasses slide; he jams them back with one finger without losing the line.
- **Setting:** Stone rushing up. A tower window. A gargoyle. The ball.
- **Who:** Harry, robes plastered by speed, hair straight back, glasses, one hand on them, the other arm forward. The broom. No one else can keep up. Draco is a speck above.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the ball, eyes watering from the wind, intent, not heroic for an audience. Concentration.

#### C8-P3-P3

- **Beat:** The courtyard rushes up. Students scream.
- **Image:** From the ground, students who have run to the courtyard cover their mouths. Harry is a blur coming down. The ball is inches from the flagstones. It looks too late.
- **Setting:** A courtyard of gray stone, morning, a few older students, Hermione and Ron arriving at a run, a teacher’s distant shout.
- **Who:** Hermione, hands over her mouth, bushy hair. Ron, white under his freckles, neck craned. Other students scattering. Harry large at the top of the panel, hand outstretched. The Remembrall at the bottom, about to break.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks up, terrified. Ron looks up, unable to blink. A student looks away. Harry’s face, distant, is all focus.

#### C8-P3-P4

- **Beat:** His hand closes on the ball inches from the flagstones.
- **Image:** The catch, inches above stone. Fingers around glass. The broom’s bristles skim a flagstone and spark. His knee almost hits. He has it.
- **Setting:** Extreme low angle on the courtyard floor. The ball and the hand and the stone. Dust.
- **Who:** Harry’s hand, robe cuff, the Remembrall intact, white smoke swirling as if relieved. A slice of his face, glasses, scar, teeth gritted. The broom.
- **Faces:** He looks at the ball in his fist, shock that it is there. Not triumph yet. Disbelief and grip.

#### C8-P3-P5

- **Beat:** He pulls up. Grass and dust flatten. He is shaking and grinning.
- **Image:** He levels out so low that dust and a discarded leaf blast sideways. Then he is upright on the broom, hovering, shaking. The grin arrives late, after the fear.
- **Setting:** The courtyard. Students upright again. Sun. The tower he fell from high above, Draco a tiny angry shape.
- **Who:** Harry hovering, robes, hair wrecked, glasses, the Remembrall raised a little, his free hand trembling on the broom. Ron whooping, running in. Hermione furious and relieved, hands still near her face.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Ron, a shaky grin. He looks at his own hand, laughing once. Ron looks at him, ecstatic. Hermione looks at him, angry because she was frightened. Draco, far, looks down, smile gone.

#### C8-P3-P6

- **Beat:** McGonagall, from the steps, icy: Potter, with her.
- **Image:** She stands on the castle steps, emerald sharp against the stone, finger pointing at him. The courtyard goes quiet. Harry’s grin dies. He drifts down.
- **Setting:** The entrance steps. Morning. The crowd parted. Hooch is not back yet. This is worse: it is McGonagall.
- **Who:** McGonagall, bun, square spectacles, emerald robes, hat, one finger extended, the other hand behind her back. Harry landing, Remembrall in his fist, dusty, small. Ron and Hermione frozen. Draco landing apart, suddenly innocent, empty hands.
- **Faces:** McGonagall looks at Harry, icy, unreadable. Harry looks at her, the sky-joy gone, waiting to be finished. He holds the ball out a little, as if it explains him. She does not look at the ball.

### Page 4 — Seeker

#### C8-P4-P1

- **Beat:** Harry follows, sure he is finished.
- **Image:** A corridor. Her heels are precise. He trots behind, the Remembrall still in his hand because he does not know where to put it. Portraits peer.
- **Setting:** Inside the castle, a long gallery, morning light, armor, a red banner. Echo.
- **Who:** McGonagall ahead, emerald, not looking back. Harry behind, dusty robes, glasses, hair destroyed by the dive, ball in one hand, the other reaching to fix nothing. A portrait of a knight leans out to stare.
- **Faces:** McGonagall’s back is straight, no comfort. Harry looks at her back, resigned, the cupboard-feeling returning. The portrait looks at him, curious.

#### C8-P4-P2

- **Beat:** She opens a door. Oliver Wood looks up from a Quidditch diagram.
- **Image:** A small room, a chalkboard of Quidditch arrows, broom in the corner. Wood looks up, older, intense. McGonagall is in the door with a first year, which is not what he expected.
- **Setting:** A team room. Pads, a Quaffle, red and gold, morning through a high window. The diagram is a mess of brilliant tactics.
- **Who:** Oliver Wood, brown hair, Gryffindor practice robes, a leather arm guard, chalk in hand. McGonagall, emerald, ushering. Harry, dusty, the glass ball absurd in a strategy room, glasses, scar.
- **Faces:** Wood looks at Harry, puzzled, measuring. McGonagall looks at Wood, the ice becoming purpose. Harry looks at the diagram, lost, still expecting detention.

#### C8-P4-P3

- **Beat:** She says she has found him a Seeker.
- **Image:** McGonagall’s hand is on Harry’s shoulder, the first gentle touch from her. She is almost smiling. Wood’s chalk stops. Harry blinks.
- **Setting:** The team room. The diagram behind them like a future.
- **Who:** McGonagall, spectacles, emerald, hand on Harry’s dusty shoulder. Harry, small, Remembrall, robes. Wood, arm guard, chalk, leaning in.
- **Faces:** McGonagall looks at Wood, proud of her find, a rare warmth. She looks at Harry, brisk approval. Wood looks at Harry’s hands, then his size, interest replacing doubt. Harry looks at her, the word Seeker not landed yet. His mouth is open.

#### C8-P4-P4

- **Beat:** Wood measures Harry’s size, then his hands, then grins.
- **Image:** Wood crouches to Harry’s height, takes in the skinny shoulders, then turns Harry’s free hand over, the one that caught the ball. The grin is a captain seeing a season.
- **Setting:** The team room, Quaffle on a bench, red light from a banner.
- **Who:** Wood, practice robes, arm guard, both hands around the idea of Harry. Harry, glasses, scar, the Remembrall in the other fist, standing still for inspection. McGonagall by the door, arms folded, satisfied.
- **Faces:** Wood looks at the catching hand, then at Harry’s face, a grin breaking. Harry looks at Wood, hope arriving carefully. McGonagall looks at them, the icy panel from the steps rewritten.

#### C8-P4-P5

- **Beat:** On the walk, Wood explains the Snitch in three quick sketches.
- **Image:** They walk a cloister. Wood draws in the air with his finger: a tiny gold ball, hoops, Harry as a dot. Harry’s eyes track the invisible sketches and only half keep up. He still holds Neville’s ball.
- **Setting:** An open cloister, lawns through the arches, late morning, students passing and staring at a first year with the Quidditch captain.
- **Who:** Wood, tall, brown hair, gesturing a whole sport. Harry trotting beside, dusty robes, glasses, Remembrall, nodding too fast. A few older students noticing.
- **Faces:** Wood looks at the air where his diagram is, passionate. Harry looks at Wood’s hands, excited and behind. He looks like a boy who has been chosen and is afraid to drop it.

#### C8-P4-P6

- **Beat:** Ron and Hermione ambush him. He holds up the Remembrall and says he is on the team.
- **Image:** Page-turn. Around a corner, Ron and Hermione are waiting, braced for expulsion news. Harry holds up the glass ball like a trophy and his face is the news. Ron yells. Hermione’s anger collapses into a laugh she did not schedule.
- **Setting:** A sunny corridor corner. Armor. A window. The end of the morning.
- **Who:** Harry, dusty, grinning, Remembrall raised, glasses, scar, robes. Ron, red hair, freckles, both fists up, shouting. Hermione, books, hair, one hand over her smile, the other hitting Harry’s arm lightly for scaring her.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at them, proud and disbelieving, the sky still in his eyes. Ron looks at him, joy. Hermione looks at him, fond and exasperated, the expulsion fear leaving. The ball looks tiny and important between them.

---

## Chapter 9 — The midnight duel and the three-headed dog

A trap, a chase, Fluffy, and the memory of a package.

### Page 1 — The challenge

#### C9-P1-P1

- **Beat:** Draco stops Harry in the entrance hall, Crabbe and Goyle behind him.
- **Image:** The entrance hall is busy with afternoon light. Draco steps into Harry’s path so neatly it looks polite. The two big boys close the gap behind.
- **Setting:** Hogwarts entrance hall. Stone, the front doors open to sun, students, a staircase, the ceiling high.
- **Who:** Draco, platinum hair, expensive robes, green lining, hands behind his back. Crabbe and Goyle, bulky, shadows. Harry, clean robes again, glasses, bag, scar. Ron beside him. Hermione a step back, already suspicious.
- **Faces:** Draco looks at Harry, pleasant and false. Harry looks at him, guarded. Ron looks at Crabbe and Goyle, measuring the exit. Hermione looks at Draco’s smile, not buying it.

#### C9-P1-P2

- **Beat:** A wizard’s duel. Midnight. The trophy room. No teachers.
- **Image:** Draco leans in, voice low, so the hall cannot hear. His finger taps an imaginary watch. Harry’s bag strap tightens in his fist.
- **Setting:** A quieter side of the hall, under a suit of armor. Sun and dust.
- **Who:** Draco close, sleek, the challenge delivered like a gift. Harry, chin slightly down, listening. Ron leaning in, red hair, already tempted. The armor.
- **Faces:** Draco looks at Harry, eyes bright with the trick, smile small. Harry looks at Draco’s mouth, weighing pride against sense. Ron looks at Harry, eager, the decision tilting the wrong way.

#### C9-P1-P3

- **Beat:** Hermione says it is a trap and against a stack of rules.
- **Image:** She inserts herself, a book pressed to her chest like a shield of regulations. She is listing rules on her fingers. Draco is already walking away, job done.
- **Setting:** The hall. Draco’s back receding with Crabbe and Goyle. A clock.
- **Who:** Hermione, bushy hair, perfect tie, book, three fingers up and more coming. Harry and Ron, bags, being lectured. Draco from behind, platinum hair, unhurried.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at the boys, urgent, right, and annoyed she has to be. Harry looks at Draco’s back, pride prickling. Ron looks at Hermione, defensive. Draco does not look back; his shoulders look pleased.

#### C9-P1-P4

- **Beat:** Harry looks at Ron. Ron says they have to go.
- **Image:** A two-shot in a window alcove. The afternoon is gold. Ron’s mind is made. Harry’s is making itself. Hermione is a frustrated blur walking ahead.
- **Setting:** A corridor alcove, suits of armor, a view of the lawn where Harry flew.
- **Who:** Harry, glasses, scar, bag held in both hands. Ron, freckles, maroon sweater cuffs, hands open, convincing. Hermione ahead, not in the decision.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at Harry, loyal and reckless, a grin. Harry looks at Ron, the flying-courage still in him, nodding once. He looks worried and he nods anyway.

#### C9-P1-P5

- **Beat:** Night. Two boys slip out of the Gryffindor corridor.
- **Image:** A portrait hole swings. Harry and Ron creep out in pajamas and dressing gowns, wands out, ridiculous and brave. The common room fire behind them is the last warm light.
- **Setting:** Night castle. The Gryffindor entrance, a painting swung open, stone corridor, moonlight through a high window. Torches low.
- **Who:** Harry in oversized gray pajamas, glasses, wand, bare feet in slippers, hair worse from bed. Ron in a maroon pajama top that was a brother’s, wand, freckles, red hair. The Fat Lady’s portrait, annoyed, in a pink dress, swinging shut.
- **Faces:** Harry looks down the corridor, alert. Ron looks at Harry, a nervous grin. The Fat Lady looks at them, disapproving, mouth pursed.

#### C9-P1-P6

- **Beat:** Hermione is already waiting, furious and whispering.
- **Image:** She steps from behind a suit of armor, fully dressed, arms folded, whisper-shouting. They jump. Her hair is a cloud of indignation.
- **Setting:** The moonlit corridor. Armor. A tapestry. Far off, a torch.
- **Who:** Hermione in her day robes and perfect tie even at midnight, wand in hand, books not with her for once. Harry and Ron in pajamas, wands up then lowered, guilty.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at them, furious, whispering, frightened under the fury. Harry looks at her, caught. Ron looks at her, betrayed by her competence. She looks back the way they came, wanting them to return, and they do not.

### Page 2 — The trap

#### C9-P2-P1

- **Beat:** Neville, locked out in his nightshirt, begs to come.
- **Image:** Neville stands in the corridor in a pale nightshirt, toad in one hand, the portrait hole shut behind him. He looks cold and abandoned. He grabs Ron’s sleeve.
- **Setting:** Outside the common room. Night. The Fat Lady’s portrait is turned away, refusing to open.
- **Who:** Neville, sandy hair, long nightshirt, slippers missing one, Trevor the toad in his palm. Ron in maroon pajamas. Harry in gray. Hermione, robes, exasperated anew.
- **Faces:** Neville looks at them, pleading, ashamed, he forgot the password. Ron looks at the toad, then at Neville, groaning a yes. Hermione looks at Neville, sympathy winning over rules. Harry looks down the corridor, time running out.

#### C9-P2-P2

- **Beat:** The trophy room is empty. Draco is not there.
- **Image:** Glass cases of cups and shields. Moonlight. No Draco. Their four reflections look back at them, small and foolish.
- **Setting:** The trophy room. Silver and gold catching moonlight. A polished floor. Silence. Banners in cases.
- **Who:** Harry, Ron, Hermione, Neville, pajamas and one set of robes, wands lowered. Trevor on Neville’s shoulder. No Slytherins. Their reflections.
- **Faces:** Harry looks around, suspicion arriving. Ron looks at the door they entered, uneasy. Hermione looks at Harry with an I-told-you-so she does not enjoy. Neville looks at a big cup, briefly distracted, then scared again.

#### C9-P2-P3

- **Beat:** Filch’s lamp turns the corner. His cat’s eyes gleam.
- **Image:** A sick yellow lamp. Filch’s stooped shape. At his ankles, Mrs. Norris, a skinny dust-colored cat, eyes like lamps of her own. The children are at the trophy-room door, caught in the edge of the light.
- **Setting:** The corridor outside the trophy room. Night. Stone. The lamp makes huge shadows.
- **Who:** Argus Filch, stringy gray hair, brown shabby coat, a bunch of keys, the lamp, a furious stoop. Mrs. Norris, hairless-looking cat, tail stiff. The four students just inside the light’s reach.
- **Faces:** Filch looks down the corridor, hunting, a grim joy. Mrs. Norris looks straight at the door, she knows. Harry looks at the cat, alarm. Hermione looks at a side passage, calculating.

#### C9-P2-P4

- **Beat:** They run. Hermione hisses that she told them.
- **Image:** Four sets of feet running. Hermione is half-turned, whisper-shouting, still faster than Neville. The lamp light chases their heels.
- **Setting:** A narrow corridor, suits of armor they bang into, a tapestry they fling aside. Night.
- **Who:** Hermione in front, robes flying, wand lit faintly. Harry pulling Neville. Ron behind, red hair, looking back at the lamp. Neville, nightshirt, toad clutched to his chest, gasping.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks back at the boys, angry and scared. Harry looks at Neville, making sure he is there. Ron looks back at Filch’s light, pale. Neville looks at the floor, trying not to fall.

#### C9-P2-P5

- **Beat:** A door. They pile through.
- **Image:** Harry hits a door and it opens. They tumble in a knot of pajamas, robes, and toad. The door is tall and they were not looking at the sign of the corridor.
- **Setting:** A threshold. Behind them the corridor and Filch’s distant lamp. Ahead, darkness and a smell of dog, not seen yet.
- **Who:** Harry’s hand on the door, gray pajamas. Ron falling past him. Hermione grabbing Neville’s nightshirt. Neville hugging Trevor. All four crossing at once.
- **Faces:** Harry looks into the dark room, not yet afraid of the right thing. Hermione looks back at the corridor, relieved for one second. Neville’s eyes are shut.

#### C9-P2-P6

- **Beat:** Filch passes. They breathe. A growl comes from behind them.
- **Image:** They are pressed to the inside of the door, listening. Filch’s lamp light slides under the door and moves on. Their shoulders drop. Then all four faces change, because the growl is in the room with them, low and triple.
- **Setting:** Pitch dark inside. A line of lamplight under the door, then gone. Stone floor. The growl vibrates dust.
- **Who:** The four, backs to the door, faces turning over their shoulders into the black. Harry, glasses glinting. Ron, freckles gone pale. Hermione, wand raised, the light not on yet. Neville, toad under his chin.
- **Faces:** Relief collapses. Harry looks into the dark, eyes wide. Hermione looks past him, dread. Ron looks at Harry. Neville looks like he might faint before he sees anything.

### Page 3 — Fluffy

#### C9-P3-P1

- **Beat:** A dog the size of the room. Three heads.
- **Image:** Hermione’s wand-light flares. Fluffy fills the room. Three huge heads, three sets of teeth, one massive body, paws the size of doors. The children are a strip of pajamas at the bottom.
- **Setting:** The forbidden third-floor corridor room. Bare stone, a high ceiling that is still not high enough, chains or none — he is simply there. Wand-light and eye-shine.
- **Who:** Fluffy, a monstrous three-headed dog, shaggy, brown-black, drool, three collars maybe, eyes catching the wand. Harry, Ron, Hermione, Neville tiny in front of the door. Trevor leaping from Neville’s hands.
- **Faces:** All three dog heads look at the children, lips back, a growl in stereo. Harry looks up, awe and terror. Hermione looks at the paws, thinking even now. Ron looks at the middle teeth. Neville’s face is already slack.

#### C9-P3-P2

- **Beat:** Three different snarls. Neville faints.
- **Image:** Each head snarls on its own: left furious, middle curious-cruel, right barking. Neville’s eyes roll and he goes down in a pale heap. Trevor lands on his chest.
- **Setting:** The stone room, wand-light swinging as Hermione jolts.
- **Who:** Fluffy’s three heads, detailed, wet noses, huge teeth. Neville on the floor, nightshirt, sandy hair, peaceful in the faint. Trevor the toad, indignant. Harry and Ron grabbing Neville’s arms. Hermione holding the light.
- **Faces:** The dog heads look at the fallen boy and the standing ones, distracted between targets. Harry looks at Neville, urgent. Ron looks at the left head, which looked at him. Hermione looks at the floor under the paws.

#### C9-P3-P3

- **Beat:** Harry sees the paws planted on a trapdoor.
- **Image:** Between the huge paws, a wooden trapdoor with an iron ring. Harry’s eyeline is a clear arrow to it. The dog is not loose in a room; it is a lid.
- **Setting:** The floor, highlighted. Stone, scratches from claws, the trapdoor centered. Wand-light.
