## Chapter 12 — Nicolas Flamel and the Stone

Searching, the book, what the Stone does, and the wrong man watched.

### Page 1 — The search

#### C12-P1-P1

- **Beat:** The library at night. Stacks tower over them.
- **Image:** Three children at a long table, dwarfed by shelves that go up into dark. Lamps with green shades. A fortress of books they have already opened.
- **Setting:** Hogwarts library, restricted section roped off in the deep background. Night. Quiet enforced by the room itself. Dust, ladders, a globe.
- **Who:** Harry, robes, glasses, a list of crossed-out names. Ron, head on his hand, freckles, maroon sweater, bored and loyal. Hermione, surrounded by open books, quill, bushy hair, the engine of the search. Madam Pince is not in this panel yet, only her desk far off.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at a page, tireless. Harry looks at a book, hopeful and fading. Ron looks at Harry, a silent how-long. The shelves look down, indifferent.

#### C12-P1-P2

- **Beat:** Hermione’s finger down an index. Nothing.
- **Image:** Close on an index page. Her finger stops, lifts, moves. No Flamel. A smudge of ink on her fingertip. Harry’s reflection in the polished table, disappointed.
- **Setting:** Green lamp light. The table. Night.
- **Who:** Hermione’s hand, ink, a bitten nail she would deny. Harry’s face faint in the wood, glasses. No one else needed.
- **Faces:** Her finger is the expression: precise, then a small defeated tap. Harry looks at the empty line, mouth tight.

#### C12-P1-P3

- **Beat:** Madam Pince looms and tells them to stop whispering.
- **Image:** She is suddenly there, thin, tall, a shadow with a bun and a glare. One finger on her lips, the other hand closing a book they were too loud over. They shrink like first years, which they are.
- **Setting:** The library aisle. Her domain. A cart of books.
- **Who:** Madam Pince, severe, dark dress, bun, keys, ink-stained fingers, taller than the children. Harry, Ron, Hermione seated, guilty. Ron’s elbow on a book she wants.
- **Faces:** Pince looks at them, suspicious, especially at Hermione, whom she usually trusts. Hermione looks up, apologetic, a fellow believer in library rules caught breaking one. Ron looks at his elbow, moving it fast. Harry looks at the closed book, mourning it.

#### C12-P1-P4

- **Beat:** Ron is asleep on a book. Harry pokes him.
- **Image:** Ron’s cheek is on a page, drooling a little, red hair spilled. Harry’s quill pokes his ear, gentle. Hermione does not look up. A clock says it is very late.
- **Setting:** The table, lamps, the restricted rope in the dark. Quiet comedy.
- **Who:** Ron asleep, freckles, sweater, mouth open. Harry, glasses, a small smile, quill as a poke. Hermione, still reading, a stack to her eyebrow.
- **Faces:** Ron’s face is slack, peaceful, a dream of food maybe. Harry looks at him, fond. Hermione looks at her page, a tiny smile she will not admit, she heard the poke.

#### C12-P1-P5

- **Beat:** A strip of empty days: breakfast, class, library, nothing.
- **Image:** A montage panel divided into three thin beats. Left: the Great Hall, Harry staring at Snape over porridge while Ron eats. Middle: Charms, a feather, Hermione’s hand up, Harry’s mind elsewhere. Right: the library again, the same table, more books, same nothing.
- **Setting:** Hall, classroom, library. Days passing by the change of light and their heavier eyes.
- **Who:** Harry, Ron, Hermione in each slice, robes, the search wearing on them. Snape in the first slice, black, at the staff table, not looking at Harry this time. Hedwig is not here. Books accumulate.
- **Faces:** Harry looks more tired and more fixed. Ron looks loyal and sick of books. Hermione looks undefeated. Snape looks at his plate, ordinary, which frustrates Harry.

#### C12-P1-P6

- **Beat:** In a lesson, the scar twinges. Snape is watching from across the room.
- **Image:** A quiet class. Harry’s hand goes to his forehead. Across the dungeon or the hall — this one is the dungeon, a double Potions hour — Snape is looking at him again. The pain and the look arrive together.
- **Setting:** Potions dungeon. Cauldrons actually bubbling today. Torchlight. Steam. Other students working.
- **Who:** Harry, robes, glasses, one hand on the scar, the other on a stirrer, wincing. Snape at the front, black, still, eyes on him. Hermione beside Harry, noticing the wince. Ron on the other side, noticing Snape.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the cauldron, pain private, then up, and meets Snape’s eyes. Snape looks at him, unreadable. Hermione looks at Harry’s scar, worried. Ron looks at Snape, the theory hardening.

### Page 2 — The book

#### C12-P2-P1

- **Beat:** Hermione slams down a huge book she checked out weeks ago for fun.
- **Image:** The common room. A book the size of a hearthstone hits the table. Dust jumps. Ron’s wizard chess pieces scatter and swear in tiny voices. Hermione stands over it, triumphant and sooty from the library.
- **Setting:** Gryffindor common room, evening, fire, homework chaos. Red hangings.
- **Who:** Hermione, hair bigger than usual, robes, both hands on the book, proud. Harry leaning in, glasses, scar. Ron on the floor chasing a chess knight that is kicking him. The book, leather, old, a title about alchemy too worn to shout.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at them, a blaze of I-found-it. Harry looks at the book, hope surging. Ron looks at the knight, then at her face, and forgets the knight. The knight looks furious.

#### C12-P2-P2

- **Beat:** Her finger finds the name.
- **Image:** The page. Her ink-stained finger underlines Nicolas Flamel in a sea of type. A small engraved portrait of an old man beside it. The name is finally real.
- **Setting:** Firelight on paper. The common-room table. Night.
- **Who:** Hermione’s hand. Harry’s hand at the margin, not touching the name, reverent. Ron’s freckled finger pointing from the other side, a second late.
- **Faces:** Only partial. Hermione’s mouth at the top, a satisfied line. Harry’s eyes in the shine of his glasses at the edge, wide. They all look at the name.

#### C12-P2-P3

- **Beat:** All three lean in. Firelight on the page.
- **Image:** Three heads in a triangle over the book. Hair: black, red, brown. The fire makes the page gold. The rest of the common room does not exist.
- **Setting:** Close, warm, the book a campfire.
- **Who:** Harry, glasses sliding down, scar, robes. Ron, freckles, a smudge of chess-piece dirt. Hermione, bushy hair tickling both of them, she does not notice.
- **Faces:** They look down, breathless, together. Harry looks hungry for the answer. Hermione looks like a guide. Ron looks like he might finally enjoy a book.

#### C12-P2-P4

- **Beat:** Flamel is centuries old because of one object.
- **Image:** Hermione reads, finger moving. On the page, a suggestion of a long life: the engraved old man, and a smaller note of a date that is impossible. Their faces age with the idea, not literally.
- **Setting:** The same table. A clock. Other students laughing far away, ordinary.
- **Who:** The three, as before. The book doing the talking. Hermione’s mouth moving. Harry very still. Ron’s eyebrows up.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at the page, awed by the fact. Harry looks at the old engraved face, thinking of someone who does not want to die. Ron looks at Harry, the joke of treasure arriving, then fading into seriousness.

#### C12-P2-P5

- **Beat:** A picture of a small red stone.
- **Image:** The engraving fills the panel. A small, irregular, deep red stone, drawn simply, glowing in the ink. It is not large. It does not look like it should hold a war. That is why it is frightening.
- **Setting:** The page, firelight, their thumbs at the corners holding the book open.
- **Who:** No full bodies. Three thumbs. The stone.
- **Faces:** None. The stone is the face of the chapter. It looks inert and priceless.

#### C12-P2-P6

- **Beat:** Harry says that is what the dog is standing on.
- **Image:** He sits back. The common room returns. He looks at the fire, but he is seeing Fluffy’s paws and the trapdoor. His hand is flat on the picture of the stone.
- **Setting:** The common room, evening, friends around, the book open.
- **Who:** Harry, robes, glasses, scar, one hand on the red engraving. Ron and Hermione watching him, the conclusion landing on them a second later. A chess piece tugging Ron’s sock, ignored.
- **Faces:** Harry looks into the fire, certain, uneasy. Hermione looks at him, connecting dog, package, stone, nodding slowly. Ron looks at the book, then at the ceiling, toward the third floor, a silent oh.

### Page 3 — What the Stone does

#### C12-P3-P1

- **Beat:** Hermione explains gold, and a potion that keeps you alive.
- **Image:** She holds the book like a teacher, two fingers up: gold, life. The fire paints the red stone’s color onto her hand. The boys listen without jokes.
- **Setting:** The common room, closer to the fire now, the book on Harry’s knees.
- **Who:** Hermione, robes, hair, the book. Harry and Ron on the hearthrug, cross-legged, mugs. Other students out of focus, playing exploding snap, ordinary children.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at them, grave, making sure they understand the size of it. Harry looks at her fingers, then at the scar in the shine of a mug. Ron looks at her, the poor-boy understanding of endless gold flickering, then shame at thinking it.

#### C12-P3-P2

- **Beat:** Ron says that would end being poor and end being dead.
- **Image:** Ron says it simply, no greed in the drawing, just a boy who owns one rat and a lot of brothers. His hand is open, empty. Harry looks at that empty hand.
- **Setting:** The hearthrug. Fire. A hole in Ron’s sock, visible, not mocked.
- **Who:** Ron, maroon sweater, freckles, red hair, open hand. Harry beside him, quiet. Hermione with the book closed now, respecting the sentence.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at the fire, matter-of-fact, a little sad. Harry looks at Ron, affection, he will not tease this. Hermione looks at Ron, soft, the nightmare comment from Halloween fully buried.

#### C12-P3-P3

- **Beat:** Harry is quiet, thinking of his parents.
- **Image:** He is in the chair, small. The fire becomes, just for him, a soft not-quite-memory: a woman with kind eyes, a man with glasses, too vague to be a picture, more a warmth. He does not tell the others yet.
- **Setting:** The common room, but the edges fade. Firelight.
- **Who:** Harry, glasses, scar, knees up, mug forgotten. The almost-parents are light, not bodies, no costumes clear. Ron and Hermione are present and respectfully out of focus.
- **Faces:** Harry looks into the fire, young and old at once, grief he is only learning the shape of. His mouth is still. Ron looks at him and does not poke. Hermione looks at the book, giving him the silence.

#### C12-P3-P4

- **Beat:** The Stone is here, and someone inside the school wants it.
- **Image:** Hermione taps the table once, the detective again. A simple map of the castle is a folded letter she draws on: a dog, a trapdoor, an arrow inward. The danger is inside, not at the gates.
- **Setting:** The table. Night. The common room thinning out as students go to bed.
- **Who:** Hermione drawing with a quill, precise. Harry and Ron flanking, watching the arrow point at their own school. The ink dog is silly and serious.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at the arrow, certain. Harry looks at it, the Halloween blood on Snape’s leg returning to his mind. Ron looks at the ink dog, grimacing, he remembers the teeth.

#### C12-P3-P5

- **Beat:** Harry says Snape. The bleeding leg. The third-floor corridor.
- **Image:** Harry counts it on his fingers, a child’s case. On the last finger he looks very sure. The fire pops like agreement.
- **Setting:** Late common room. Just the three. Embers.
- **Who:** Harry, three fingers up, glasses, scar, robes loosened. Ron nodding hard. Hermione listening, not fully sold, quill still.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at them, convinced, a little angry. Ron looks at Harry, all in. Hermione looks at the third finger, thoughtful, she wants one more proof and knows she may not get it.

#### C12-P3-P6

- **Beat:** Ron agrees at once. Hermione says they need proof.
- **Image:** Ron’s hand slaps the table, yes. Hermione’s hand settles on the book, wait. Harry is between them, the theory already his. The panel holds the friendship and the disagreement without a fight.
- **Setting:** The dying fire. Bedtime. The portrait hole in the background, the Fat Lady dozing.
- **Who:** The three. Ron, eager fist. Hermione, book, caution. Harry, the scar and the certainty.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at Hermione, impatient loyalty. Hermione looks at Harry, not at Ron, careful love of the truth. Harry looks at the embers, he will watch Snape. He is not angry at her. He is decided.

### Page 4 — Watching

#### C12-P4-P1

- **Beat:** They trail Snape, badly hidden behind a suit of armor.
- **Image:** Three sets of feet and three heads stick out from behind one suit of armor, which is not wide enough. Snape walks ahead, black, and the armor’s spear wobbles because Ron bumped it.
- **Setting:** A daytime corridor. Students. High windows. Ridiculous spy-work.
- **Who:** Snape, black robes, greasy hair, papers in hand, walking with purpose. Harry, Ron, Hermione crammed behind the armor, robes, Hermione hissing, Ron’s elbow in Harry’s ear. The armor, dignified and abused.
- **Faces:** Snape looks ahead, he has not turned, bored with his own errand. Harry looks at Snape’s back, intent. Hermione looks at Ron, furious whisper. Ron looks at the spear he bumped, oops. The armor looks ashamed.

#### C12-P4-P2

- **Beat:** He turns. They freeze. He walks on.
- **Image:** Snape in profile, a half-turn, eyes sliding toward the armor. They are statues. A bead of sweat on Ron’s nose. Snape’s mouth thins, maybe he knows, and he chooses to walk on. His cloak snaps.
- **Setting:** The corridor, sun, the armor’s shadow hiding them badly.
- **Who:** Snape, sallow, hooked nose, black, papers. The three, frozen, Harry’s glasses a giveaway glint. He looks at the glint and lets it go.
- **Faces:** Snape looks at the armor, a flicker of contempt, then away. Harry looks at him, not breathing. Hermione looks at Harry’s glasses, too late. Ron looks at Snape’s back, when he is gone, and sags.

#### C12-P4-P3

- **Beat:** Quirrell passes the other way and flinches when he sees Snape.
- **Image:** In the same corridor, Quirrell comes from the other direction. He sees Snape. His whole body flinches, turban tipping, a stammer in his hands. Snape does not even slow. They pass like a shark and a minnow.
- **Setting:** The corridor. Students giving both teachers room. Daylight.
- **Who:** Quirrell, purple robes, large turban, pale, clutching a book to his chest. Snape, black, tall, unbothered, papers. Harry peeking, seeing the flinch and filing it under Snape-is-scary, not under Quirrell-is-guilty.
- **Faces:** Quirrell looks at Snape, fear, a sweat, eyes darting. Snape looks through him, cold. Harry looks at Quirrell’s fear and feels it proves his theory. He looks satisfied, which is the mistake.

#### C12-P4-P4

- **Beat:** Hermione notices the flinch. Harry is fixed on Snape.
- **Image:** Two eyelines. Hermione watches Quirrell’s back, thoughtful, a question. Harry watches Snape’s back, case closed. They are looking opposite ways in the same panel.
- **Setting:** Behind the armor, or just stepping out. Afternoon. The corridor emptying.
- **Who:** Hermione, books, hair, head turned toward the purple turban. Harry, glasses, head turned toward the black robes. Ron between them, looking at both, not knowing who to follow.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at Quirrell, uneasy, not ready to accuse, a small frown. Harry looks at Snape, certain, jaw set. Ron looks from one to the other, loyal to Harry, a little lost.

#### C12-P4-P5

- **Beat:** That night Harry writes Flamel on a scrap and burns it.
- **Image:** In the dormitory, after lights out. Harry kneels by the stove or a candle. He writes the name, looks at it, and puts the scrap in the flame. Ash curls. He is afraid of the name on paper in a school where the thief lives.
- **Setting:** The Gryffindor boys’ dormitory. Four-posters, moonlight, Ron a lump in the next bed, Neville asleep, Trevor on a bedpost. A single candle.
- **Who:** Harry in gray pajamas, glasses, scar, kneeling, the scrap in his fingers, almost burned. Hedwig on a perch by the window, gold eyes open, watching him. Ron’s red hair on a pillow.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the burning name, serious, protective of the secret. Hedwig looks at him, calm. Ron does not look; he is asleep, mouth open, trusting Harry to still be there in the morning.

#### C12-P4-P6

- **Beat:** The ash curls. He says they have to keep the Stone where it is.
- **Image:** Page-turn. The ash lifts. Harry’s face is lit from below by the last of the flame. He is not speaking loudly; Ron is asleep. The promise is to himself and to the dark. Hedwig’s eyes are the witness.
- **Setting:** The dormitory, moonlight and a dying candle. The castle quiet. The third floor somewhere above, unshown, the dog standing on the Stone.
- **Who:** Harry, pajamas, glasses, empty fingers, ash on one thumb. Hedwig, white, gold-eyed. The sleeping friends as soft shapes.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the ash, then toward the window and the castle, resolved. Young, stubborn, a little afraid. Hedwig looks at him, steady. The decision sits on his face more clearly than any word.

---

## Chapter 13 — Christmas and the Mirror of Erised

An empty castle, a father’s cloak, a mirror, and a warning.

### Page 1 — Staying

#### C13-P1-P1

- **Beat:** The castle empties. Carriages leave. Harry watches from a window.
- **Image:** From a high window, horseless carriages and students stream away across the snow-dusted lawn. The castle behind Harry is already quieter. His hand is on the cold glass.
- **Setting:** A corridor window, December, pale sun, first snow, the lake iron-gray. Trunks, scarves, goodbyes far below.
- **Who:** Harry, school robes, a red-and-gold scarf, glasses, scar, one palm on the window. He is staying. Below, tiny students, and one red-haired family group that will not all leave. Hedwig on his shoulder or in the reflection, white against the winter.
- **Faces:** Harry looks down, lonely and pretending not to be. His mouth is still. Hedwig looks out too, calm. He does not wave. No one is looking up for him.

#### C13-P1-P2

- **Beat:** Ron stays too, so Harry will not be alone.
- **Image:** Ron appears at Harry’s shoulder with a letter in Molly’s handwriting and a lopsided grin that means he argued with his mother and won a Christmas at school. Two scarves. Two boys. The carriages can go.
- **Setting:** The same window. Snow. A nearly empty corridor. A house-elf-free ordinary stone stillness.
- **Who:** Ron, maroon sweater, Weasley scarf, freckles, red hair, the letter half-folded, a packed night-bag at his feet that says he is staying. Harry, scarf, glasses, the loneliness interrupted.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at Harry, shy about the kindness, covering it with a joke in his eyes. Harry looks at him, surprised, then a real smile, the hollow filling. They look at the window together, and the leaving students matter less.

#### C13-P1-P3

- **Beat:** Christmas morning. A pile of Weasley jumpers, one labeled for Harry.
- **Image:** The common room, morning, a small tree someone enchanted, real presents for once. A lumpy parcel in brown paper with Harry’s name in a mother’s hand. Ron is already wearing a new maroon sweater and looks both pleased and persecuted.
- **Setting:** Gryffindor common room, Christmas morning, snow light, fire, almost empty, which makes the presents louder. Paper, string, a few cards.
- **Who:** Harry in pajamas and the school scarf, kneeling, the parcel in his lap. Ron in a fresh maroon hand-knit sweater, hair wild from sleep, his own opened boxes around him. Fred and George’s parcels are here too, identical paper, chaos pending.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at his name on the paper, stunned, gentle. He looks at Ron. Ron looks at the sweater on his own body, groaning, and at Harry’s face, glad. A shy pride.

#### C13-P1-P4

- **Beat:** He pulls the homemade sweater on. The first gift that is only his.
- **Image:** The sweater goes over his head. It is too big, emerald green — Molly did not know his size and guessed with love — with a big H. His hair comes out worse. He looks down at the H and touches it.
- **Setting:** The common room. Paper around his knees. Snow light.
- **Who:** Harry, the green knit sweater, pajamas underneath, glasses back on, scar, both hands on the letter H. Ron in maroon, watching. No Dursley gift in sight. That absence is the background.
- **Faces:** Harry looks down at the sweater, a tight throat, a smile he does not aim at anyone. Ron looks at him, soft, a little embarrassed by his mother’s reach, and glad. Harry does not look at Ron yet. He is having a private first.

#### C13-P1-P5

- **Beat:** Fred and George set off crackers. Smoke and a terrible smell.
- **Image:** The twins, who stayed or whose gifts explode anyway — they are here, they stayed for the joke — yank a wizard cracker. It detonates into purple smoke and a smell that bends the tree. A hat or a bad joke falls out. Harry laughs.
- **Setting:** The common room, now purple. The tree tilted. Snow outside untouched and clean, a contrast.
- **Who:** Fred and George, identical red hair, matching sweaters with letters, identical wicked grins, the cracker between them. Harry in the green H sweater, laughing. Ron coughing, also laughing. A singed card.
- **Faces:** The twins look at each other, delighted with the damage. Harry looks at them, laughing with his whole face, the cupboard years not in this panel. Ron looks at the smoke, choking, happy.

#### C13-P1-P6

- **Beat:** Harry laughs with his whole face. Ron looks pleased with himself.
- **Image:** Page of warmth. Close on the two of them in the clearing smoke, sweaters, Christmas, no fame. Ron’s look says this was the plan: not the crackers, the staying.
- **Setting:** The common room settling. Purple haze. Fire. Snow light. The tree crooked and fine.
- **Who:** Harry, green sweater, glasses, messy hair, a paper crown from the cracker crooked on his head. Ron, maroon sweater, freckles, a matching crown, arms folded, pretending he is not proud.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Ron, bright, thank you without a speech. Ron looks at Harry, pleased, ears red, a grin he tries to make smaller. It does not get smaller.

### Page 2 — The cloak

#### C13-P2-P1

- **Beat:** One parcel has no name. The note says the cloak was his father’s.
- **Image:** Under the tree, a last parcel, soft, no tag. Harry holds a small note. His face goes still. The sweater morning becomes something older.
- **Setting:** The common room, later, twins gone to make trouble, Ron nearby. The fire low. The parcel is wrapped in plain paper, slightly silvery at the fold.
- **Who:** Harry, green sweater, glasses, scar, the note in both hands. Ron leaning in, curious, not grabbing. The parcel on Harry’s knees, unopened a second more.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the note, the smile gone, replaced by a careful ache. He looks at Ron, needing a witness. Ron looks at the note, then at Harry, quiet, the jokes parked. Respect.

#### C13-P2-P2

- **Beat:** He lifts the cloth. His hand is gone.
- **Image:** The cloak spills out, fluid, silver-gray, more water than wool. Harry’s hand goes under it and the hand ends at the wrist. Ron’s eyes are huge. The fire shows through where a hand should be.
- **Setting:** Close on their knees and the cloth. Firelight. No one else.
- **Who:** Harry, sweater, the cloak in his lap, one hand vanished. Ron, both hands hovering, not touching yet, red hair, freckles. The note on the floor, his father’s name a quiet weight.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the missing hand, wonder and a lump in the throat. He looks like a boy meeting his father through an object. Ron looks at the emptiness, delighted and hushed. He looks at Harry’s face and does not cheer loudly.

#### C13-P2-P3

- **Beat:** Night. Only his shoes show under the hem.
- **Image:** A corridor at night. A pair of Harry’s sneakers walks by themselves. The hem of the cloak brushes the floor, a faint shimmer if you know to look. Moonlight does not quite catch him.
- **Setting:** A sleeping corridor. Suits of armor. A window of snow. Torches low. Christmas night, the castle hollow.
- **Who:** Harry invisible except shoes and a slight distortion, wearing the cloak over pajamas and the green sweater, glasses unseen, the cloak’s note in a pocket. No Ron in this panel; he is lookout or asleep. Harry wanted the first walk alone.
- **Faces:** No face. The loneliness and the freedom are in the unaccompanied shoes. A portrait’s eyes follow the shoes, puzzled, looking at a person she cannot see.

#### C13-P2-P4

- **Beat:** He slips past Filch. Filch sniffs and walks on.
- **Image:** Filch and Mrs. Norris stand in a pool of lamp light. The empty-but-not shoes pass behind them, close enough to touch the cat’s tail. The cat’s head turns. Filch sniffs the air, suspicious, and shrugs on down the hall. The shoes are already gone.
- **Setting:** A night corridor, Filch’s lamp, Mrs. Norris’s eye-shine. Christmas stillness.
- **Who:** Filch, shabby coat, lamp, keys, stringy hair, nose lifted. Mrs. Norris, tail twitching, looking at the place Harry was. Harry, only a disturbance of dust and the shoes just leaving frame.
- **Faces:** Filch looks at the dark, suspicious, then dismisses it, sour. Mrs. Norris looks at the cloak’s trail, she knows, eyes thin. Harry is faceless, but the panel feels like held breath.

#### C13-P2-P5

- **Beat:** A door he has never seen.
- **Image:** The shoes stop. A door stands where a wall was, or a door he passed all term and never opened. It is tall, unused, a little silver in the carving. His invisible hand — we see the door handle depress by itself.
- **Setting:** A forgotten bit of castle, dust, a tapestry of a wizard chess game, moonlight, no torches. Far from the common room.
- **Who:** No visible body. The handle moving. The cloak’s hem. The door.
- **Faces:** None. Suspense. The door looks like it has been waiting.

#### C13-P2-P6

- **Beat:** A dark room. Something tall under a sheet.
- **Image:** He steps in and the cloak slips back so we see him again, small, in the green sweater and pajamas, glasses, the cloak over one arm. In the center of a bare room, a tall shape under a dusty white sheet. Moon from a high window. His breath clouds.
- **Setting:** An unused classroom or chamber. Desks pushed aside. Dust. One window. No fire. The sheeted object is the only furniture that matters, man-high and a little wider.
- **Who:** Harry, visible now, green H sweater, gray pajamas, slippers, glasses, scar, the invisibility cloak bundled in his arms. He holds it against his chest like proof his father was real. The sheeted mirror, unknown.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the sheet, curious, cautious, the Christmas laugh far away. He looks young. He takes a step. His face is open, unguarded, which is dangerous in this room.

### Page 3 — The parents

#### C13-P3-P1

- **Beat:** He pulls the sheet. An ornate mirror. Only himself.
- **Image:** The sheet slithers off. An old, tall mirror with a carved frame, words along the top too worn to read easily. In it: Harry, sweater, cloak in his arms, and the empty room. Ordinary. He is almost disappointed.
- **Setting:** The bare chamber, moonlight, dust in the air from the sheet. The mirror dominates.
- **Who:** Harry, green sweater, pajamas, glasses, scar, cloak bundled. His reflection, the same, accurate, alone. No one else in the glass. Yet.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at his reflection, a small frown, the expectation deflating. The reflection looks back, same expression. Lonely and ordinary. He was hoping without admitting it.

#### C13-P3-P2

- **Beat:** Then figures appear behind the reflection.
- **Image:** Over the reflection’s shoulders, the glass clouds and clears into people. They are not in the room. They are only in the mirror. Harry’s real shoulders, in front of the glass, have no one behind them. The panel shows both truths: empty room, crowded glass.
- **Setting:** The mirror and the moonlit room, split by the glass.
- **Who:** Real Harry, alone, sweater, cloak lowered to his side now. In the mirror: Harry, and behind him a woman with long dark-red hair and kind eyes, a man with messy black hair and round glasses, and more faces fading back, a family crowd, smiling, waving.
- **Faces:** Real Harry looks at the glass, frozen. Mirror-Harry looks astonished. The woman looks at him with love. The man looks at him with pride and a sadness that is gentle. They look at Harry, not at each other.

#### C13-P3-P3

- **Beat:** A woman with kind eyes. A man with glasses like his.
- **Image:** Close inside the mirror. Lily’s face, kind, hair the color of Ron’s but darker, a soft smile, her hand lifting. James beside her, glasses like Harry’s, untidy hair, a crooked grin that Harry has never seen on his own face and suddenly recognizes. They are young. They are gone. They are here.
- **Setting:** Only the glass. A warm light that does not match the moon. No room.
- **Who:** Lily, simple robes or a dress, no costume fuss, kind eyes, one hand raised in a wave. James, glasses, messy black hair, a hand on mirror-Harry’s shoulder that the real Harry cannot feel. Mirror-Harry between them, small, green sweater.
- **Faces:** Lily looks at Harry, tender, knowing him. James looks at Harry, grinning, proud, the grin a little broken by how much he wants to be on the other side of the glass. Harry, in the mirror, looks from one to the other, starving.

#### C13-P3-P4

- **Beat:** He turns. The room is empty.
- **Image:** He spins. Moonlight, dust, desks, the sheet on the floor. No one. His hand is out, reaching for a shoulder that is not there. The mirror is behind him, still glowing faintly on his back.
- **Setting:** The bare chamber. Cold. Empty. The contrast is the point.
- **Who:** Harry alone, green sweater, pajamas, glasses, scar, one hand reaching into nothing, the cloak dropped at his feet. No parents. The mirror’s frame at the edge, behind him.
- **Faces:** He looks at the empty air, wrecked, a child’s betrayal by physics. Eyes wet. Mouth open. He looks smaller than in the glass.

#### C13-P3-P5

- **Beat:** He turns back. They wave. His hand on the glass.
- **Image:** He is pressed to the mirror. Both palms flat. His forehead nearly touches it, scar against the cool surface. Inside, his parents press their hands to the same place. They do not quite meet. The glass is a fact.
- **Setting:** The mirror, moonlight, his breath fogging the lower corner and fading.
- **Who:** Harry, sweater, pajamas, hands spread on the glass, glasses, tears he is not wiping. Lily and James in the mirror, hands opposite his, smiling, waving when he meets their eyes. The family crowd soft behind them.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at his mother, then his father, greedy and gentle, crying without sound. Lily looks at him, loving, she cannot come through. James looks at him, the grin softened to something that hurts. They look only at him.

#### C13-P3-P6

- **Beat:** He sits on the floor until the fire dies. There is no fire. He sits until the moon moves.
- **Image:** Time passes in one picture: the moon has traveled in the window. Harry sits on the dusty floor, knees up, still facing the glass, the cloak around his shoulders now. His parents are still there whenever he looks. He looks thin and willing to stay forever.
- **Setting:** The chamber, colder, moon moved, dust, the sheet forgotten. Night deep.
- **Who:** Harry, cloak over the green sweater, pajamas, slippers, glasses, sitting. The mirror family still waving, patient, which is part of the danger. No one comes to get him. Yet.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at them, exhausted, happy, lost. A smile that is not healthy. Lily looks at him, and if a mirror could worry, her eyes would. He does not see the worry. He sees only that she is there.

### Page 4 — The warning

#### C13-P4-P1

- **Beat:** Another night. Ron sees himself with a captain’s badge and a cup. Harry cannot see them.
- **Image:** Ron stands where Harry stood, cloak off, and laughs. In the glass he is older, a Quidditch captain’s badge, a cup in his hands, taller. Harry stands beside the real Ron and sees, in the glass, only Ron’s back and his own parents still there. They do not share the vision.
- **Setting:** The same chamber, another night. The cloak in Harry’s arms. Moon.
- **Who:** Ron, maroon sweater, freckles, red hair, in front of the glass, delighted. Mirror-Ron, older, badge, cup, a crowd cheering that is only his. Harry beside him, green sweater, seeing a different picture: Lily and James, and a Ron who is just Ron.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at his glorious self, a huge grin, ears red, wanting it and joking because he wants it. Harry looks at the glass, confused, he cannot see the cup. He looks at Ron’s real face, realizing the mirror is not a window. It is a wish.

#### C13-P4-P2

- **Beat:** Ron cannot see the Potters. The mirror is different for each of them.
- **Image:** A clean split panel. Left, what Ron sees: himself triumphant. Right, what Harry sees: his family. The same frame, two truths. They look at each other, the joke thinning.
- **Setting:** The mirror room, moonlight, dust.
- **Who:** Both boys, sweaters, pajamas. Two versions of the glass.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at Harry, the grin fading into curiosity, a little guilt that his wish was a trophy. Harry looks at Ron, the privacy of his parents suddenly obvious. They look at each other with more care. Neither mocks what the other wanted.

#### C13-P4-P3

- **Beat:** Dumbledore is suddenly there, as if he stepped out of the dark.
- **Image:** They turn. Dumbledore is in the room, tall hat brushing dust, no cloak-rustle, no door open. He looks at the mirror, not at them first, as if greeting an old acquaintance he does not trust.
- **Setting:** The chamber. He brought no lamp. The moon and the mirror-light are enough. The door is still shut.
- **Who:** Dumbledore, purple robes with stars, half-moon spectacles, long silver beard, pointed hat, hands folded. Harry, cloak in his arms, guilty. Ron, a step behind Harry, startled. The mirror behind them, now showing something we do not fully see for Dumbledore, perhaps only his own eyes, carefully not revealed.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks at the mirror, familiar and sad. Then he looks at Harry, kind, not angry. Harry looks up, caught, the child who stayed too long. Ron looks at Dumbledore, awed into silence.

#### C13-P4-P4

- **Beat:** The mirror shows the deepest desire of the heart, and not the truth.
- **Image:** Dumbledore speaks gently, one hand indicating the glass without touching it. In the glass, faintly, Harry’s parents fade because he is listening to a living person. The lesson is the picture: the wish thins when he looks away.
- **Setting:** The three of them and the mirror. Moon. Quiet.
- **Who:** Dumbledore, tall, beard, spectacles, a hand open toward the frame. Harry looking at him, cloak clutched, green sweater. Ron listening, the captain fantasy not mocked.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks at Harry, tender and firm. Harry looks at him, taking it in, still wanting to look back at the glass. Ron looks at the floor, understanding his cup was a wish too. No one looks triumphant.

#### C13-P4-P5

- **Beat:** People have wasted away in front of it. It will be moved.
- **Image:** Dumbledore’s face, close, the kindness edged with a warning. Behind him, a suggestion of other people, not shown clearly, pale, who sat too long — only empty chairs in the dust, a visual metaphor, not ghosts of strangers. The sheet is in his other hand. He means to cover it.
- **Setting:** The chamber. He is between Harry and the glass.
- **Who:** Dumbledore, purple, the sheet gathered. Harry, small, the cloak, looking past Dumbledore’s arm for one more glimpse. Ron at the door, ready to leave, looking back for Harry.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks at Harry, loving, the warning real. Harry looks at the sliver of mirror, aching, obedient anyway. Ron looks at Harry, a friend’s come-on, gentle.

#### C13-P4-P6

- **Beat:** It does not do to dwell on dreams and forget to live. Harry looks back once.
- **Image:** Page-turn. They are at the door. Dumbledore remains by the mirror. Harry looks back once over the green sweater and his father’s cloak. The mirror shows his parents one last time, small in the distance, waving. He does not go back. His face is grief and a choice.
- **Setting:** The doorway. Moon. The corridor ahead, which is the castle, which is living. Snow light. Ron already in the hall.
- **Who:** Harry in the doorway, cloak over his arm, green Christmas sweater, glasses, scar, one hand on the frame. Ron in the corridor, waiting, not rushing him. Dumbledore inside, tall, watching Harry choose the corridor. The mirror family tiny, waving.
- **Faces:** Harry looks back, eyes wet, a goodbye. Then he looks at Ron, choosing the friend who is actually here. Dumbledore looks at Harry, proud and sad. Ron looks at Harry, patient, there. The parents look at their son, and the door closes.
---

## Chapter 14 — The dragon

An illegal egg, a bite, a spy, a midnight handoff, and detention.

### Page 1 — The egg

#### C14-P1-P1

- **Beat:** Hagrid’s hut. He is grinning too widely, hiding something.
- **Image:** The door opens on Hagrid filling it, smile too big for an ordinary visit. One hand is behind his back. Fang’s tail thumps. The boys and Hermione are on the step with a teapot excuse, and they can already tell.
- **Setting:** The hut at dusk. The forest edge dark. A warm interior, fire, the crossbow, herbs. Spring-ish light after Christmas, the grounds muddy and green again.
- **Who:** Hagrid, moleskin coat half off, huge knit vest, beard, the hidden hand. Harry, school robes, glasses, scarf loose. Ron, maroon sweater. Hermione, books, already suspicious. Fang, black boarhound, delighted, drooling.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at them, bursting, trying to look casual and failing. Harry looks at the hidden hand, a smile starting. Hermione looks at Hagrid’s eyes, wary. Ron looks at Fang, then at Hagrid, curious. Fang looks at everyone, pure dog.

#### C14-P1-P2

- **Beat:** A black egg in the fire. He says he won it at cards.
- **Image:** The fire is banked high. In the coals sits a large black egg, leathery, bigger than a pumpkin, rocking slightly from the heat. Hagrid’s hands are spread over it like a proud parent. A deck of cards is on the table, a losing hand still laid out.
- **Setting:** The hearth. Sparks. The hut crowded with the children now inside. Night settling outside the window.
- **Who:** Hagrid kneeling, vest, beard almost in the fire, coat sleeves rolled. The egg, black, scaled, hot. Harry, Ron, and Hermione crowded behind him, faces lit orange. Fang kept back by a chair, whimpering.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at the egg, besotted. Harry looks at the egg, uneasy wonder. Ron looks at it, a grin he knows he should not have. Hermione looks at the cards, then the egg, the legal problem already on her face.

#### C14-P1-P3

- **Beat:** Hermione says dragon breeding is illegal. Hagrid says it is only an egg.
- **Image:** Hermione with both palms up, the rule offered plainly. Hagrid lifts the egg a little, as if its being unhatched is a defense. Ash on his beard. She is not persuaded. He is not listening.
- **Setting:** The hearth, tight. The egg between them like a third person.
- **Who:** Hermione, robes, hair, no book needed, she knows this one. Hagrid, huge, the egg in a folded blanket now, tender. Harry and Ron in the middle, literally, looking from one to the other.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at Hagrid, firm, worried for him more than for the rule. Hagrid looks at the egg, not at her, defensive and dreamy. Harry looks at Hermione, agreeing, and at Hagrid, not wanting to crush him. Ron looks at the egg, the grin losing to sense.

#### C14-P1-P4

- **Beat:** The egg rocks. A crack.
- **Image:** A crack runs. A chip falls into the coals. All four of them lean in so fast their heads nearly knock. The sound is small and enormous.
- **Setting:** The fire, night, the hut gone quiet except the crack. Fang’s ears up.
- **Who:** The egg, a bright line in the black shell. Hagrid’s huge fingers hovering, not touching, afraid to help wrong. Three children’s faces in a row, orange-lit: glasses, freckles, bushy hair.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at the crack, breathless, joy. Hermione looks at it, dread and awe fighting. Harry looks at it, the wonder winning for one second. Ron looks at it, mouth open.

#### C14-P1-P5

- **Beat:** A wet, ugly, beautiful head. Hagrid names him Norbert.
- **Image:** A dragon’s head pushes out, small, slick, spines crumpled, eyes milky then clearing, a thread of smoke from the nostrils. It is ugly. It is beautiful. Hagrid is crying. He whispers the name.
- **Setting:** The hearth. Shell pieces. Firelight making the wet scales shine. The hut a nursery it should not be.
- **Who:** Norbert, a baby Norwegian Ridgeback, black-bronze, tiny wings glued down, claws already too sharp, head the size of a cat and growing in the minute. Hagrid’s hands a cradle. Harry, Ron, Hermione a step back, heat on their faces.
- **Faces:** Norbert looks at Hagrid, imprinting, a squeak. Hagrid looks at him, wrecked with love. Harry looks at the dragon, soft despite himself. Hermione looks at Hagrid, the scolding paused, she sees the love and it complicates her. Ron looks at the claws, delighted and doomed.

#### C14-P1-P6

- **Beat:** Over the flames, the three friends look at each other. This is a disaster.
- **Image:** The dragon and Hagrid are a warm blur. In the sharp foreground, three faces turn toward each other. No one has to say it. The fire pops. A decision will be required and none of them knows it yet.
- **Setting:** The hut, night, forest pressing the window.
- **Who:** Harry, glasses, scar, robe collar open from the heat. Ron, freckles, a scorch mark on his sleeve from leaning in. Hermione, hair frizzed by the fire, mouth a line. Behind, Hagrid rocking a dragon.
- **Faces:** They look at one another, the same thought. Harry looks worried. Hermione looks resolute. Ron looks like he wants to laugh and hide. Friendship as a silent vote: this cannot stay.

### Page 2 — The problem grows

#### C14-P2-P1

- **Beat:** Norbert is larger. He bites Ron. The hand swells green.
- **Image:** Days later. Norbert is the size of a wolfhound, wings working, still in the hut, which he no longer fits. He lunges, playful and terrible, and Ron’s hand comes back with a crescent bite. The skin around it is already an angry green.
- **Setting:** The hut, cramped, scorched beams, Fang hiding under the table, daylight through a cracked window. A bucket of something meaty, spilled.
- **Who:** Norbert, adolescent-fast, black-bronze scales, smoke, teeth on Ron’s sleeve. Ron, maroon sweater, freckles gone pale, the bitten hand held out. Hagrid scolding the dragon like a naughty puppy, vest singed. Harry grabbing Ron’s wrist. Hermione with a bottle she brought, already prepared.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at his hand, sick, betrayed by how cool he thought this was. Norbert looks pleased with himself. Hagrid looks at Ron, guilty, still defending the dragon. Harry looks at the green swelling, alarm. Hermione looks at the bite, clinical and furious.

#### C14-P2-P2

- **Beat:** Hagrid croons and will not hear of sending him away.
- **Image:** Hagrid has Norbert’s snout in both hands, forehead to forehead, singing nonsense. Behind him Hermione is making the case, ignored. The hut is one spark from a fire.
- **Setting:** Interior, daylight, chaos of blankets and bones. The forest outside looks like the right place and the wrong place.
- **Who:** Hagrid, beard, a new burn on his wrist he does not mind, crooning. Norbert, eyes half-shut, spoiled. Hermione, arms folded, hair, the picture of reason. Harry and Ron by the door, Ron’s hand bandaged in a huge clean rag.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at Norbert, besotted, he cannot hear her. Norbert looks at Hagrid, smug. Hermione looks at Hagrid’s back, frustrated, caring. Harry looks at the bandaged hand, the fun over. Ron looks at Norbert, respect replaced by pain.

#### C14-P2-P3

- **Beat:** Through the window, Draco’s face.
- **Image:** A small high window. Draco’s pale face is pressed to it, platinum hair, one wide eye and a delighted, poisonous smile. Inside, they have not seen him. Norbert’s wing blocks Harry’s view of the glass.
- **Setting:** Exterior dusk meeting interior firelight. The hut wall, wet wood, Draco in the mud below the window, robes hem filthy, which he hates.
- **Who:** Draco, expensive robes, a smear of mud he has not noticed, face at the glass. Inside, soft: Hagrid, the dragon, the three friends. Draco is the only sharp focus.
- **Faces:** Draco looks in, triumph, the smile of a boy who has been waiting for them to break a real rule. He looks at Norbert, then at Harry’s back. Greed for the report. No one inside looks at the window.

#### C14-P2-P4

- **Beat:** Draco slips off toward the castle.
- **Image:** He runs, but it is a composed run, robes gathered, across the dark lawn toward the lit castle. He looks back once, the smile still there. The hut window is a small orange square behind him.
- **Setting:** The grounds at nightfall. The forest, the hut, the long slope to the castle. A few window lights. No teachers outside.
- **Who:** Draco alone, platinum hair, muddy hem, a secret in his mouth. No Crabbe and Goyle; this one he wants for himself. The hut’s occupants are unaware shapes in the orange square.
- **Faces:** Draco looks back, pleased, a little breathless. He looks at the castle, already composing the telling. Cold satisfaction.

#### C14-P2-P5

- **Beat:** The hospital wing. Feverish Ron says the dragon has to go tonight.
- **Image:** Ron is in a hospital bed, greenish, sweating, the bitten hand huge in bandages. Madam Pomfrey bustles out of frame. He grabs Harry’s sleeve and says it through his teeth. Hermione is already nodding.
- **Setting:** The hospital wing. White curtains, moonlight, neat beds, a potion smell, quiet. Night.
- **Who:** Ron, pajama top, freckles vivid against the sick green, red hair damp, the bad hand on a pillow. Harry, robes, glasses, leaning in. Hermione, a letter already started in her hand, quill. Madam Pomfrey, a stout witch in a healer’s apron and stiff white, leaving with a bottle, stern and kind, not hearing the plot.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at Harry, fever-bright, serious. Harry looks at the hand, then at Ron, agreeing. Hermione looks at the letter, determined. Pomfrey looks at her bottles, trusting the children less than she should.

#### C14-P2-P6

- **Beat:** Hermione’s letter to Charlie Weasley. An owl leaves the sill.
- **Image:** A window in a quiet corridor. Hermione ties a letter to a school owl’s leg. The owl lifts into the night toward the mountains, where a brother works with dragons. Harry watches it go. The plan has started.
- **Setting:** A castle window, night, stars, the forest below, the owl a small shape becoming smaller. Spring cold.
- **Who:** Hermione, robes, hair, careful fingers on the knot. A brown school owl, patient. Harry beside her, glasses, scarf, hands in pockets. The letter, Charlie’s name visible as handwriting, not a speech.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at the owl, focused, a little proud of the plan. Harry looks at the departing owl, hope and nerves. The owl looks at the night, businesslike.

### Page 3 — The plan

#### C14-P3-P1

- **Beat:** Charlie’s reply. Friends will take the dragon at the Astronomy Tower at midnight.
- **Image:** Morning. The reply is in Hermione’s hands in the common room. A sketch of the tower, a time. Ron is still pale but upright, reading over her shoulder, the bandage smaller. Harry looks at the clock.
- **Setting:** Gryffindor common room, daytime, sun, ordinary noise that makes the secret sharper.
- **Who:** Hermione, the letter, robes. Ron, maroon sweater, freckles, bandaged hand, a weak grin. Harry, glasses, scar, a hand on the back of her chair. Other students out of focus, not reading over them.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at the instructions, relieved and already scheduling the risks. Ron looks at his brother’s handwriting, fond, a bit left out that he cannot go if he is sick — he will try. Harry looks at the word midnight, the old duel-hour, uneasy and ready.

#### C14-P3-P2

- **Beat:** A crate under the invisibility cloak. A tail sticks out.
- **Image:** Night. They creep a corridor. The cloak covers Harry and Hermione and a crate, badly. A spiny tail sticks out the back and smokes a little. Ron is under the front, only his bandaged hand and red hair visible, sweating. It is absurd and dangerous.
- **Setting:** A castle corridor, midnight, moonlight, armor, a long walk to the tower stairs. Torches low.
- **Who:** Harry, pajamas and sweater, glasses, under the cloak, holding the crate handle. Hermione under it too, robes, hair escaping the hem of invisibility. Ron, weaker, bandage, carrying the front. Norbert in the crate, one tail and one annoyed gold eye at a knothole. The cloak, silver-gray, failing to cover the tail.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the tail, grimacing, walk faster. Hermione looks ahead, mapping Filch. Ron looks at his hand, in pain, stubborn. Norbert’s eye looks furious. No one looks like a hero. They look like children moving a problem.

#### C14-P3-P3

- **Beat:** Draco steps from a corner and says he is telling McGonagall.
- **Image:** The cloak does not save them. Draco is already there, arms folded, clean again, a perfect ambush. He looks at the tail, then at Harry’s visible shoe, and his smile is the report he is about to file.
- **Setting:** A corner near the tower stairs. Moon. A statue. No teachers yet, which is Draco’s complaint.
- **Who:** Draco, platinum hair, expensive robes over pajamas or just robes, he dressed for the catching. Harry half-visible, cloak slipping. Hermione furious under the shimmer. Ron pale, bandage, the crate. Norbert’s tail.
- **Faces:** Draco looks at Harry, victorious, the courtyard mockery upgraded to a real crime. Harry looks at him, cold, not begging. Hermione looks at Draco, contempt. Ron looks at the tail, then at Draco, a spark of we-still-have-to-finish-this.

#### C14-P3-P4

- **Beat:** They lose him on a moving staircase.
- **Image:** A staircase swings. Draco lunges and the steps carry him away, robes flaring, shout swallowed. The crate party stumbles onto the landing they needed. For a second the castle itself takes their side.
- **Setting:** The moving stairwell, night, moonlight through tall windows, several staircases in motion like a machine.
- **Who:** Draco borne away, platinum hair, fury, one hand still reaching. Harry, Hermione, Ron, and the smoking crate on the right landing, cloak askew. Norbert scrabbling inside.
- **Faces:** Draco looks back, the victory cracking into rage. Harry looks at the swinging gap, a breath of thanks, not a smile. Hermione looks up the tower stairs, no time. Ron looks at his bandage, dizzy, still moving.

#### C14-P3-P5

- **Beat:** The tower. Wind. Two riders take the crate.
- **Image:** The Astronomy Tower is open to the sky. Two brooms drop out of the dark, riders in dragon-leather, Charlie’s friends, faces wrapped. They grab the crate handles. The wind is a third character. Harry’s cloak snaps like a flag.
- **Setting:** The top of the tower, battlements, huge sky, stars, a long fall, the forest a black rug below. Midnight.
- **Who:** Two riders, goggles, scarves, sturdy brooms, gloved hands on the crate. Harry, hair wrecked, glasses, the cloak in one fist so it will not fly away. Hermione, robes snapping, helping lift. Ron sitting against the wall, greenish, bandage, watching. Norbert’s eye at the knothole, suddenly small against the sky.
- **Faces:** The riders look at the crate, professional, a little charmed by the baby. Harry looks at them, grateful, wind-tears. Hermione looks at the knots, checking. Ron looks at the sky, a weak proud grin, his brother’s people came.

#### C14-P3-P6

- **Beat:** Norbert’s eye shines through a slat, then he is gone into the sky.
- **Image:** The crate lifts. For one panel the gold eye meets Harry’s. Then the brooms are two specks and a crate against the moon. The tower is suddenly empty and very windy. The problem is gone. The trouble is not.
- **Setting:** The tower, the huge sky, the moon, the tiny departing shape. Battlements. Night.
- **Who:** Norbert’s eye, then distance. Harry, Hermione, and Ron small on the tower, looking up. The cloak wrapped around Harry’s arm. No dragon left.
- **Faces:** Harry looks up, relief and a pang, he liked the ugly head. Hermione looks up, the plan completed, her shoulders dropping. Ron looks up, smiling, then wincing at his hand. The night looks large.

### Page 4 — Caught

#### C14-P4-P1

- **Beat:** On the way down, McGonagall. Draco is with her, smug.
- **Image:** The staircase bottom. McGonagall stands like a closed gate, emerald even at night, a lamp. Draco is at her side, chin up, the informer. The three children stop so fast the cloak almost falls.
- **Setting:** A tower stair landing. Stone. Her lamp. Portraits pretending to sleep and peeking.
- **Who:** McGonagall, bun, spectacles, emerald dressing gown or severe robes, lamp, fury held at a precise temperature. Draco, platinum hair, clean, a smirk. Harry, cloak over his arm, pajamas, green sweater, glasses. Hermione, robes thrown on, hair wild. Ron, bandage, barely upright.
- **Faces:** McGonagall looks at them, disappointed more than loud. Draco looks at Harry, smug, waiting for the explosion. Harry looks at McGonagall, the excuse dying. Hermione looks at Draco, cold. Ron looks at the floor, sick and caught.

#### C14-P4-P2

- **Beat:** Filch holds the cloak Harry dropped.
- **Image:** Filch steps from the side passage, and over his arm is the invisibility cloak, shimmering, betrayed. Harry’s hand goes to the empty air where it should be. He dropped it on the stairs. Mrs. Norris sits on Filch’s boots, satisfied.
- **Setting:** The landing. Lamp light on the silver cloth, which looks like water.
- **Who:** Filch, shabby coat, keys, a horrible pleasure, the cloak displayed. Mrs. Norris, eyes gleaming. Harry, reaching, then stopping, pajamas, the loss on his face. McGonagall seeing the cloak and understanding more than Filch does. Draco looking at it, curious, not the point for him.
- **Faces:** Filch looks at the children, triumphant, sour joy. Harry looks at the cloak, stricken, it was his father’s. McGonagall looks at the cloak, a flicker of recognition and sadness under the discipline. Mrs. Norris looks at Harry, unblinking.

#### C14-P4-P3

- **Beat:** Detention. All four of them. Draco was out of bed too.
- **Image:** McGonagall’s verdict lands on all of them, and her eyes include Draco. His smirk freezes. Four children, one punishment. She is fair, which ruins his night.
- **Setting:** The landing. Her lamp. The castle night.
- **Who:** McGonagall, finger not needed, voice enough. Draco, the smirk half-dead. Harry, Hermione, Ron, and Draco in a line of the condemned. Filch delighted in the background with the cloak.
- **Faces:** McGonagall looks at Draco last, cool, you were out too. Draco looks at her, the triumph collapsing into offense. Harry looks at Draco, not gloating, just seeing it. Hermione looks straight ahead, accepting. Ron looks like he might faint, a tiny grim smile.

#### C14-P4-P4

- **Beat:** Draco’s smugness dies.
- **Image:** Close on Draco. The smile is gone. His mouth is open on a protest he swallows because it is McGonagall. A flush on the pale face. He looks younger.
- **Setting:** Tight crop. Her emerald sleeve at the edge. Lamp light.
- **Who:** Draco, platinum hair no longer a weapon, expensive robes, empty hands. No bodyguards.
- **Faces:** He looks at McGonagall, offended, then at Harry, blaming him anyway. The smugness is dead. What replaces it is smaller and meaner: humiliation.

#### C14-P4-P5

- **Beat:** Points pour off Gryffindor. The common room will hate them.
- **Image:** Later. The common-room hourglasses, or the feeling of them: red jewels dropping. They step through the portrait hole into a room that goes quiet. Students look up from the fire. The welcome of the first night is not here. Hermione’s chin is up. Ron looks ill. Harry looks at the floor.
- **Setting:** Gryffindor common room, late, fire, a crowd that was celebrating nothing and now has a reason to be angry. Red hangings. The Fat Lady’s portrait swinging shut, she heard.
- **Who:** Harry, Hermione, Ron in the entrance, detention written on their posture. Neville by the fire, sad for them, toad in his lap. Fred and George, unusually unsmiling. Other students, folded arms. Hermione’s hair still wild from the tower.
- **Faces:** The room looks at them, disappointed, some angry. Hermione looks back, she will not cry here. Ron looks at his brothers, ashamed. Fred looks at Ron, not cruel, just tired of losing points. Harry looks at no one, the cloak gone, the dragon gone, the cost staying. Neville looks at Harry, sympathetic, which helps and hurts.

#### C14-P4-P6

- **Beat:** Harry lies awake. The cloak is gone. He feels bare.
- **Image:** Page-turn. The dormitory. Ron asleep, bandaged hand on the blanket, breathing uneven. Harry on his back, eyes open, one hand on the empty space at the foot of the bed where he used to fold the cloak. Moonlight. He feels seen by the whole castle and protected by nothing.
- **Setting:** The boys’ dormitory, four-posters, moonlight, Neville asleep, Trevor a lump. Quiet after the common room’s stares.
- **Who:** Harry, gray pajamas, no sweater, glasses off on the nightstand so his face is more vulnerable, scar clear, messy hair on the pillow. Ron asleep in the next bed, bandage, red hair. Hedwig at the window, awake, gold eyes.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the ceiling, hollow, guilty, missing his father in the form of a cloth. Hedwig looks at him, steady. Ron’s face is slack with fever-sleep, not available. The panel is bare on purpose.

## Chapter 15 — Detention in the Forbidden Forest

Lanterns, a dead unicorn, a hood, a centaur, and the reason.

### Page 1 — Into the trees

#### C15-P1-P1

- **Beat:** Hagrid at the forest edge. Lanterns. Fang.
- **Image:** Night. The forest is a wall of black trees. Hagrid waits with a lantern and Fang on a rope. Four students arrive in cloaks, not talking to each other. Detention has a meeting point and it is here.
- **Setting:** The edge of the Forbidden Forest. A path. The castle lights far behind, small. Mist at the roots. Owls.
- **Who:** Hagrid, moleskin coat, crossbow on his back, lantern, beard, trying to look cheerful for a grim job. Fang, huge boarhound, nervous, which is a bad sign. Harry, cloak (school cloak, not the invisible one), glasses, scar. Hermione, practical, wand pocketed. Neville, night cloak, toad left behind, pale. Draco, expensive cloak, arms folded, above this.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at them, apologetic and serious. Fang looks at the trees, whining. Harry looks at the dark, alert. Hermione looks at Hagrid, trusting him and not the forest. Neville looks at Fang, then the trees, scared. Draco looks at the mud, disgusted.

#### C15-P1-P2

- **Beat:** Something in the trees has been hurting unicorns.
- **Image:** Hagrid crouches and shows them a silver splash on a root, unicorn blood, bright in the lantern, too beautiful for the violence it means. His voice is low. The forest seems to lean in.
- **Setting:** The first trees. Lantern light. A silver stain. Darkness beyond the light’s edge.
- **Who:** Hagrid, lantern close to the ground, big hand indicating, not touching the silver. The four students bent or standing: Harry closest, Draco furthest, Hermione studying it, Neville behind Harry. Fang sniffing and then backing up.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at the silver, angry and sad, this is why they are here. Harry looks at it, a chill. Hermione looks, grave. Neville looks away. Draco looks, a crack in the disgust, he knows what unicorn blood means and he does not like it. Fang looks at the dark, afraid.

#### C15-P1-P3

- **Beat:** Draco sneers. Hagrid tells him to shut it, gently and completely.
- **Image:** Draco opens his mouth for a slight. Hagrid does not shout. He looks down, mild, and the slight dies unfinished. The size difference is the whole panel. Draco’s mouth closes.
- **Setting:** The path, lantern, trees.
- **Who:** Hagrid, huge, coat, lantern, crossbow, calm. Draco, platinum hair, fine cloak, smaller than he likes to feel. The others watching, Harry a witness, not a smirker.
- **Faces:** Draco looks up, the sneer collapsing. Hagrid looks at him, gentle, immovable, a little disappointed. Harry looks at Hagrid, a flicker of gratitude. Hermione looks at the path, ready to move. Neville looks at his own feet.

#### C15-P1-P4

- **Beat:** The trees close. Light shrinks to lantern circles.
- **Image:** A wide panel of darkness with four small circles of lantern light moving between trunks. Branches knit overhead. The castle is gone. Sound is owls and Fang’s panting.
- **Setting:** Deep Forbidden Forest. Massive trees, roots, fog, no sky. Night.
- **Who:** Tiny in their own lights: Hagrid and Fang, Harry, Hermione with Neville, Draco forced close to a lantern he did not want to share. Cloaks, wands unused.
- **Faces:** Far away. What reads is posture: Hagrid sure-footed, Fang cowering, Neville bunched, Draco stiff, Harry turning his head, listening, Hermione holding her lantern steady.

#### C15-P1-P5

- **Beat:** They split. Harry with Draco. Hermione with Neville.
- **Image:** A fork in the path. Hagrid points, sorry about the pairs. Hermione and Neville go left with Fang. Harry and Draco go right, the two boys who hate each other, one lantern between them. Hagrid takes the middle, crossbow ready.
- **Setting:** A split path, roots like stairs, fog, two darknesses.
- **Who:** Hagrid directing, lantern, crossbow. Hermione, reluctant, a hand near Neville’s sleeve. Neville, grateful it is her and not Draco. Fang pressed to Hermione’s leg. Harry taking the right lantern. Draco, arms still folded, refusing to stand near Harry and standing near him anyway.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at Harry, a silent be careful. Harry looks at the right-hand dark, accepting. Draco looks at Harry, contempt covering nerves. Hermione looks back at Harry, worried. Neville looks at Hermione, trusting her. Fang looks at Hagrid, do not leave.

#### C15-P1-P6

- **Beat:** Harry dislikes the pairing. Draco dislikes it more.
- **Image:** The two of them on a narrow path, one lantern, shoulders not touching. The light makes their faces opposite masks. An owl. A silver fleck on a leaf, which both of them see and neither mentions yet.
- **Setting:** The right-hand path. Close trees. Fog. The lantern a small world.
- **Who:** Harry, school cloak, glasses, scar, lantern in his hand because Draco would not take it. Draco, platinum hair limp from the damp, fine cloak muddied at the hem, empty hands, wand pocketed, too proud to draw it.
- **Faces:** Harry looks ahead, jaw tight, the dislike quiet. Draco looks at Harry, then at the dark, the dislike louder, and under it a fear he will not show. They do not look at each other for long. The forest looks back.

### Page 2 — The blood

#### C15-P2-P1

- **Beat:** A silver shape. Harry thinks it is moonlight.
- **Image:** Ahead, between trunks, a pale glow on the ground. Harry lifts the lantern, hoping it is the moon through a gap. There is no gap. The light is coming from the thing itself.
- **Setting:** A clearing about to open. Fog. Black trees. The glow silver-white, liquid-looking.
- **Who:** Harry in front, lantern, glasses catching the silver. Draco a step behind, the sneer gone because he has not remembered to wear it. Their cloaks damp.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the glow, hopeful for a second, then uncertain. Draco looks at it, and the uncertainty hits him faster. He looks at Harry, not for friendship, for confirmation that this is bad.

#### C15-P2-P2

- **Beat:** A dead unicorn. The moonlight is its blood.
- **Image:** The clearing. A unicorn lies on its side, white coat, slender, horn broken or dim, beautiful and wrong. Silver blood pools and crawls, luminous, staining the moss. It is not gory in a cheap way. It is sacred and ruined.
- **Setting:** A small clearing. Fog. The silver pool is the only light besides the lantern, and it is brighter. Flowers bent. No birds.
- **Who:** The unicorn, dead, slender legs folded wrong, mane in the blood. Harry and Draco at the edge, small, cloaks, the lantern useless against that shine. No attacker yet.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the unicorn, grief, a hand half-up as if he could still help. His mouth is open. Draco looks at it, the pure-blood boy who knows the weight of this, genuinely shaken. The sneer is gone. Fear is clean on him.

#### C15-P2-P3

- **Beat:** Draco makes a small sound and steps back.
- **Image:** Draco’s foot slips in moss. He steps back into a tree, one hand up, the sound small and childlike. Harry does not mock him. Harry is not looking at him. Harry is looking at the far side of the clearing, where the pool is disturbed.
- **Setting:** The clearing’s edge. Silver light. A dark gap in the trees opposite.
- **Who:** Draco, platinum hair, cloak, back to a trunk, pale. Harry, lantern lowered, glasses, scar beginning to prick, body still. The unicorn in the foreground, still.
- **Faces:** Draco looks at the blood, then at the dark gap, a whimper he hates. Harry looks at the gap, focused, the scar a warning. He looks calm and he is not.

#### C15-P2-P4

- **Beat:** A hooded figure crouched over the body, drinking.
- **Image:** The figure is there, as if it was always in the dark and the dark sat up. A cloak with a hood, no face, bent to the unicorn’s throat, silver blood at the hood’s edge, drinking. It is the most wrong thing in a forest of wrong things.
- **Setting:** The clearing. The silver pool. The hooded shape breaking it.
- **Who:** The hooded figure, tattered dark cloak, hunched, hands like pale spiders, face hidden, mouth at the wound. The unicorn. Harry and Draco small at the edge, Harry’s lantern shaking a fraction.
- **Faces:** No face in the hood, only a blackness and a glint. Harry looks at it, horror, scar blazing under his hair. Draco looks at it, the last of his posture gone. The figure does not look at them yet. It is feeding.

#### C15-P2-P5

- **Beat:** Harry’s scar tears. He drops to a knee.
- **Image:** Pain is a white crack across the panel. Harry is down on one knee, hand clamped to his forehead, lantern fallen, its light rolling. The hood lifts because the pain called it, or because he made a sound.
- **Setting:** Moss, silver blood nearby, the lantern on its side. The clearing tilting.
- **Who:** Harry, cloak, glasses askew, both knees wanting to go, one hand on the scar, the other in the moss. Draco standing but barely, behind him. The hooded figure straightening.
- **Faces:** Harry’s eyes are squeezed, pain, he looks at the ground not the figure. Draco looks at Harry, then at the hood, and the calculation is simple: run. The hood looks toward Harry. The glint in it finds him.

#### C15-P2-P6

- **Beat:** The hood lifts. No readable face. Draco runs.
- **Image:** The hood comes up. What is under it is not a face the panel will explain: a blur, a paleness, a wrongness, Voldemort not restored. Draco breaks. He runs back down the path, cloak flying, platinum hair, abandoning Harry without a speech. Harry is still on one knee.
- **Setting:** The clearing. The path back. Silver light. The figure between Harry and the unicorn.
- **Who:** The hooded figure, tall now, cloak, unreadable head, a step toward Harry. Draco running, back to us, fast, cowardice and survival the same motion. Harry kneeling, glasses, scar, lantern out of reach.
- **Faces:** Draco does not look back. His back is the expression: panic. Harry looks up at the hood, unable to stand, seeing nothing he can name. The hood looks at Harry, and the scar answers.

### Page 3 — Firenze

#### C15-P3-P1

- **Beat:** The figure glides toward Harry. He cannot stand.
- **Image:** The hooded thing does not walk so much as arrive closer. Harry’s legs will not obey. Silver blood on the figure’s mouth, or where a mouth would be. Harry’s hand finds his wand and it feels like a twig.
- **Setting:** The clearing, fog torn by movement. The unicorn behind the figure like an accusation.
- **Who:** The hooded figure, cloak dragging through the silver pool, pale hands out. Harry on the ground, school cloak, wand in a shaking hand, glasses, scar white-hot. No Draco. No lantern steady.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the hands, terror held down by pain. He looks very small. The hood looks at him with a hunger that is not only for blood. No readable features, so the hunger is in the lean of the body.

#### C15-P3-P2

- **Beat:** Hooves. A centaur blocks the hood.
- **Image:** Hooves crash into the clearing. A centaur rears between Harry and the hood, a living wall: human torso, horse body, bow up, hair flying. The hood recoils from him, not from Harry’s wand.
- **Setting:** The clearing, suddenly violent with motion. Moss torn. Silver splashed.
- **Who:** Firenze, palomino body, cream-gold coat, bare chest, long blond hair, a bow, stern young face, hooves between Harry and death. The hooded figure shrinking back. Harry on the ground behind the centaur’s legs, looking up at a savior he did not expect.
- **Faces:** Firenze looks at the hood, cold, ancient anger, no fear. The hood looks at him, reluctant, hungry, retreating. Harry looks at Firenze’s back, shock and relief crashing together.

#### C15-P3-P3

- **Beat:** Firenze tells Harry to climb on. Harry does.
- **Image:** Firenze kneels, impossible grace, and Harry scrambles onto the horse back, clumsy, fists in the pale mane. The hood is at the tree line, not gone, watching.
- **Setting:** The clearing’s edge. The unicorn still. The path away.
- **Who:** Firenze, kneeling, blond hair, a hand back to help, bow in the other. Harry, cloak tangled, glasses, swinging a leg up, small on the broad back. The hooded figure, paused between trees.
- **Faces:** Firenze looks toward the path, urgent, calm voice, he does not look afraid but he will not linger. Harry looks at the mane, then at the hood, he does not want to turn his back and he does. The hood looks at Harry being taken, a promise more than a glare.

#### C15-P3-P4

- **Beat:** They move. The hood does not follow past the centaur.
- **Image:** The forest streaks. Firenze gallops. Harry clings. Behind, at the edge of the silver clearing, the hood stands still, unwilling to cross some line the centaur carries with him. Then the trees take the view.
- **Setting:** The forest at speed. Trunks, fog, mane, the clearing shrinking.
- **Who:** Firenze running, powerful, hair and Harry’s cloak streaming together. Harry bent low, arms around him as much as pride allows, glasses pressed to his face. The hood small and still behind.
- **Faces:** Harry looks back once, the scar easing with distance, fear still high. Firenze looks ahead, profile stern, he does not look back. The hood looks after them, patient. It can wait. It has been waiting.

#### C15-P3-P5

- **Beat:** Firenze says he has never seen a unicorn die like that.
- **Image:** They have stopped at a stream. Firenze’s head is bowed, the sternness cracked by grief. Harry slides down, legs unsteady. The forest is quiet in a mourning way.
- **Setting:** A moonlit stream, safer trees, water over stones, no silver blood. The castle is still far.
- **Who:** Firenze, palomino, blond hair shadowing his face, bow lowered. Harry, cloak torn at the hem by branches, glasses, scar faded from white-hot to sore, standing small beside a centaur.
- **Faces:** Firenze looks at the water, grieved, angry. Harry looks at him, the question waiting in his mouth. He looks young and polite and badly shaken. He does not rush the centaur’s grief.

#### C15-P3-P6

- **Beat:** Harry, still holding the scar, asks what that thing was.
- **Image:** His fingers are on the scar again, a habit now. He looks up the long height of Firenze. The question is the panel. An owl. The stream.
- **Setting:** The stream bank. Moon. A centaur and a boy.
- **Who:** Harry, school cloak, pajamas or clothes under it from detention, glasses, scar under his fingers. Firenze, bare chest, horse body, bow, looking down.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Firenze, needing the name, afraid of it. Firenze looks at Harry, measuring whether a child should hear this. His face is kind and severe. He decides to tell.

### Page 4 — The curse

#### C15-P4-P1

- **Beat:** Unicorn blood will keep you alive an inch from death.
- **Image:** Firenze speaks. In the air between them, Harry imagines it without a full vision panel: the hood at the unicorn’s throat, the silver keeping a ruined thing in the world. Harry’s hand stays on his scar.
- **Setting:** The stream. Moon on the water. Quiet.
- **Who:** Firenze, gesturing once, restrained. Harry listening, cloak wrapped tight, glasses, small.
- **Faces:** Firenze looks at Harry, unwilling teacher. Harry looks at him, the horror settling into sense. He looks ill. He looks angry on the unicorn’s behalf.

#### C15-P4-P2

- **Beat:** The price is a half-life, cursed from the moment you drink.
- **Image:** Firenze’s face, close, the sentence landing. Harry takes a step back without meaning to, not from Firenze, from the idea. The stream goes on, clean, a contrast with cursed blood.
- **Setting:** Moonlight, water, trees.
- **Who:** Firenze’s face, blond hair, solemn eyes. Harry, a step off, cloak, the scar.
- **Faces:** Firenze looks at Harry, pity and warning. Harry looks at the ground, imagining a half-life, then back up, braver. He asks with his eyes: who would pay that.

#### C15-P4-P3

- **Beat:** Harry says the name he is afraid of. Firenze does not correct him.
- **Image:** Harry says it. The trees do not like it. Firenze’s ears — human — do not flinch into denial. A leaf falls. The name is accepted. That is the answer.
- **Setting:** The stream bank. Stillness after a dangerous word.
- **Who:** Harry, mouth just closed on the name, glasses, cloak, fists. Firenze, towering, a short nod.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Firenze, hoping to be told he is wrong. Firenze looks at him, grave, he will not give that comfort. Harry’s face changes: the hope closes. Knowledge arrives.

#### C15-P4-P4

- **Beat:** Voldemort is in these trees because of the Stone.
- **Image:** Firenze turns his head toward the castle, far through the trees, a few lights. The line from the forest to the school is the story. Harry follows the look. The Stone is in there. The thing in the hood wants it.
- **Setting:** A gap in the trees. Distant castle windows. The forest vast around a small boy and a centaur.
- **Who:** Firenze, profile, blond hair, bow, looking toward Hogwarts. Harry beside the palomino shoulder, looking the same way, suddenly seeing the castle as a vault.
- **Faces:** Firenze looks at the castle, troubled for the world, not only the boy. Harry looks at the lights, the dog, the package, the book, all connecting. His face is pale and certain.

#### C15-P4-P5

- **Beat:** The Stone would give Voldemort a body without the curse.
- **Image:** Harry’s hands open and close. He understands the theft. Not gold. A body. A return. The hood would not need unicorn blood if it had the Stone. He looks at his own hands, the boy who already survived that return once.
- **Setting:** The stream. Moon. Close on him, Firenze a tall presence at the edge.
- **Who:** Harry, glasses, scar, cloak, empty hands, the understanding. Firenze watching him, not interrupting.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at his hands, then at the scar, fear and a fierce no. Firenze looks at him, respect, a child who understood quickly. Sadness, because understanding is not safety.

#### C15-P4-P6

- **Beat:** Castle lights ahead. He looks at his friends, sure now that Snape serves the hood.
- **Image:** Page-turn. They have reached the forest edge. Hermione and Neville and Hagrid run up, lanterns bouncing. Draco is already there, pale, not meeting eyes. Harry looks at his friends and does not say the centaur’s whole speech. His face says he has a villain. He is wrong about the face, and he is sure.
- **Setting:** The forest edge, the castle blazing with ordinary windows, safety looking close and false. Night. Mist.
- **Who:** Harry, cloak torn, glasses, scar, Firenze already fading back into the trees, a nod goodbye. Hermione reaching him, hair, lantern, fear for him. Neville behind her, crying with relief. Hagrid, huge, crossbow, devastated that Harry was alone. Draco apart, cloak muddy, looking at the ground. Fang licking Harry’s hand.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Hermione, alive, and past her toward the castle, the theory locked: Snape. Hermione looks at Harry, scanning him for wounds. Hagrid looks at the trees, guilty. Draco looks at no one, shame. Firenze looks back once, unreadable, and the dark takes him.

---

## Chapter 16 — They decide to protect the Stone

Exams, Hagrid’s slip, a warning refused, and a flute in a dark corridor.

### Page 1 — Exams and a slip

#### C16-P1-P1

- **Beat:** The exam hall. Harry writes, and thinks about the forest.
- **Image:** Rows of desks. Sun. Harry’s parchment has a paragraph and then a blank, because his quill has stopped. In his head the hood is still drinking. A bead of ink drops.
- **Setting:** The Great Hall turned into an exam room. Morning. Invigilators. Floating clocks. Summer at the windows, green and unfair.
- **Who:** Harry, robes, tie, glasses, scar, quill stalled. Hermione two desks away, already on the second page, hair, perfect handwriting. Ron biting his quill. McGonagall prowling, emerald, spectacles.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the parchment, not seeing the question. He looks haunted and ordinary. Hermione looks at her work, focused, a glance at him that is worry. McGonagall looks at the room, fair and watchful.

#### C16-P1-P2

- **Beat:** Hermione finishes early and checks the page twice.
- **Image:** She sets her quill down, then picks it up, then sets it down. Checking is how she loves people and essays. She looks across at Harry’s blank lines and does not pass him an answer. Her foot taps.
- **Setting:** The exam hall. A clock. Summer light.
- **Who:** Hermione, finished parchment, neat, robes, a smudge she resents on one finger. Harry still stalled. Ron still biting, a doodle in the margin he will regret.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at her last line, critical, then at Harry, soft. She looks back at her page, discipline. Harry does not look up. His face is in the forest.

#### C16-P1-P3

- **Beat:** Ron stares at a question and accidentally draws a dragon.
- **Image:** The corner of Ron’s parchment. A dragon, quite good, smoking. The actual answer is one line and doubtful. He stares at the question as if it bit him. The bandage is gone; a pale scar of a bite remains on his hand.
- **Setting:** His desk. Sun. McGonagall’s shadow about to pass.
- **Who:** Ron, red hair, freckles, ink on his nose, the dragon doodle, quill. His bitten hand, healed, resting on the paper.
- **Faces:** He looks at the question, despair, then at the dragon, a private grin, then panic as the shadow nears. He looks like a boy at the end of a long year.

#### C16-P1-P4
