- **Beat:** Later, Hagrid’s hut. He weeps into a handkerchief the size of a sail.
- **Image:** Exams are over. The hut again. Hagrid is at the table, sobbing, a handkerchief like a tablecloth at his face. The children came to celebrate and found a crisis. Tea is poured and ignored.
- **Setting:** The hut, late afternoon, summer, door open, Fang’s head on Harry’s knee. No egg. No dragon. The absence is loud.
- **Who:** Hagrid, vest, beard wet, the huge handkerchief. Harry, Ron, Hermione in lighter clothes under open robes, end of term. Fang, mournful because Hagrid is.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks into the cloth, guilty, wrecked. Harry looks at him, alarm replacing the exam haze. Hermione looks at Hagrid, gentle, ready to ask. Ron looks at the empty hearth, understanding this is about Norbert or worse.

#### C16-P1-P5

- **Beat:** He says he should never have told. The three go still.
- **Image:** The sentence drops. Three teacups stop. Fang’s tail stops. Hagrid looks up, red-eyed, realizing he has said the outside of the secret.
- **Setting:** The table. Afternoon light. Stillness.
- **Who:** Hagrid, handkerchief lowered, vest, miserable. The three, cups, eyes up.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at them, regret. Harry looks at him, the forest and the Stone rising fast. Hermione looks at him, careful, she will not pounce. Ron looks at Harry, here we go. All of them very still.

#### C16-P1-P6

- **Beat:** Harry asks, very carefully, told what.
- **Image:** Harry’s hands are flat on the table, a boy trying not to spook a wounded animal. His voice is the quietest thing in the hut. Hagrid’s mouth works. The handkerchief twists.
- **Setting:** Close across the table. Mugs. Fang’s eye. Summer.
- **Who:** Harry, glasses, scar, open robes, careful hands. Hagrid, huge and reduced, beard, twisting cloth.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Hagrid, kind and relentless. Hagrid looks at the handkerchief, then at Harry, he wants to be stopped and he will not be. Trust and dread.

### Page 2 — The stranger

#### C16-P2-P1

- **Beat:** A man in a pub kept buying the drinks and kept Hagrid talking.
- **Image:** A memory, softer edges. The Hog’s Head or the Leaky Cauldron, a dark corner, a hooded stranger pushing a mug across a wet table. Hagrid, flushed, talking with his hands. Cards or a game nearby. The stranger’s face is in shadow on purpose.
- **Setting:** A grimy wizard pub, memory-colored. Smoke. A hood. Two mugs.
- **Who:** Memory-Hagrid, coat, beard, too many empty mugs, earnest. The stranger, hood up, hands pale, one hand pushing a drink, face hidden. Not clearly turban or Snape. Ambiguous, which is honest to Hagrid’s memory.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at the stranger, lonely and glad of the company, unsuspecting. The stranger looks down, listening too well. We do not get eyes.

#### C16-P2-P2

- **Beat:** The man asked how anyone would get past the three-headed dog.
- **Image:** The stranger’s pale hand taps the table on the question. Hagrid’s face, in the memory, lights up the way it does when he can talk about a creature he loves. He is proud of Fluffy. That pride is the trap.
- **Setting:** The pub table, close, memory.
- **Who:** The stranger’s hands, hood edge. Hagrid leaning in, fond of his dog.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks proud, a little drunk, about to boast. The hood tilts, hungry for the answer. No smile we can trust.

#### C16-P2-P3

- **Beat:** Hagrid swears he never said, then admits he said music puts Fluffy to sleep.
- **Image:** Back in the hut, present. Hagrid’s hands are up in denial, then they fall, and he admits it into the handkerchief. Hermione’s eyes close. The word music sits in the room like a key.
- **Setting:** The hut, afternoon, tea gone cold.
- **Who:** Hagrid, vest, miserable honesty. Hermione, eyes shut, one hand on her brow. Harry very straight. Ron staring.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at the table, ashamed. Hermione looks at nothing, the plan of the thief completing itself behind her eyelids. Harry looks at Hagrid, not angry, afraid. Ron looks at the door, as if the thief might be on the step.

#### C16-P2-P4

- **Beat:** Hermione closes her eyes.
- **Image:** A quiet panel. Just Hermione’s face, eyes closed, summer light, hair. She is not resting. She is seeing the trapdoor and a flute and a stranger who already knows.
- **Setting:** The hut, soft background, Hagrid a blur of beard.
- **Who:** Hermione, robes, a line between her brows, hands clasped on the table.
- **Faces:** Eyes shut. Mouth tight. Fear managed by thinking. When she opens them — we see the lids about to — she will be ready to move.

#### C16-P2-P5

- **Beat:** Hagrid cannot remember the face. A hood, or maybe a turban.
- **Image:** Hagrid squints into his memory, one hand waving at his own head, hood, turban, he cannot land it. The children watch his hand. The ambiguity is a door Harry will shut too fast.
- **Setting:** The hut. Late light. Fang asleep, the only one at peace.
- **Who:** Hagrid, gesturing around his head, beard, frustrated with himself. Harry, Ron, Hermione watching the gesture as if the face might appear in the air.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks into the middle distance, trying, failing, guilty. Hermione looks at his hand, filing both possibilities. Ron looks confused. Harry looks certain already, the turban mention sliding off him. He wants the memory to be Snape.

#### C16-P2-P6

- **Beat:** Harry hears “Snape” in his head. The panel shows Quirrell’s turban, and Harry does not see it.
- **Image:** Split. On Harry’s side, his imagination: Snape in the pub hood, black hair, hook nose, sneering over a mug. On the truth side of the panel, unseen by Harry: Quirrell’s purple turban and nervous hands pushing the drink, the hood down a little. A line between them. Harry is looking at the wrong half.
- **Setting:** The hut’s air, a vision panel, summer dust.
- **Who:** Imagined Snape, black, cruel, false. True Quirrell, turban, pale, sweating, the buyer of drinks. Harry in the foreground, glasses, looking at the Snape he invented.
- **Faces:** Imagined Snape looks villainous, which is how Harry needs him. Quirrell looks frightened and purposeful, a worse combination. Harry looks convinced. His certainty is the danger. Hermione, small at the edge, looks at Harry, not sharing the certainty.

### Page 3 — The warning refused

#### C16-P3-P1

- **Beat:** They run to McGonagall’s office.
- **Image:** Three children run a sunlit corridor, robes flying, end-of-term clutter, other students with trunks staring. Hermione is fastest. Harry’s scarf trails. They look like trouble and they are trying to prevent it.
- **Setting:** Castle corridor, late afternoon, summer, portraits turning. McGonagall’s office door ahead, stern wood.
- **Who:** Hermione, hair streaming, a plan. Harry, glasses, scar, determined. Ron, red hair, freckles, keeping up, the healed hand swinging.
- **Faces:** They look at the door, urgent. Hermione looks resolved. Harry looks angry at the time they have lost. Ron looks like he hopes she is in.

#### C16-P3-P2

- **Beat:** Hermione, unusually messy, says someone is going to steal the Stone.
- **Image:** Inside the office. Hermione stands in front of the desk, hair escaped, the speech tumbling, hands moving. She is never this untidy. That is how McGonagall knows it is serious, and also why the scene feels off-balance.
- **Setting:** McGonagall’s office. Green tartan, a desk, neatly stacked exams, a sunbeam, a sleeping cat that might be a teacup. Order, and three children breaking it.
- **Who:** Hermione, robes askew, hands open, pleading the case. McGonagall behind the desk, emerald, spectacles, exams in hand. Harry and Ron flanking, backup.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at McGonagall, urgent, a child asking an adult to be the adult. McGonagall looks at her, listening, already bracing to refuse. Harry looks at McGonagall, willing her to hear. Ron looks at a suit of armor in the corner, nervous in offices.

#### C16-P3-P3

- **Beat:** McGonagall says it is well protected and they are to stop meddling.
- **Image:** She sets the exams down, aligned, a gesture of the subject being closed. Her hand is up, not unkind, final. The Stone is not their business. The desk between them is a wall.
- **Setting:** The office. Sun. The cat-teacup flicks an ear.
- **Who:** McGonagall, precise, emerald, the raised hand. The three children on the other side, the speech stopped.
- **Faces:** McGonagall looks at them, firm, a teacher protecting children from a fight she thinks is already guarded. Hermione looks at the hand, the plan hitting an adult no. Harry looks at her, frustration rising. Ron looks at Harry, he knew this would happen.

#### C16-P3-P4

- **Beat:** Harry says Snape. Her face closes.
- **Image:** Harry steps beside Hermione and says the name. McGonagall’s warmth shuts. Accusing a colleague crosses a line. Her mouth becomes a line. The sunbeam does not help.
- **Setting:** The office, tighter.
- **Who:** Harry, glasses, scar, the accusation. McGonagall, spectacles flashing, emerald, still. Hermione wishing he had kept the name back. Ron wincing.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at her, earnest, he thinks the name is proof. McGonagall looks at him, offended on Snape’s behalf and worried about Harry’s certainty. Hermione looks at Harry, a small shake of the head, too late. The door of the conversation closes in McGonagall’s eyes.

#### C16-P3-P5

- **Beat:** She says Dumbledore will be told, and sends them out.
- **Image:** She is around the desk, guiding them by presence, not shoves, toward the door. A promise: Dumbledore will hear. It sounds like safety. It is about to be hollow.
- **Setting:** The office door, corridor bright beyond.
- **Who:** McGonagall, a hand indicating the door, stern care. Hermione looking back over her shoulder. Harry, unsatisfied. Ron, relieved and not.
- **Faces:** McGonagall looks at Harry, she means the promise. Harry looks back, he does not feel safer. Hermione looks at the corridor, thinking ahead. Ron looks at his shoes.

#### C16-P3-P6

- **Beat:** A portrait tells them Dumbledore has already left the school.
- **Image:** In the corridor, a portrait of an old witch leans out of her frame, delighted to gossip. Dumbledore was called away. Urgent. A carriage. Gone. The children’s faces fall in a row. The promise is empty.
- **Setting:** The corridor outside the office. Afternoon going gold. The portrait’s painted room behind her, fussy and calm, unlike the news.
- **Who:** The portrait witch, lace cap, eager. Hermione, Harry, Ron, stopped in the sun.
- **Faces:** The portrait looks thrilled with her news. Hermione looks at her, the floor dropping. Harry looks down the corridor, as if he could see the carriage already gone. Ron looks at Harry, the mad plan arriving in his eyes whether he wants it or not. Fear, all three, and no adult left in the building who will hear them.

### Page 4 — The choice

#### C16-P4-P1

- **Beat:** The common room. Ron whispers that this is mad.
- **Image:** They have a corner to themselves. Trunks are half-packed around the room, end of term, everyone else happy. Ron leans in, whispering, both hands open: this is mad. He is not refusing. He is naming it.
- **Setting:** Gryffindor common room, evening, gold light, packed trunks, laughter that is not theirs. A last night feeling.
- **Who:** Ron, maroon sweater, freckles, whisper. Harry, scarf off, glasses, listening. Hermione, a book closed, she is past books.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at them, scared, honest, still here. Harry looks at him, grateful he said it. Hermione looks at the happy students, then at Ron, she agrees it is mad. None of them look ready to go back to packing.

#### C16-P4-P2

- **Beat:** The other protections fail if the thief already has the first one.
- **Image:** Hermione’s finger taps the table in a short rhythm, like counting locks. One tap is broken: music. The rest are a guess. Her face is the logic, clean and frightened.
- **Setting:** The corner. Evening. A trunk gaping nearby, clothes, a reminder of the train home they might still catch if they stop.
- **Who:** Hermione, hair, robes, tapping finger. Harry and Ron watching the finger as if it were a lockpick.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at her own hand, certain of the logic, hating it. Harry looks at her, the decision settling. Ron looks at the packed trunk, then back, he is not going to the train.

#### C16-P4-P3

- **Beat:** If Voldemort returns, it will be because they did nothing.
- **Image:** Harry says it quietly, no speechifying. His hand is on the scar. The common-room noise continues behind him, obscene in its ordinariness. The sentence makes Ron go still and Hermione close her book that was already closed.
- **Setting:** The corner. Fire. Other children’s laughter, soft.
- **Who:** Harry, glasses, scar under his fingers, the forest still on him. Ron and Hermione, the audience that matters.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at them, not dramatic, just clear. Young and responsible in a way he should not have to be. Hermione looks at him, the last objection folding. Ron looks at him, a swallow, then a nod.

#### C16-P4-P4

- **Beat:** They look at each other. The argument ends.
- **Image:** Three faces, close, no more talking. A nod each. The portrait hole is behind them. End of term trunks. They are going tonight.
- **Setting:** The common room, the fire, the decision.
- **Who:** Harry, Ron, Hermione. No wands out yet. Just the look.
- **Faces:** They look at one another, fear shared so it is lighter. Harry looks steady. Hermione looks brave and tidy again, a plan forming. Ron looks terrified and loyal, a crooked almost-smile. Friends.

#### C16-P4-P5

- **Beat:** Night. Three small figures. Harry raises a flute Hagrid gave him.
- **Image:** The third-floor corridor, the one from the feast warning. Moon. They stand before the door. Harry has a small wooden flute, a Hagrid gift from some earlier kindness, raised to his mouth. Hermione has her wand. Ron has his. They look tiny against the door.
- **Setting:** The forbidden corridor, night, high windows, moonlight, the door to Fluffy. Summer night, warm stone, cold errand.
- **Who:** Harry, school robes over clothes, glasses, scar, the flute at his lips, not playing yet. Hermione, wand out, hair tied back for work. Ron, wand, freckles, red hair, a look at the flute like he hopes Hagrid was right. No cloak of invisibility. They are just three children.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the door, the flute a strange gentle weapon. Hermione looks at the lock, ready. Ron looks at Harry, trust. All of them look afraid and finished with waiting.

#### C16-P4-P6

- **Beat:** A growl answers from behind the door.
- **Image:** Page-turn. The flute is just about to sound. From behind the door, a growl, low, triple, the door shivering. Dust sifts down. Their eyes go wide. The year of whispers has a throat.
- **Setting:** The door, close. Moonlight. The corridor empty of adults. The growl visible as a tremor in the wood.
- **Who:** The three, small, Harry’s fingers on the flute, Hermione’s wand light starting, Ron’s shoulders up. The door. Fluffy unseen, announced.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the door, a breath in, he will play. Hermione looks at the hinges, calculating. Ron looks at Harry, a silent now. The door looks back, trembling. They go in on the turn of the page.
---

## Chapter 17 — The underground challenges

A sleeping dog, a strangling plant, a flock of keys, a chess sacrifice, and a logic problem with only one swallow forward.

### Page 1 — Fluffy and the snare

#### C17-P1-P1

- **Beat:** Harry plays, badly. The heads droop one at a time.
- **Image:** The door is open. Fluffy fills the room again, three heads snarling — then the flute’s thin tune wobbles out and the left head blinks, heavy. The middle head resists. The right head yawns, enormous teeth and a pink tongue. Harry’s fingers stumble and he keeps going.
- **Setting:** The third-floor room, night, moonlight from a high slit, stone, the trapdoor under the paws. The flute is a small sweet sound in a huge smell of dog.
- **Who:** Fluffy, three heads, shaggy, collars, drool, the left eye already shut. Harry in front, robes, glasses, flute at his mouth, cheeks puffed, scar. Hermione and Ron pressed to the door behind him, wands lit, tiny.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the heads, concentrated, not a musician, a boy who will not stop. The middle head looks at him, suspicious, then glassy. Hermione looks at the paws, waiting for the moment. Ron looks at the teeth, pale, staying.

#### C17-P1-P2

- **Beat:** They haul a head aside and drop through the trapdoor.
- **Image:** One huge head is a snoring weight they roll by the ear. The trapdoor ring is free. Hermione lifts it. A dark square. They look at each other and drop, Harry last, the flute tucked in his belt. A snore follows them down.
- **Setting:** The room above, the square of darkness below. Moonlight on the dog’s closed eyes. No ladder. A fall.
- **Who:** Hermione lifting the ring, robes, hair tied, determined. Ron sitting on the edge, freckles, red hair, swinging his legs in, scared and going. Harry, flute in his belt, glasses, one look back at Fluffy to be sure. The dog, asleep, three tongues out, ridiculous and lethal.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks down, a swallow, then she goes. Ron looks at Harry, a crooked brave grin. Harry looks at the sleeping heads, a flash of thanks to Hagrid’s music, then he jumps. Fluffy does not look at anything.

#### C17-P1-P3

- **Beat:** They land in something soft. It feels lucky.
- **Image:** They hit a mass of pale vines and sink, laughing once with relief. It is soft. It is dark. Harry’s glasses are askew and he is grinning. For one panel the fall was the danger and the landing is kind.
- **Setting:** A pit under the trapdoor. Wand-light. Thick pale plants, leaves like fingers, a dirt smell, no floor visible. The trapdoor a small square of moonlight far above.
- **Who:** Harry, Ron, Hermione waist-deep in vines, robes rumpled, wands up, the flute still in Harry’s belt. They look like children who got away with something.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Ron, a breathless laugh. Ron looks at the leaves, pleased. Hermione looks at the vines, the laugh dying first, she recognizes them. Her face changes before theirs.

#### C17-P1-P4

- **Beat:** Vines tighten. Ron is pulled under.
- **Image:** The plants wake. Loops cinch. Ron yelps and is yanked down to his chest, then his shoulders. A vine crosses his mouth. Harry grabs his wrist and is pulled too. The softness was a mouth.
- **Setting:** The pit, wand-light swinging, leaves wrapping, moonlight far above useless.
- **Who:** Ron, red hair, freckles, eyes huge, one arm up, the rest disappearing into pale green. Harry, both hands on that wrist, glasses, robes caught. Hermione higher on a thicker stem, one arm free, wand in it. Devil’s Snare, many fingers, no face, patient.
- **Faces:** Ron looks up at Harry, panic, he cannot shout. Harry looks at Ron’s disappearing shoulder, fear, pulling. Hermione looks at the plant, thinking hard, afraid and angry at it. The plant has no face. Its grip is the expression.

#### C17-P1-P5

- **Beat:** Hermione shouts that Devil’s Snare hates light.
- **Image:** She has it. Her mouth is open on the name. Her wand is already turning. Harry looks at her, lost, still pulling. Ron’s fingers slip in his. The knowledge is a light before the spell is.
- **Setting:** The pit, dark leaves, her face the brightest thing.
- **Who:** Hermione, hair escaping the tie, robes, wand up, the answer in her whole posture. Harry straining. Ron’s hand, freckled, weakening.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at the vines, fierce, certain. She looks at the boys, a command in her eyes: do not fight it, I am here. Harry looks at her, trusting. Ron’s eyes look at her, the panic pausing half a second.

#### C17-P1-P6

- **Beat:** She conjures fire. The vines recoil. They crawl free, coughing.
- **Image:** A bright, controlled fire bursts from her wand, blue-white then honest flame. The vines whip back like burned fingers. Ron surges up, gasping. They scramble onto a stone lip. Smoke. The plant sulks in the dark behind them.
- **Setting:** The edge of the pit, a passage beginning, stone, their wands and the fire the only light. The trapdoor moon above, left behind.
- **Who:** Hermione, wand burning, hair wild, soot on her cheek, robes. Ron on hands and knees, coughing, red hair full of leaves, freckles, alive. Harry pulling him, glasses smudged, flute safe, a vine-burn on his wrist.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at the retreating plant, shaken and proud. Ron looks at her, coughing, adoring gratitude. Harry looks at Ron, making sure, then at Hermione, a nod that is a thank-you. They look singed and together.

### Page 2 — The keys

#### C17-P2-P1

- **Beat:** A room of wings. Hundreds of keys batter the air.
- **Image:** The next chamber is tall and bright with motion. Hundreds of winged keys whirl, gold and silver and brass, a storm with teeth. The door on the far side is locked. A broom leans by the near door, waiting for a Seeker.
- **Setting:** A stone room, high windows or none, torch brackets, the air itself occupied. The locked door, old and heavy, a keyhole.
- **Who:** The keys, small metal bodies, delicate wings, hostile. Harry, Ron, Hermione in the doorway, leaves still in their hair, looking up. The broom, old, school-issued, against the wall.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the broom, recognition, the courtyard and the pitch in his hands already. Ron looks at the flock, ducking a key. Hermione looks at the lock, then at Harry, she knows this one is his. A key looks, somehow, vicious.

#### C17-P2-P2

- **Beat:** Harry takes the broom without being asked.
- **Image:** He is astride before the plan is spoken. Hermione opens her mouth and closes it. Ron hands him nothing; there is nothing to hand. The flock rustles, interested.
- **Setting:** The near wall. The broom. The storm of keys above.
- **Who:** Harry, robes, glasses, scar, leaves in his hair, hands right on the broom, the flute no longer the point. Hermione, a half-gesture of be careful. Ron, a thumbs-up he immediately regrets as uncool and leaves up anyway.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the lock, then at the flock, calm the way he is only in the air. Hermione looks at him, trust. Ron looks at him, pride. Harry does not look back for long. The job is up.

#### C17-P2-P3

- **Beat:** He flies. The keys swirl like a hostile flock.
- **Image:** He kicks off and the room becomes sky. Keys dive at his face. He banks. A silver one clips his ear. The flock turns as a school of fish. He is the only living shape among metal wings.
- **Setting:** The full height of the room. Torches. The locked door small below. Ron and Hermione tiny, shouting clues he half hears.
- **Who:** Harry, scarlet-less school robes streaming anyway, glasses, broom, a line of blood starting at his ear or hand. The keys, a whirlwind. His friends, two upturned faces.
- **Faces:** Harry looks through the flock, searching, not swatting, Seeker-eyes. Pain flickers and gets filed. Hermione looks up, tracking a large old key with her finger. Ron looks up, flinching for him.

#### C17-P2-P4

- **Beat:** One old silver key, bent wing.
- **Image:** There. Among the bright new keys, one heavy old silver key with a bent wing, favoring one side, circling near the door it belongs to. Hermione’s pointing finger and Harry’s gaze meet on it. It knows it has been seen and darts.
- **Setting:** Mid-air, the flock parting for one second around the old key. Torchlight on silver.
- **Who:** The old key, tarnished, one wing crooked, larger than the others. Harry banking hard toward it. Hermione below, arm straight, she found it. Ron hopping.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the bent wing, focus narrowing to a point. The key, if metal can panic, panics. Hermione looks at Harry, a fierce go. Her face is a teammate’s.

#### C17-P2-P5

- **Beat:** It fights and cuts his hand. His fist closes on it.
- **Image:** The catch is not clean. The key rakes his palm, a bright cut, wings beating against his knuckles. He closes his fist anyway. Blood and silver. He pulls up before the wall.
- **Setting:** Close to the far wall, the locked door under him, torches.
- **Who:** Harry’s hand, the key crushed carefully inside it, wings still struggling, blood between his fingers. His face, glasses, a grimace that is also a grin. The broom.
- **Faces:** He looks at his fist, pained, certain he has the right one. The key does not stop fighting. He looks like the boy who caught the Remembrall, older by a year of Saturdays.

#### C17-P2-P6

- **Beat:** The lock turns. They run through before the flock regroups.
- **Image:** He is down, the key in the lock, the door swinging. The flock, cheated, dives as one. Hermione yanks Ron through. Harry kicks the door shut. A dozen keys ping off the wood. Silence, then their breath.
- **Setting:** The doorway between rooms. Behind, the angry metallic storm. Ahead, a cold quiet and a chessboard glimpsed.
- **Who:** Harry, bleeding hand, broom left behind, glasses, shoulder to the shut door. Hermione pulling Ron. Ron, red hair, a key-cut on his cheek, laughing once in shock. The old key, spent, in the lock.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at his hand, then at them, a shaky smile. Hermione looks at the cut, already thinking of the next room, a thank-you in the look. Ron looks at the door, giddy to be unpecked. They run forward.

### Page 3 — The chessboard

#### C17-P3-P1

- **Beat:** Giant chess. The pieces are stone and armed.
- **Image:** A hall-sized board. The squares are the floor. The pieces are taller than the children, carved stone, swords and halberds real enough. They turn their heads as the three enter. A king. A queen with a blank, terrible face. The far door is beyond the last rank.
- **Setting:** A cold chamber, black and white squares, torchlight, no windows. Echo. The pieces’ shadows are longer than they are.
- **Who:** Harry, Ron, Hermione, small at the edge, leaves and cuts and wands. The white pieces nearest, a pawn’s stone eyes rolling down. The black pieces waiting on the far side, hostile.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at the board, and for the first time tonight he looks at home. The fear is still there. The understanding is bigger. Harry looks at Ron, seeing it. Hermione looks at the queen, wary. A pawn looks at them, impassive, waiting to be commanded or to crush them.

#### C17-P3-P2

- **Beat:** Ron walks the board the way other people read. He places his friends.
- **Image:** Ron steps onto the squares without flinching. He points Harry to a bishop’s empty place, Hermione to a rook’s. He talks with his hands, the fever and the jokes gone, a commander. The pieces allow it. One knight steps aside for him.
- **Setting:** The board, mid-field, torches, the huge pieces making aisles.
- **Who:** Ron, maroon sweater under robes, freckles, red hair, bandaged no longer, the bitten hand gesturing, sure. Harry moving to a bishop square, trusting. Hermione to a castle square, a book-person suddenly a piece, accepting it. A stone knight, horse-head, stepping back.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at the whole board, not at his fear. Calm, bright, the best of him. Harry looks at Ron, respect, he will go where he is told. Hermione looks at Ron, a new understanding, she is not the clever one in this room. The knight looks at Ron, as if recognizing a player.

#### C17-P3-P3

- **Beat:** Harry a bishop. Hermione a castle. Ron takes a knight.
- **Image:** They are in place. Harry stands on a bishop’s square, diagonal world. Hermione on a rook’s, straight lines. Ron swings onto the stone knight’s back, small on the carved horse. The black pieces stir. The game can start.
- **Setting:** The board from slightly above, so the squares read. Three children among giants.
- **Who:** Harry, glasses, scar, bleeding hand, on his square, wand in his sleeve. Hermione, hair, robes, serious. Ron astride the knight, red hair a flag, freckles, a nod to begin. Stone pieces, armed.
- **Faces:** Ron looks across at the black king, already ten moves ahead. Harry looks at Ron, waiting for the order. Hermione looks along her line, ready to move when told. She looks like she hates not knowing the plan and trusts it anyway.

#### C17-P3-P4

- **Beat:** Pieces smash each other apart. Dust and swords.
- **Image:** The game is violent. A black pawn explodes into rubble under a white piece’s sword. Dust rolls. Hermione’s rook charges and she hangs on, hair whipping, a shout of effort. A stone head rolls past Harry’s foot. This is not a classroom board.
- **Setting:** The board in chaos. Torchlight through dust. Cracks in the floor. The far door still shut.
- **Who:** Hermione riding or running her piece, fierce, a scrape on her cheek. Harry moved diagonal, a bishop’s lunge, glasses dusty. Ron on the knight, shouting a move, in his element and terrified. Stone soldiers breaking.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at the wreckage, calculating, a wince when a piece dies, he feels it. Hermione looks at the next square, determined, not screaming. Harry looks at Ron for the next order, a soldier. The black queen turns her blank face toward Ron’s knight.

#### C17-P3-P5

- **Beat:** Ron sees the only win and rides forward to be taken.
- **Image:** Quiet inside the noise. Ron sees it. His face goes still. He looks at Harry, then at the queen, then he smiles a small, crooked, serious smile and puts his heels to the knight. He rides into the square where the queen can take him. He does not look like a boy messing about. He looks like a choice.
- **Setting:** The board, dust settling for one second. The queen’s shadow over his square. The far door close now.
- **Who:** Ron, red hair, freckles, maroon sleeves, on the knight, small. The black queen, tall, sword, blank face, turning toward him. Harry several squares away, realizing too late. Hermione further, a cry starting.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at Harry, the smile, a go-on already in it. Love and terror and pride. Harry looks at him, no, the word not out yet. The queen looks at Ron, nothing human, the sword rising. Hermione looks at Ron, understanding a second after Harry, horror.

#### C17-P3-P6

- **Beat:** The queen strikes him down. He has already told them to go on.
- **Image:** The sword comes down. Ron is flung from the knight, a small body on the stone, still. Dust. The king is in check from the move he bought; Harry’s square is the mate, and he will have to take it. Ron’s eyes are closed. His hand is open toward the far door.
- **Setting:** The board. Rubble. Torch. The door beyond the king.
- **Who:** Ron on the stones, red hair fanned, freckles, a bruise already, breathing — he is alive and out. The queen, toppled or spent by the sequence, not the focus. Harry on his bishop square, the winning move under his feet, looking back. Hermione running toward Ron and stopping because the game is not over until they move.
- **Faces:** Ron’s face is slack, a ghost of the smile, unconscious. Harry looks at him, wrecked, then at the door, because Ron already told him. Hermione looks at Ron, tears she blinks away, then at Harry, we have to finish. Grief and obedience.

### Page 4 — Logic and goodbye

#### C17-P4-P1

- **Beat:** A troll, already unconscious. Hermione does not slow down.
- **Image:** The next room has a mountain troll, the bathroom’s cousin, flat on its back, snoring, club loose. It is a solved problem. Hermione steps around its hand without looking twice. Harry follows, the chess-dust still on him. They do not have time to be surprised.
- **Setting:** A stinking stone room, the troll, a door beyond. Torchlight. No fight.
- **Who:** The troll, gray, huge, asleep, harmless as a hill. Hermione, hair, a cut on her cheek, robes, walking. Harry, bleeding hand, glasses, looking back once toward where Ron lies in the previous room, then forward.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at the next door, focused, she will not spend Ron’s move on a fainted troll. Harry looks at the troll, a grim almost-laugh, then at her back, following. The troll’s face is slack, tongue out, comic and ignored.

#### C17-P4-P2

- **Beat:** Seven bottles and a riddle.
- **Image:** A table. Seven bottles of different sizes, stoppers, liquids: some clear, some amber, one ruby, one black. A riddle burned into a parchment or into the air in thin flame. Two doors: one back, one forward. No monster. A brain.
- **Setting:** A small chamber, cleaner, colder, the table centered. Torches. The forward door iron, the back door the way they came.
- **Who:** Hermione and Harry, alone, dusty, cut, standing before the bottles. No Ron. The riddle is the third presence.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at the riddle, the world narrowing to logic, lips parting. Harry looks at the bottles, then at her, he knows this is hers the way the keys were his. He looks relieved and afraid of that relief.

#### C17-P4-P3

- **Beat:** She reads it once, then again, lips moving.
- **Image:** Close. Her eyes move. Her lips shape the puzzle. A finger lifts, counting bottles without touching them. Harry does not interrupt. The chess bruise of the evening is on both of them and she sets it aside to think.
- **Setting:** The table, bottle-light, her face.
- **Who:** Hermione, bushy hair singed at one tip, a scratch, robes, empty hands, the respect of not touching until she knows. Harry a soft presence at the edge, watching her work.
- **Faces:** She looks at the riddle, utterly concentrated, the eager classroom girl become something quieter and stronger. A flicker of fear she will be slow. Harry looks at her, trust, he does not hover. His face says take the time. Ron bought it.

#### C17-P4-P4

- **Beat:** One swallow goes back. One swallow goes forward.
- **Image:** Her hand finally moves. She touches two bottles. One small, for the way back, enough for one. One smaller, for the way on, enough for one. The other five are death or wine or nettle, and she leaves them. The puzzle is solved in the placement of her fingers.
- **Setting:** The table, top-down feeling, seven bottles, two chosen.
- **Who:** Hermione’s hands, ink from another life under the dust, sure. Harry’s hand not touching any bottle. The two chosen vials, one clear and cold-looking, one tiny and bright.
- **Faces:** Only her mouth and eyes at the top of the frame, certain, sad, because the amounts mean a goodbye. Harry’s eyes at the side, he has understood. They look at the two bottles, not at each other yet.

#### C17-P4-P5

- **Beat:** She says she is not the one who should face what is next, and puts the forward bottle in Harry’s hand.
- **Image:** She presses the tiny bottle into his bleeding hand, careful of the cut. She keeps the other. The iron door is behind him. Her face is close, urgent, not a speech, a decision. She is sending him and staying with the way back to Ron.
- **Setting:** The chamber. The forward door. Torchlight. Two children at the end of the trio.
- **Who:** Hermione, the back bottle in her other hand, hair, scratch, robes. Harry, glasses, scar, the tiny bottle in the cut palm, flute still absurd in his belt, chess dust. No one else.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at Harry, fierce love, fear, she hates this part. She looks at his cut hand, practical even now. Harry looks at her, he wants to argue and does not. Gratitude. A goodbye neither calls a goodbye. His jaw is set.

#### C17-P4-P6

- **Beat:** He drinks. The door opens. Her face is the last thing he sees as it shuts.
- **Image:** Page-turn. He drinks the swallow. Cold fire. The iron door opens on black. He steps through. It begins to close. Through the narrowing gap, Hermione’s face, one hand raised, not quite a wave, the other bottle clutched. Then the door shuts. He is alone.
- **Setting:** The threshold. Behind her, bottles and torch. Ahead of him, darkness, a hint of a tall frame that might be a mirror. The door, iron, closing.
- **Who:** Harry, alone on the far side, the taste of the potion, glasses, scar, empty tiny bottle dropped. Hermione on the near side, reaching, then gone as the door meets stone.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at him, brave, eyes bright, stay alive. Harry looks at her, the same, a small nod. When the door shuts, his face is alone in the dark, the nod unfinished. The year of three becomes one.

---

## Chapter 18 — Quirrell, not Snape

The mirror, the wrong teacher, a lie, a burning touch, and a collapse.

### Page 1 — The wrong teacher

#### C18-P1-P1

- **Beat:** A chamber. The Mirror of Erised at the far end.
- **Image:** He steps into a round, cold chamber. Torches. At the far end stands the mirror he knows, ornate, tall, uncovered. No dog, no chess, no friend. His footsteps are the only sound. He looks small, and the mirror looks like Christmas and a trap.
- **Setting:** The last underground room. Stone, torches in a ring, the mirror centered, the floor empty. No Snape. The emptiness is suspicious.
- **Who:** Harry alone, robes torn and dusty, glasses, scar, bleeding hand, the flute a silly shape at his belt, empty bottle gone. The mirror, carved frame, waiting. His reflection, when he is close enough, not yet the point.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the mirror, recognition and dread. He looks around for Snape and finds no one. His mouth is slightly open. Young, alone, not running.

#### C18-P1-P2

- **Beat:** Harry says Snape’s name. A polite voice says Snape is not here.
- **Image:** Harry turns, the name half-said. A polite voice comes from the side, mild, amused. The speaker is still in shadow near a pillar. Harry’s shoulders drop, then rise again, because the voice is wrong for a rescue.
- **Setting:** The chamber’s edge. Torch shadow. The mirror behind Harry, watching.
- **Who:** Harry, dusty, glasses, scar, turned. A tall shape in the shadow, not yet clear, purple more than black. Hands folded.
- **Faces:** Harry looks toward the voice, expecting a hooked nose and black eyes, confused when the voice is courteous. Fear changes flavor. The shadowed head tilts, a smile we cannot quite see.

#### C18-P1-P3

- **Beat:** Quirrell steps out, turban neat, no stutter.
- **Image:** He steps into the torchlight. Professor Quirrell. The purple turban is perfectly wrapped. The stammer is gone. His shoulders are straight. He looks like a different man who has been wearing a timid one as a coat. Harry takes a step back toward the mirror.
- **Setting:** The chamber, full light now on the teacher. Torches. The mirror. No Snape anywhere, and the absence is the twist.
- **Who:** Quirrell, pale, purple robes, large turban, hands calm, a new posture, taller than Harry by the usual adult measure and by the surprise. Harry, small, dusty, the cut hand, glasses, scar prickling.
- **Faces:** Quirrell looks at Harry, polite, almost friendly, a cold intelligence. No stutter in the mouth. Harry looks at him, the year’s theory collapsing. Shock. He looks at the turban, the pub, Hagrid’s hand waving at his own head. Oh.

#### C18-P1-P4

- **Beat:** Snape’s muttering at the match was a countercurse. Snape was saving him.
- **Image:** Quirrell says it lightly, a teacher correcting a student’s essay. Harry’s face runs back through the year: the match, the muttering, the blue flame, the bleeding leg. In a small memory flash at the edge, Snape’s hem burns and his eyes were on the broom to save it, not to curse it. Harry looks ill with the revision.
- **Setting:** The chamber. The memory is a thin panel-within, the Quidditch box, Snape’s mouth moving, McGonagall shouting, the broom leveling.
- **Who:** Quirrell, turban, calm hands, enjoying the correction. Harry, the present, dusty, one hand half-raised as if to stop a story. Memory-Snape, black, intense, protective and unliked.
- **Faces:** Quirrell looks at Harry, a thin smile, he likes this part. Harry looks at the floor, ashamed and reeling, the hatred he spent all year mis-aimed. He looks younger. He looks angry at himself, then back at Quirrell, the anger finding the right target.

#### C18-P1-P5

- **Beat:** Harry backs up. Quirrell says he has served Voldemort since before the year began.
- **Image:** Harry’s heels touch the mirror’s base. Quirrell does not hurry. He speaks the name without flinching, which is obscene after a year of flinches. His hand rests on his own turban, a hint. Harry’s scar answers.
- **Setting:** The mirror’s foot. Torches. Nowhere behind Harry but glass.
- **Who:** Quirrell, purple, turban under his fingers, pale, sure. Harry, back to the carved frame, glasses, scar beginning to burn, wand in his sleeve, not drawn yet because the shock is still faster than his hand.
- **Faces:** Quirrell looks at Harry, devout and calm, a servant glad to be near the end of a task. Harry looks at him, horror, the forest hood and this polite face trying to become the same person. His mouth is tight. He does not beg.

#### C18-P1-P6

- **Beat:** Quirrell turns. A flat, snake-like face is on the back of his head.
- **Image:** Page of the turn, literal. Quirrell rotates. The turban is gone, unwound in his hands. On the back of his skull, under where the cloth was, a face: flat, pale, slit-nosed, red eyes, a mouth. Voldemort, not a body, a passenger. Harry’s scar goes white. The mirror is behind Harry. The face looks past Quirrell’s shoulder.
- **Setting:** The chamber. Torchlight cruel and clear. The unwound turban in Quirrell’s hands like a shed skin.
- **Who:** Quirrell, back of the head to us and to Harry, the face embedded there, purple robes. Harry, glasses, both hands half-up, scar livid, dusty robes, alone.
- **Faces:** The face on the skull looks at Harry, hatred and a horrible fondness, a snake’s attention. Quirrell’s own face is turned away, we see only the set of his neck, obedient. Harry looks at the face, pain and recognition, the thing from the forest finally has a place to be. He does not look away.

### Page 2 — The mirror’s test

#### C18-P2-P1

- **Beat:** Voldemort’s voice tells Quirrell to use the boy.
- **Image:** The face speaks. Quirrell answers his own skull, a grotesque conversation, one man two mouths. A pale hand points at Harry and then at the mirror. Harry is the tool. The room feels smaller.
- **Setting:** The chamber. Mirror. Torches. The pointing finger.
- **Who:** Quirrell, front face strained, back face commanding. Harry, against the mirror, small, wand now in his hand at last, a twig again. The mirror, dark.
- **Faces:** The skull-face looks at Harry, impatient, hungry. Quirrell’s front face looks at Harry, a servant’s urgency, sweat finally, the stammer not back but the fear of failing is. Harry looks at the wand in his own hand, then at them, jaw set. He looks at the mirror, remembering Dumbledore’s warning and needing the glass anyway.

#### C18-P2-P2

- **Beat:** Quirrell sees himself holding the Stone in the glass and cannot take it.
- **Image:** Quirrell stares into the mirror. In the glass, his reflection holds the red Stone and smiles a mad smile. He paws at the glass. His hands meet nothing. The real Stone is not in the reflection’s pocket for him. He snarls, the polite man gone. The skull-face hisses.
- **Setting:** The mirror, close. Torchlight and the red of the imagined Stone.
- **Who:** Quirrell, both faces frustrated, purple robes, hands on the glass. In the mirror: Quirrell triumphant with the Stone, a lie the mirror tells a thief. No Harry in this crop, or only his eye at the edge.
- **Faces:** Quirrell looks at his reflection, greed, then rage when he cannot reach. The skull-face looks at the glass, furious, ancient. The reflection looks smug and useless. Desire without worth.

#### C18-P2-P3

- **Beat:** He forces Harry in front of the mirror.
- **Image:** Quirrell grabs Harry by the shoulder — not skin to skin yet, a fistful of robe — and plants him before the glass. Harry’s dusty face fills the lower mirror. Quirrell looms behind, both his faces eager. Use the boy.
- **Setting:** The mirror, the chamber, Harry centered by force.
- **Who:** Harry, robes bunched in Quirrell’s fist, glasses, scar, bleeding hand, looking at the glass because he is made to. Quirrell behind, turban gone, the skull-face peering around, hideous. The mirror waking.
- **Faces:** Harry looks into the glass, afraid of what he wants. Quirrell looks at the back of Harry’s head, hungry. The skull-face looks at the mirror, whispering. Harry’s reflection begins as only himself.

#### C18-P2-P4

- **Beat:** Harry’s reflection slips the Stone into a pocket.
- **Image:** Inside the glass, Harry sees himself, not as a champion, as a boy who finds the Stone and puts it away, safe, not used. The reflection winks, almost, and drops the red Stone into a pocket. It is a small motion. Harry’s real eyes widen.
- **Setting:** The mirror’s world. Warm, strange. The real room pale around the frame.
- **Who:** Mirror-Harry, cleaner, a small smile, the red Stone between finger and thumb, pocketing it. Real Harry, dusty, watching. Quirrell behind, not seeing what Harry sees.
- **Faces:** Mirror-Harry looks at real Harry, conspirator, calm. Real Harry looks at the pocketing, understanding flooding him. He wants to find the Stone, not use it. The mirror knows. His face is awe and a secret. Quirrell’s reflected back-face looks impatient, seeing only Harry’s back.

#### C18-P2-P5

- **Beat:** The real weight drops into Harry’s pocket. He understands.
- **Image:** A weight. In the real pocket of his real robe, the Stone arrives, small, hard, warm. His hand covers the pocket from the outside, casual, a boy hiding a sweet. The mirror’s trick is done. He understands the test in the same second: it comes to the one who would not use it.
- **Setting:** Close on the pocket, the robe, the dusty hand. The mirror glow fading from the cloth.
- **Who:** Harry’s hand, cut palm, pressing the outside of the pocket. The Stone, a round hardness, not shown naked, felt. Quirrell’s robe at the edge, not touching skin.
- **Faces:** Harry’s face above, a flash of wonder he crushes into stillness. He looks at his own hand, then schools his face. He looks like a terrible liar about to try. The scar hurts. He ignores it.

#### C18-P2-P6

- **Beat:** He lies, clearly. He only sees himself winning the House Cup and shaking Dumbledore’s hand.
- **Image:** Harry turns from the glass, a bright, bad, child’s lie on his face. He describes a trophy and a handshake, the most ordinary famous-boy wish he can invent. His hand stays over the pocket. Quirrell leans in, both faces, hunting the truth in Harry’s mouth.
- **Setting:** The chamber. Mirror behind Harry. Torches.
- **Who:** Harry, dusty, glasses, the lie in his posture, pocket guarded. Quirrell bent down, front face eager, skull-face suspicious around the side. The red Stone hidden.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Quirrell, earnest, lying, a shake of the head, I only see the Cup. He is not good at this and he is clear. Quirrell’s front face looks at him, wanting to believe, frustrated. The skull-face looks at Harry’s eyes, not his mouth. It does not believe.

### Page 3 — The face

#### C18-P3-P1

- **Beat:** Voldemort says the boy lies.
- **Image:** The skull-face snarls the truth. Quirrell’s front face twists, obedient, and his hand shoots toward the pocket. Harry twists away. The lie is over. The chamber jumps from talk to fight.
- **Setting:** The mirror foot. Torches flaring. Close, violent, no room.
- **Who:** The skull-face, red eyes, mouth ugly. Quirrell lunging, purple, pale hands. Harry turning, pocket side away, wand up at last, glasses crooked.
- **Faces:** The face looks at Harry, hatred, certainty. Quirrell looks at the pocket, desperate servant. Harry looks at the hand coming, fear and refusal. He will not give it over.

#### C18-P3-P2

- **Beat:** Quirrell grabs Harry’s arm. The hands smoke.
- **Image:** Skin meets skin. Quirrell’s fingers lock on Harry’s bare wrist where the sleeve rode up. Smoke jumps from the grip. A scream. The Stone is still in the pocket. The touch itself is the weapon, and it is not Harry’s wand.
- **Setting:** Extreme close. Wrist, fingers, smoke, torchlight. The mirror a gold smear behind.
- **Who:** Quirrell’s hand, pale, already blistering, locked on Harry’s wrist. Harry’s arm, thin, a child’s, the scar not here but the mother’s protection in the skin. Smoke, not fire from a wand.
- **Faces:** Not fully in frame. Harry’s mouth at the edge, shocked by the scream that is not his. Quirrell’s front face further, agony, confusion. He looks at his own smoking hand as if it betrayed him.

#### C18-P3-P3

- **Beat:** Quirrell screams and lets go. The skin is blistered.
- **Image:** He reels back, holding his burned hand with the other. Blisters. Smoke. Harry staggers free, wrist red but whole. They stare at the hand. The skull-face is screaming too, a thinner sound.
- **Setting:** The chamber, a step of space between them, torches, the mirror.
- **Who:** Quirrell, purple robes, burned hand outstretched, disbelief. Harry, wrist clutched, pocket still heavy, glasses, dusty, wand in the other hand unused. The skull-face twisted in rage.
- **Faces:** Quirrell looks at his palm, agony, betrayed by a child’s skin. Harry looks at his own wrist, stunned, he does not understand the gift yet. The skull-face looks at Harry, incandescent hate. Harry looks back, the stun becoming a choice.

#### C18-P3-P4

- **Beat:** Voldemort shrieks at him to hold on.
- **Image:** The skull-face is all mouth and command. Quirrell, crying with pain, turns back because he is more afraid of the face than of the burn. He reaches again. Harry sets his feet. He has decided to be touched.
- **Setting:** The chamber. Harsh light. The mirror witnessing.
- **Who:** Quirrell, both faces, reaching, burned hand and the other. Harry, small, sleeves pushed up now on purpose, glasses, scar white, pocket guarded by his body.
- **Faces:** The skull-face looks at Quirrell, commanding, contempt for his pain. Quirrell looks at Harry, terror and obedience. Harry looks at the reaching hands, afraid and resolved. His chin is down. He will grab first.

#### C18-P3-P5

- **Beat:** Harry grabs the face and the arm on purpose. Both of them burn.
- **Image:** Harry lunges. One hand on Quirrell’s wrist. The other, awful and brave, against the skull-face. Smoke erupts from both contacts. Quirrell’s scream fills the room. Harry’s own hands hurt. He does not let go. It is ugly. It is a child holding on.
- **Setting:** The center of the chamber. Smoke. Torches. The mirror flaring white for a second. No grace, no duel poses.
- **Who:** Harry, small, both hands locked on, face twisted with effort and disgust and pain, glasses askew, scar blazing. Quirrell, buckling, purple, flesh blistering where Harry touches. The skull-face under Harry’s palm, smoking, a shriek.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the face he is holding, tears from pain, teeth set, he hates this and he will not stop. Quirrell’s front face looks at the ceiling, screaming. The skull-face looks at Harry from under the hand, rage and, for the first time, fear.

#### C18-P3-P6

- **Beat:** The Stone is a hard point in the pocket. Harry does not let go.
- **Image:** Amid the smoke, a small still detail: the pocket, the round hardness of the Stone against Harry’s hip, safe. His hands are locked above. He is failing and winning in the same picture. His knees buckle and he hangs on by the grip itself.
- **Setting:** Smoke, firelight, the chamber floor coming closer as they sag.
- **Who:** Harry’s torso, the pocket, his blistering-but-whole hands on Quirrell. Quirrell folding.
- **Faces:** Harry’s face, close, eyes shutting, opening, shutting. Pain. Stubbornness. He looks at nothing now. He looks like someone about to pass out who has not finished. The skull-face’s visible eye looks afraid.

### Page 4 — Collapse

#### C18-P4-P1

- **Beat:** Quirrell comes apart under Harry’s hands.
- **Image:** Where Harry holds, Quirrell’s form fails, not gore for its own sake: ash, smoke, a man losing the shape of his arms. The purple robe empties at the edges. Harry is still attached, horrified, unable to release because letting go might let the face go free.
- **Setting:** The chamber, smoke thick, the mirror a pale rectangle, torches guttering.
- **Who:** Harry, small, coughing, hands locked, glasses cracked or just dirty, scar a white line. Quirrell, dissolving at the contact, one human eye wide, the skull-face peeling toward smoke.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at what his hands are doing, horror, he did not want this, he wanted the hands to stop. Quirrell’s remaining face looks at Harry, agony, a servant at the end. The skull-face looks beyond Harry, toward escape.

#### C18-P4-P2

- **Beat:** The face on the back of the head is furious and afraid.
- **Image:** Close on that face as the head fails. Fury, and under it fear, a thing that drank unicorn blood rather than die, meeting a child’s skin and losing. Red eyes. A mouth that has ordered murders, open now on a thinner scream.
- **Setting:** Smoke. No room. Just the face and Harry’s palm.
- **Who:** The face, Voldemort unbodied, and Harry’s hand, small, burning, not letting go.
- **Faces:** The face looks at Harry, hate and fear in equal measure. Harry’s eyes, at the edge, look back, crying, refusing. Neither looks noble. Both look at the end of the grip.

#### C18-P4-P3

- **Beat:** The scar is white-hot. Harry’s vision tunnels.
- **Image:** From inside Harry’s eyes. The room narrows to a circle. Smoke at the edges. The face in the center, then blurring. The scar’s pain is a white band across the top of the panel. His hands are still in the circle. Just.
- **Setting:** Subjective. The chamber reduced to a porthole of torch and smoke.
- **Who:** Harry’s view. His own hands, failing. The face. No friends. No Dumbledore yet.
- **Faces:** We see the face he sees, raging. We feel his face from the way the world shakes. He is going under. He looks at the pocket, a last check, the Stone still there, a hard truth in the blur.

#### C18-P4-P4

- **Beat:** He hears his own name in Dumbledore’s voice, far off.
- **Image:** The circle of vision is almost black. A voice, not a body: his name, calm, the headmaster’s. A hint of purple stars and silver beard at the edge of the dark, maybe real, maybe the last thing he wants. His fingers loosen.
- **Setting:** Near-black. A rim of torch. A suggestion of a tall figure arriving through smoke, not fully drawn, so we stay with Harry’s fading.
- **Who:** Harry’s hands falling. The voice. Perhaps Dumbledore’s hand entering the dark, long fingers, a ring, not clearly seen. Quirrell a heap of smoke and robe.
- **Faces:** No clear face. The feeling on Harry is relief so strong it looks like surrender. If Dumbledore’s eye is there, it looks at the boy, urgent, gentle, too late to stop the fainting and in time for the rest.

#### C18-P4-P5

- **Beat:** Quirrell falls. A dark smoke tears loose and flees through Harry.
- **Image:** The body collapses, a ruined robe. A stream of black smoke rips free, passes through Harry’s chest like a cold wind, and shoots toward the ceiling and away. It has no face now. It is escape. Harry’s hair lifts. His pocket stays heavy. The smoke does not take the Stone.
- **Setting:** The chamber, clearing slightly, the mirror, the fallen robe, a crack of air.
- **Who:** Quirrell’s empty robes, still. The smoke, a fleeing streak. Harry, upright for one last second, arms out, glasses, scar, the Stone in the pocket, a hole of cold where the smoke passed.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the smoke, fading, a question he cannot hold. The smoke has no face. It looks like flight. His own face is white, the stubbornness used up.

#### C18-P4-P6

- **Beat:** Harry hits the floor. The mirror shows an empty room.
- **Image:** Page-turn. He falls. The Stone is a small lump under him, safe against the stone floor and his robe. Dust. His glasses skid. In the mirror, for one cruel or kind second, the room is empty: no parents, no Cup, no thief. Just a fallen boy, and then the mirror goes ordinary. Silence.
- **Setting:** The chamber floor. Torches steadying. The mirror. Smoke thinning. No Quirrell standing. No friends yet. Dumbledore’s hem perhaps at the edge, a promise, not a rescue pose.
- **Who:** Harry, face-down or on his side, glasses off, scar quiet, dusty, burned-but-whole hands, the pocket between him and the floor. The mirror. The empty robes.
- **Faces:** Harry’s eyes are closed. Peace, finally, not death if we can help the next chapter, but the panel does not wink. He looks young. The mirror shows no desire. It shows a boy who did the thing and stopped. The room looks at him. No one in the glass waves.

---

## Chapter 19 — The hospital wing and the explanation

Bandages, a destroyed Stone, a mother’s protection, an unanswered question, and chocolate.

### Page 1 — Waking

#### C19-P1-P1

- **Beat:** White curtains. Sun. Bandaged hands.
- **Image:** He wakes to white. Sun through a high window. Both hands are bandaged, resting on a clean blanket. The underground is gone. A jug of water. The quiet of a school hospital, not a chamber.
- **Setting:** The hospital wing, daytime, summer, white curtains moving a little, other beds empty, flowers somewhere. Peace so bright it is suspicious.
- **Who:** Harry, in a hospital shirt, hair flat on one side, glasses set neatly on the side table by someone else, scar pale, bandaged hands. No one else in frame yet. Hedwig is not here. The Stone is not in sight.
- **Faces:** He looks at his hands, groggy, trying to remember why they hurt. Confused. Alive. His eyes move toward the glasses, wanting them, not reaching yet.

#### C19-P1-P2

- **Beat:** Dumbledore sits beside the bed as if he has been there a while.
- **Image:** With the glasses on — he has fumbled them up — Harry finds Dumbledore in the chair, long beard, half-moon spectacles, a pile of sweets he is not eating, a book closed on his knee. He looks like he has been guarding the sleep. Sun in his silver hair.
- **Setting:** The bedside. Curtains. A quiet wing. Summer afternoon.
- **Who:** Dumbledore, purple robes simpler today, or the starry ones, hat off and on his knee under the book, spectacles, beard, hands folded. Harry, bandaged, glasses, hospital shirt, small in the bed.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks at Harry, relief deep under the calm, a smile that is not a joke. Harry looks at him, the voice in the dark connecting to the man. Trust. A boy checking the world is still there. Dumbledore looks a little tired. He has been there a while.

#### C19-P1-P3

- **Beat:** Harry’s first reach is for the pocket. It is empty.
- **Image:** His bandaged hand paws the hospital shirt where a pocket is not. Panic. The Stone. He tries to sit. Dumbledore’s hand, gentle, rests on the blanket, not pinning him, steadying. The pocket is empty because there is no pocket. The panic is the first real expression.
- **Setting:** The bed. Sun. The chair.
- **Who:** Harry, struggling up, glasses, bandages, alarm. Dumbledore, one hand on the blanket’s edge, calm.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at his own side, frightened, did he fail after all. Dumbledore looks at him, understanding at once, kind. He looks at the empty hand. He is about to give the answer. Harry looks at him, begging for it.

#### C19-P1-P4

- **Beat:** Dumbledore says the Stone is safe, and that it will be destroyed.
- **Image:** He says it simply. Harry sinks back. Safe. Then the second clause. Destroyed. Harry’s face does both: relief, then a question. Dumbledore’s hands open, no Stone in them, nothing to show, the news is the whole picture.
- **Setting:** The quiet wing. A curtain breathes.
- **Who:** The two of them. Harry, bandaged, small. Dumbledore, tall even seated, beard, spectacles.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at him, relief first, the forest-hood losing. Then confusion about destroyed. Dumbledore looks at Harry, gentle, sure, a little sad for the object and not for the choice. He looks at Harry’s hands, grateful they are the cost and not a grave.

#### C19-P1-P5

- **Beat:** Harry asks why. He is thinking of endless life.
- **Image:** Harry’s question is small. In his eyes, a flicker of the book’s engraving, the red stone, Ron’s open hand, his parents’ faces in a mirror. Endless life. Why break that. He does not say the parents out loud. Dumbledore hears it anyway.
- **Setting:** The bed. Sun. A bowl of grapes or chocolate frogs waiting, ordinary, against the size of the question.
- **Who:** Harry, glasses, scar, bandaged hands on the blanket, the question. Dumbledore listening, beard, a long patience.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Dumbledore, earnest, a child who has seen what the Stone costs and still asks. Dumbledore looks at him, tender, the mirror-night remembered. He is not angry at the question.

#### C19-P1-P6

- **Beat:** Endless life and endless gold are not gifts the world should have.
- **Image:** Dumbledore looks toward the window, the summer grounds, children — tiny, far — dragging trunks, ordinary lives. His answer is quiet. Harry looks at those tiny students. The Stone would have bent all of them. The panel is a window and two faces, not a lecture.
- **Setting:** The hospital window. Lawns. End of term. Sun. Distant laughter, harmless.
- **Who:** Dumbledore, profile, silver, spectacles, sad wisdom. Harry, three-quarter, glasses, following his gaze. Tiny students outside, no faces needed.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks at the children outside, protective of a whole world, not only Harry. Harry looks out, then at Dumbledore, accepting it before he fully understands. A nod, small. His face is tired and older than September.

### Page 2 — Why Quirrell burned

#### C19-P2-P1

- **Beat:** Harry asks why his touch burned Quirrell.
- **Image:** He holds up the bandaged hands, the evidence. The question is practical and enormous. Dumbledore looks at the bandages as if they are a relic. The wing is very quiet.
- **Setting:** The bedside. White linen. Sun.
- **Who:** Harry, both hands raised a little, bandages, glasses, hospital shirt. Dumbledore, leaning in, spectacles low on his nose.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at his hands, uneasy with them, then at Dumbledore. He is afraid the answer will be that he is dangerous. Dumbledore looks at the hands, moved, a shine in the eye he does not hide. He looks at Harry’s face, ready to give him the better truth.

#### C19-P2-P2

- **Beat:** Dumbledore begins to talk about Harry’s mother.
- **Image:** He says her name with care. Harry goes still in the way he did before the mirror. A flower in a vase on the bedside table, ordinary, stands in for tenderness. Dumbledore’s voice is the light in the panel.
- **Setting:** The wing. The flower. Summer.
- **Who:** Dumbledore, hands folded, speaking. Harry, still, glasses, scar, listening with his whole body.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks at Harry, gentle, choosing words for a child. Harry looks at him, hungry, the mirror-mother already rising. Hope and fear of hearing it wrong. His mouth is closed. He is listening harder than he has listened all year.

#### C19-P2-P3

- **Beat:** A quiet panel. Lily as light and a shape between a crib and a green curse.
- **Image:** Not a spectacle. A dim nursery. A crib. A young woman with long dark-red hair stands between the crib and a spear of green light. She is seen mostly as a silhouette and a fall of hair, one hand raised, not a portrait to be copied, a feeling: someone stepping into the way. The green stops at her. The baby is only a small dark-haired shape.
- **Setting:** A cottage nursery at night, simplified, memory-soft, not the full ruin. A mobile. A blanket. The green light from a doorway.
- **Who:** Lily, hair, a simple dress or nightclothes, her face turned away or lost in light, one arm out. Baby Harry in the crib, a tuft of black hair. The attacker is not shown. Only the green.
- **Faces:** Her face is not a clear portrait. The love is in the stance, the way she will not step aside. The baby’s face is calm, asleep or just waking. No sneer, no duel. A person choosing.

#### C19-P2-P4

- **Beat:** Her love left a protection in Harry’s skin.
- **Image:** Back in the hospital. Dumbledore’s finger, very light, indicates Harry’s bandaged hand, not the scar. The protection is in the skin, not the lightning. Harry looks at his hand as if it belongs to her too.
- **Setting:** The bedside. Sun. The flower.
- **Who:** Dumbledore, spectacles, beard, a careful finger. Harry, the bandaged hand between them, glasses, hospital shirt.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks at the hand, reverent. Harry looks at it, the horror of Quirrell’s burning rewritten. Eyes wet. He looks at Dumbledore, a question and a thank-you mixed, though the thank-you is for her.

#### C19-P2-P5

- **Beat:** Quirrell, full of hate and of Voldemort, could not bear that touch.
- **Image:** Dumbledore says it plainly. Harry sees, small and far, the smoke of that chamber, now explained. He pulls the blanket a little higher, not from fear, from the size of it. A child held a monster off with his mother’s love and it looked like burning.
- **Setting:** The wing. Quiet. A curtain.
- **Who:** The two of them. No Quirrell. The explanation is the picture, their faces, his covered hands.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the blanket, overwhelmed, grieving and proud in a private way. Dumbledore looks at him, making sure he hears love and not a weapon. Kind, serious. Harry looks up, and the shame of the burning eases.

#### C19-P2-P6

- **Beat:** Harry looks at his bandaged hands as if they belong to someone else.
- **Image:** A quiet close-up. The hands on the white blanket. Bandages. A bit of soot still at the wrist the nurse missed. He turns them. They are his. They are also hers. He does not flex them into fists. He rests them.
- **Setting:** Linen. Sun. No one else in the crop.
- **Who:** Harry’s hands. The edge of his chin, glasses, a tear he has not wiped because his hands feel too important to use for that.
- **Faces:** His mouth, visible, is soft. Wonder. Sorrow. He looks at his hands the way he looked at the cloak. Inheritance.

### Page 3 — The question he is not ready for

#### C19-P3-P1

- **Beat:** Harry touches the scar and says it hurts when Voldemort is close.
- **Image:** He forgets the bandages and almost touches the scar with a wrapped finger. He says it as a fact he has been carrying since the forest, the match, the classroom. Dumbledore does not wave it away.
- **Setting:** The bed. Afternoon softening. The wing.
- **Who:** Harry, glasses, scar, a bandaged finger near it. Dumbledore, attentive, spectacles.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Dumbledore, checking if he is allowed to say the name here. He is. His face is serious. Dumbledore looks at the scar, not hungry like Ollivander, not hating like Snape. Studying, gentle. He nods.

#### C19-P3-P2

- **Beat:** Dumbledore says that is so, and that it is not a small detail.
- **Image:** He nods, once, the confirmation. Then he looks at Harry a long moment, the not-small part in his eyes. Harry straightens, the way he did when he was made Seeker, a burden recognized.
- **Setting:** The chair and the bed. Quiet.
- **Who:** The two. A plate of chocolate frogs now visible, waiting, a child-thing beside a large truth.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks at Harry, honest, he will not pretend the scar is only a mark. Harry looks back, a little afraid, steadied by being believed. No one smiles. Respect.

#### C19-P3-P3

- **Beat:** Harry asks why Voldemort wanted to kill a baby.
- **Image:** The question is the smallest voice in the chapter and the largest. Harry’s hands are fists in the bandages now. He looks very eleven. Dumbledore’s face goes still. The summer window is too bright.
- **Setting:** The wing. The window. The bed.
- **Who:** Harry, hospital shirt, glasses, the question. Dumbledore, beard, spectacles, a man with an answer he will not spend today.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at him, pleading, plain. He wants the reason the way he wanted the letter. Dumbledore looks at Harry, love and a gate. He looks older. He looks sorry.

#### C19-P3-P4

- **Beat:** Silence, held for a whole panel.
- **Image:** No speech. The curtain moves. A bird crosses the window. Dumbledore’s hands stay folded. Harry waits. The silence is not empty. It is a decision.
- **Setting:** The hospital wing, summer, a clock’s tick you could hear if this were sound.
- **Who:** Both of them, unmoving. The chocolate frogs. The flower.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Dumbledore, hope thinning into patience. Dumbledore looks at his own hands, then at the boy, choosing. Sad. Certain. Kind.

#### C19-P3-P5

- **Beat:** Dumbledore says Harry will know when he is older. Not today.
- **Image:** He leans in and says it, a promise and a refusal. His hand covers Harry’s bandaged fist, brief. Not today. Harry’s face falls and then accepts, because this man has not lied to him about the mirror or the Stone.
- **Setting:** The bedside, close.
- **Who:** Dumbledore’s hand over the bandages. Harry’s face, glasses, scar. The beard. The spectacles.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks at Harry, loving, immovable. Harry looks at him, disappointed, trusting anyway. A small nod. Childhood extended one more year by an adult who can still do that. His eyes are wet and he is not arguing.

#### C19-P3-P6

- **Beat:** A box of chocolate frogs on the blanket. Dumbledore leaves the answer unfinished.
- **Image:** He sets the box on the blanket, a ridiculous gentle gesture after that question. He stands, hat in hand, very tall. Harry looks at the frogs, then at Dumbledore’s back as he goes. The answer stays in the room, unfinished, on purpose.
- **Setting:** The wing. The door. Sun. End of a long conversation.
- **Who:** Dumbledore, hat on now, purple, leaving, a look back. Harry, the box in his lap, bandaged, glasses. Madam Pomfrey in the far doorway, apron, approving of sweets and not of excitement, a nod to the headmaster.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks back, a small smile, the twinkle returning carefully. Harry looks at him, a smaller smile, thank you and not yet. Pomfrey looks at Harry, professional tenderness. The unfinished question sits in Harry’s eyes after the smile.

### Page 4 — The friends

#### C19-P4-P1

- **Beat:** Ron and Hermione burst in, remember to be quiet, and burst anyway.
- **Image:** The door bangs. Pomfrey hisses. They tiptoe at a run, which is useless. Ron’s head is bandaged. Hermione has a stack of cards and a cake that is leaning. Harry’s face opens all the way.
- **Setting:** The hospital wing door. Sun. A scolding healer. White curtains flying.
- **Who:** Ron, freckles, red hair sticking up around a white bandage, maroon sweater, a grin. Hermione, summer dress or robes, hair, cards, the cake. Madam Pomfrey, apron, finger up, defeated by them. Harry sitting up, glasses, bandaged hands.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at Harry, joy, a shout he turns into a whisper and then abandons. Hermione looks at Harry, scanning for damage, then smiling so hard. Harry looks at them, the alone-in-the-chamber feeling breaking. Pomfrey looks at the ceiling, fond, exasperated.

#### C19-P4-P2

- **Beat:** Ron’s head is bandaged from the chess piece. He is proud of it.
- **Image:** Ron taps his bandage like a medal. He retells the queen in a small smash of hands, then winces, then grins. Harry laughs, it hurts his ribs, he does not care. Hermione rolls her eyes and lets him have it.
- **Setting:** The bedside. Cards. Cake. Sun.
- **Who:** Ron, the bandage, freckles, proud. Harry, laughing, glasses, hospital shirt. Hermione, arms folded, a smile she is not hiding well.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at Harry, proud, needing him to have seen the move even though he was busy almost dying. Harry looks at the bandage, gratitude that hurts, the smile watery. Hermione looks at Ron, fond, she will add the true details later. Friendship, loud.

#### C19-P4-P3

- **Beat:** Hermione says she solved the riddle fast and then could not go with him.
- **Image:** Her voice drops. She looks at her hands, the ones that held the back bottle. She hates that the logic ended in sending him alone. The cake leans. Ron goes quieter.
- **Setting:** The bed. A quieter panel among loud ones.
- **Who:** Hermione, hair, the confession. Harry, bandaged. Ron, bandage, listening.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at her hands, guilty, precise about her own limits. Harry looks at her, shaking his head, she is wrong to hate it. Ron looks at her, the chess still in his body, he knows what it is to be the one who stays behind. Kindness, all three.

#### C19-P4-P4

- **Beat:** Harry says she is why he got there. Ron says he is why too. They argue, happy.
- **Image:** Harry points at Hermione with a bandaged hand, then Ron points at himself and nearly pokes his bandage, and they are all talking at once, a happy pile of credit. Cake is threatened. It is the opposite of the chamber.
- **Setting:** The wing. Sun. Pomfrey listening from her office, pretending not to smile.
- **Who:** The three, overlapping, hands, bandages, hair red and brown and black. The cake. The frogs.
- **Faces:** Harry looks from one to the other, insisting, bright. Hermione looks at him, the guilt loosening, a laugh. Ron looks at both, fake-offended, delighted. They look like the trio the door tried to split.

#### C19-P4-P5

- **Beat:** Harry tells them it was Quirrell. Both go still, rewriting the year.
- **Image:** He says the name. The happy noise stops. Ron’s hand stays in the air. Hermione’s mind visibly runs back through a turban, a flinch in a corridor, a Halloween faint, a forest hood. The rewrite is in their eyes. Snape, somewhere in the castle, is suddenly a different shape in their story.
- **Setting:** The bedside. Sun still, the mood changed.
- **Who:** Harry, quiet now, glasses, scar, the truth. Ron and Hermione, stilled.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at them, tired, sure. Hermione looks at the middle distance, rearranging the year, a shiver. Ron looks at Harry, the Snape-theory falling off his face, a silent wow. They look at Harry’s hands. Understanding. No joke.

#### C19-P4-P6

- **Beat:** The three of them on the bed with chocolate. The lake outside is bright.
- **Image:** Page-turn. They sit on the bed, allowed, a blanket ruined with cake crumbs and chocolate frog cards. Harry in the middle, bandaged hands held out so Ron can unwrap a frog for him. Hermione has her shoes off. Through the window the lake is bright, the castle in summer, a boat memory far and small. They are safe this hour.
- **Setting:** The hospital wing, late afternoon, gold, the window, the lake, green grounds. End of the danger, not of the story.
- **Who:** Harry, center, glasses, scar, hospital shirt, a chocolate frog being put in his bandaged hands. Ron, bandage, freckles, maroon sweater, doing the unwrapping, gentle. Hermione, tucked on the other side, a card in her fingers, hair, a smudge of chocolate. The lake outside.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the frog, then at them, content, the unfinished question set down for now. Ron looks at Harry’s hands, careful, joking softly. Hermione looks at the card, then at the lake, a small peaceful smile. They look like children again. The window looks like summer.
